audited, shown in the history; `QM005` → `409 ncr_rule`); `reference` can never change. **Integrations set their source:** inspection raise → `production_inspection` (the person can switch it to `final_inspection` on the NC), audit finding → `internal_audit`, calibration → `calibration` with the Calibration category preselected, 8D start maps source `customer`→customer, `supplier`/`goods_receiving`→supplier, else internal; `raise_ncr_from_inspection` takes `p_open` and optional `p_category_id` (old 9-argument form dropped). `app.backfill_ncr_sources()` sets origins from `inspection_ncr_links` / `audit_ncr_links` / `calibrations.nonconformance_id` only, guessing nothing; no category is guessed. **API:** `GET /ncrs` takes comma-separated `source`, `categoryId`, `uncategorised=true` and sorts by `source`/`category`; rows and detail carry `category` and (detail) `sourceChangeReason`; `POST /ncrs/{id}/source {source, reason}`; `/ncrs/ranges` and `/ncr-dashboard` take several sources, the dashboard trend is `{start, total, bySource, cost}` and it adds `bySource` (all eleven in fixed order) and `byCategory` ("Uncategorised" for none); classification routes take `kind=category`. **UI:** one register (`NonConformance.jsx`: Source and Category columns, filter drawer with source pills, category combo incl. "Uncategorised", customer and supplier pickers; the quick source tabs are gone from the register (`NC_TABS` in `lib/ncr.js` still drives the dashboard's All / Internal / Customer / Supplier switch and covers every source once: Customer = `customer`, Supplier = `supplier,goods_receiving`, Internal = all the others); columns for work order, customer reference and purchase order start hidden via `NC_DEFAULT_HIDDEN`), an **8D column** (last, from `capaCount` / `capa` on the NC list rows — the 8D's number as a link to it when exactly one live 8D is linked, "N 8Ds" when several, "—" for none or when the caller cannot read 8Ds; sortable as `capaCount`), **New NC asks for the source first** (`NewNcDialog`), NC page (`NcrPage.jsx`) with a Source field (a combo while new; read-only with **Change** → `ChangeSourceDialog` once saved) and a Category combo (required to open; cleared only while a draft; "Uncategorised" for old NCs), party fields by source (`toBody` in `formModel.js`), `LegacyNcrRedirect` for the old addresses, Settings → Data Management → Categories (`ClassificationList kind="category"`) and a read-only Sources list, dashboard tabs All/Internal/Customer/Supplier (`?tab=`) with By source and By category charts, a Category choice in the inspection raise-NC dialog (needed to open it at once). Tests: `supabase/tests/ncr_category_source.test.sql` (with fixture `fixtures/20251217100000_audit_findings.sql`), `api/test/ncr-category-source.e2e-spec.ts`, `Ncr.test.jsx`, `NcDashboard.test.jsx`, `lib/ncr.test.js`; **tests that insert an NC directly as open must give it a category** (`pg_temp.cat(org)` in `supabase/tests/helpers.sql`, `categoryId(admin, orgId)` in `api/test/helpers.ts`). **Not built:** renaming the fixed source labels, finding owner → NC responsible employee, a "final inspection" notion inside the inspection module, an external-audit module (that source is chosen by hand).
- **Audits** (`docs/audits-plan.md`; **its own menu module "Audits"** (`/Audits`: Dashboard (the default page), Audit schedule `/Audits/Schedule`, Audit templates `/Audits/Templates` (the audit definitions; `/new`, `/:id`, `/:id/edit`), Audit records `/Audits/Records[/:id]`, Audit findings `/Audits/Findings[/:id]`; needs `audits.read` on its own, not `documentation.read`; **the old `/Documentation/Audit…` addresses redirect** through `LegacyAuditRedirect` / `legacyAuditPath` in `lib/audits.js`); Documentation now holds only My alerts and Quality alerts; the notes below that say Documentation → Audits mean this module; slices AU1–AU4, decisions A1–A8 agreed; **all slices (AU1–AU3, AU5 response types, Phase A findings) done and applied live; AU4 exit check done: `docs/audits-exit-report.md`** (evidence, live checks, what is not built and not browser-checked; the report predates the versions and the dashboard below)):
  - **Definitions (AU1):** permissions `audits.read/.manage/.perform` (**not** `audit.*`: `audit.read` is the audit trail; quality manager and engineer manage, inspector performs, viewer reads). `audit_definitions` (`AUD-001`; title, type, purpose, procedure, frequency daily/weekly/monthly/quarterly/six-monthly/yearly/every N days/on demand + first due + grace days + weekdays only,
    expected auditor person/role/department (shown, not enforced: anyone with `audits.perform` may do it), pass mark; retired with a reason, never deleted), `audit_questions` (ten response types, see AU5 below; critical flag only on a question that can fail), `audit_targets` (**what the audit is relevant to: machines, operation codes, departments**).
    Header under RLS; questions and targets only through `app.set_audit_checklist` / `set_audit_targets` (replace as a whole). Editing a definition is free and audited; records keep a frozen copy. API `api/src/audits/` (`GET|POST /audits`, `GET|PATCH /audits/{id}`, `POST …/retire|reactivate`); the register and page also carry `lastRecord` and `nextDueOn`
    (the list makes the schedule's slots first). UI `src/components/audits/` (`AuditsPage` with Last done / Next due columns, `AuditPage` with a Start audit button, `AuditForm`), hooks `useAudits.js`, `lib/audits.js`. Errors `QM018` → `409 audit_rule`.
  - **Schedule (AU2):** `audit_occurrences` = one slot per audit per due date, status `scheduled|skipped` only (**done, overdue and missed are worked out, never stored**), read-only, audited. `app.generate_audit_occurrences(org, until)` is called by the API when the schedule or the audits register is read (**no worker**): idempotent, never before the audit existed, ≤ 120 days back and ≤ a year ahead,
    month-end clamping from the *first* due date, weekdays-only moves a weekend slot to Monday; a changed rhythm, a retired or an on-demand audit removes only future *scheduled* slots with no record. `app.skip_audit_occurrence(id, reason)` / `unskip` (`audits.manage`; a slot with a record cannot be skipped). API `GET /audit-schedule?from&to&state&auditId&machineId&operationCode&departmentId`
    (default ±30 days; `counts` incl. pass rate and on-time rate = done by due + grace ÷ (done + missed); `truncated` at 500), `POST /audit-occurrences/{id}/skip|unskip`. State order: skipped, done, in progress, then upcoming (due today or later) / overdue (past due, grace last day included) / missed. UI `AuditSchedulePage.jsx` (`/Audits/Schedule`: figures that filter by state, list and Monday-first month calendar, start / skip / put back, URL `view|month|state|audit|machine`). **Its filters are in a pane** (`AuditScheduleFilterPane.jsx`, the registers' right-hand drawer: Audit, Machine and State combos applied with Apply; a Filter button with the count in the header, removable chips and Clear all under it; the state narrows the list only, so it is not a chip in the calendar view). **The calendar is its own rounded scroll area** (`role="grid"`: `flex-1 overflow-auto rounded-2xl`, at least `min-h-96`): it fills the rest of the page, the weeks scroll inside it (each at least 6rem tall, sharing the height when there is room) and the day names stay at the top (`sticky top-0`). **In the calendar view only the calendar scrolls**: from the `md` width up the page root is `md:overflow-hidden` and the calendar `md:min-h-0`, so it takes whatever height is left however short the window (a minimum height made the whole page scroll on a laptop); on a phone the page scrolls and the calendar keeps `min-h-96`. The list view scrolls the page as before.
  - **Records (AU3):** `audit_records` (`AR-YY-NNNN`, frozen title/procedure/pass mark, one non-abandoned record per slot, in progress | completed | abandoned), `audit_record_questions` (frozen checklist), `audit_record_targets` (**which of the audit's machines/operations/departments it was carried out on, one or several**), `audit_responses`, `audit_ncr_links`;
    **no table INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE grants** — every write is a SECURITY DEFINER function needing `audits.perform`: `app.start_audit(audit, slot?, targets?)`, `set_audit_record_targets`, `record_audit_response` (the database works out each answer's result), `update_audit_record`, `complete_audit`, `abandon_audit(reason)`, `raise_ncr_from_audit` (needs `ncr.create`; draft internal NC, one live NC per failed answer, never silent);
    `app.audit_record_problems` is the "still needed" list (every scored question answered, a note on every failure, "carried out on" chosen when the audit lists anything). **Result: fail if any critical question failed or score (passes ÷ passes + fails, n/a left out) < the pass mark**, else pass; a completed record is frozen. API `GET /audit-records` (page-based with filters incl. `performedBy`, `mine`), `POST /audit-records`, `GET|PATCH /audit-records/{id}`,
    `PUT …/targets|responses`, `POST …/complete|abandon|raise-ncr`. UI `AuditRecordsPage.jsx` (`/Audits/Records`), `AuditRecordPage.jsx` (`/Audits/Records/:id`: answers saved as given with `useAnswerAudit` (optimistic, rolled back on error), targets with `useSetAuditTargets`, Complete, Abandon, Raise non-conformance), `StartAuditDialog.jsx`.
    **Not built:** files on the procedure, email/push reminders, a menu count of overdue audits, corrections to a completed record (a new record is the correction). Pushing the API before the migrations breaks the audits routes (they select the new tables and functions).
  - **Response types (AU5, `docs/audits-plan.md` §10):** migration `20251216100000_audit_question_types.sql`. The template sets per question how it is answered and **the audit record form shows exactly that control**. Types (`answer_type`): `yes_no` (which answer passes), `compliant` (compliant / non-compliant), `pass_fail`, `yes_no_na` (the only type with N/A),
    `numeric` (optional unit and limits; scored only when a limit is set), `text` (a note), `multiple_choice` (2–20 options each marked acceptable or not; one choice, stored as the option's label in `audit_responses.choice`), `measurement` (nominal, lower and upper limit and unit all required; the form works the limits out from nominal ± tolerance), `evidence` (no answer: **at least one live file is needed to complete**), `rating`
    (whole number 1..`rating_max` 3–10 default 5, scored only with `pass_rating`). `app.audit_question_valid(...)` is the one definition of a valid question (CHECK on `audit_questions` and the frozen `audit_record_questions`, which also copy `options`, `nominal`, `rating_max`, `pass_rating`); **critical only on a scored question**. `app.set_audit_checklist` takes `answerType`, `passAnswer`, `unit`, `lowerLimit`, `upperLimit`,
    `nominal`, `options [{label, passes}]`, `ratingMax`, `passRating`, `critical`. `app.record_audit_response(record, question, answer, value, note, choice)` works out the result per type (compliant/non_compliant answers; a multiple-choice option matched ignoring case; a rating must be whole and on the scale; numeric/measurement limits inclusive); `app.audit_record_problems` also asks for a file on every evidence question.
    **Evidence files:** `audit_attachments` (per question; written only by `app.add_audit_attachment` (evidence questions, the record's own folder, in progress, `audits.perform`) / `remove_audit_attachment(id, reason)` / `restore_audit_attachment`; never deleted; private `audit-files` bucket at `<org>/audit/<record id>/<uuid>-<name>`, read with `audits.read`, uploads only into an in-progress record via `app.audit_file_allowed`).
    API: `GET /audits/operation-codes` (control plan operation codes plus those already on an audit, for choosing what an audit is relevant to; declared before `:id`), `GET|POST /audit-records/{id}/attachments` (`questionId`, `deleted=include`; the POST body takes `questionId` and the stored path), `POST /audit-attachments/{id}/delete|restore`; the record's questions carry `nominal`, `options`, `ratingMax`, `passRating`, `fileCount`, responses carry `choice`; `PUT …/responses` takes `choice`.
    UI: **the Define an audit form uses the custom combo box for every drop-down** (`ui/ComboField.jsx` wraps `RHFComboBox` for `{ value, label, hint? }` lists; no native `select` is left in it) and **Relevant to is three combo boxes (machines, operations, departments) that add to chips**; `QuestionRow` in `AuditForm.jsx` shows the fields of the chosen type (limits, nominal ± tolerance with the limits shown, an options editor, rating scale and lowest acceptable rating); `lib/audits.js` holds `ANSWER_TYPES` (with an example question each), `isScoredType`,
    `questionsBody`/`questionToForm`/`questionProblems`/`questionRule`/`provisionalResult`; the record page (`AuditRecordPage.jsx`) renders buttons, a number box, a note, a combo box, the rating buttons or an `AttachmentDrop` (`extraBody={{ questionId }}`) by type.
  - **Findings and NC promotion (Phase A, `docs/audits-plan.md` §11; gap analysis `docs/audits-spec-gap-analysis.md`; migration `20251217100000_audit_findings.sql`, applied live):** `audit_finding_classifications` (per organisation, seeded `major_nc` / `minor_nc` / `observation` / `ofi` by `app.seed_audit_finding_classifications` for existing organisations and a trigger on new ones; kinds `nonconformity | observation | opportunity`; `audits.manage` inserts and updates label / default severity / order / active under RLS; **key and kind never change**, nothing deleted),
    `audit_findings` (`AF-YY-NNNN` per organisation and year; one audit record, optionally **one answer** — standalone findings have none; at most one live finding per answer; classification, title, what was found, requirement text, owner = employee, due date; `open | closed | withdrawn`) and `audit_finding_attachments` (evidence; functions only, removed with a reason, never deleted, `audit-files` bucket under `<org>/audit/<record>/findings/<finding>/`). **No table writes on findings or their files — SECURITY DEFINER functions only:** `app.create_audit_finding` (`audits.perform`; an in-progress or completed record, never an abandoned one), `update_audit_finding(id, jsonb)` (keys present are set, null clears owner / due / requirement; a finding with a live NC must stay a non-conformity), `withdraw_audit_finding` (reason; not once it has a live NC),
    `close_audit_finding` / `reopen_audit_finding` (`audits.manage`, note / reason; a finding closes only once its live NC is closed), `app.create_ncr_from_audit_finding(finding, title?, description?, severity?)` (`ncr.create`; the classification must be a non-conformity; makes a **draft internal NC** — `source` stays `internal`, the origin is `audit_ncr_links.finding_id` — with the finding's text as the NC's actual result and requirement, severity from the classification unless given, site from the audit's machine; **idempotent**: the finding row is locked and a live NC (not cancelled, not deleted) already linked is returned with `created: false`; evidence files are not copied, the NC page lists them),
    `add / remove / restore_audit_finding_attachment`, replaced `app.audit_file_allowed` (finding folders: open finding, record not abandoned) and `app.audit_record_problems` (**a failed critical answer needs a live finding before the audit can be completed**). `app.raise_ncr_from_audit` and `POST /audit-records/{id}/raise-ncr` are **gone** (a finding is raised first; one finding → one NC); older `audit_ncr_links` rows (`response_id` only) still show on the NC page. `audit_ncr_links.response_id` may now be empty. Events `audit.finding.created`, `audit.finding.ncr_created`.
    API `api/src/audits/audit-findings.*`: `GET|POST /audit-finding-classifications` (`?all=true` includes switched-off), `PATCH /audit-finding-classifications/{id}`; `GET /audit-findings` (page-based: `q`, `recordId`, `auditId`, `classificationId`, `kind`, `status`, `ownerId`, `overdue`, `from`, `to`, `sort`, `order`, `page`, `pageSize`), `GET|POST /audit-records/{id}/findings`, `GET|PATCH /audit-findings/{id}`, `POST …/withdraw|close|reopen|create-ncr`, `GET|POST …/attachments`, `POST /audit-finding-attachments/{id}/delete|restore`, `GET /ncrs/{id}/audit-source` (`{ available, items }`; `available: false` for someone who may read NCs but not audits). Record answers carry `response.finding`.
    UI: **Findings card** and **Create finding** on every failed answer on the record page (`FindingDialog.jsx`: classification suggested from the question — major for critical, else minor —, title and what was found prefilled from the answer, owner = employee picker, due date), finding page `/Audits/Findings/:id` (`AuditFindingPage.jsx`: facts, evidence `AttachmentDrop`, **Create non-conformance** dialog, close / withdraw / reopen with reasons), register `/Audits/Findings` (`AuditFindingsPage.jsx`, `findingColumns.jsx`, URL filters via `useAuditFindingFilters`), **Settings → Audits** (`Settings/SettingsAudits.jsx`: classifications, optimistic on/off switch via `useUpdateClassification`), **Source audit** card on the NC page (`NcrAuditSource.jsx`, `useNcrAuditSource`). Helpers in `lib/audits.js` (`FINDING_*`, `suggestClassification`, `findingFromAnswer`). Tests: `supabase/tests/audit_findings.test.sql` (41 mutants, all caught), `api/test/audit-findings.e2e-spec.ts`, `AuditFindings.test.jsx`, record-page tests in `AuditRecords.test.jsx`; the journey raises the finding and the NC.
    **Not built (later phases of the gap analysis):** repeat-finding link, several answers on one finding, reviewer approval / auditee acknowledgement (Phase B), templates with versions (C), criteria library (D), programmes and dashboard (E), notifications for findings.
- **Audit versions and the audits dashboard** (`docs/audits-versions-dashboard-plan.md`; slices AV1–AV3, AD1–AD2 done; **migration `20251219100000_audit_versions.sql` applied live** (2026-11-08: 1 existing audit got version 1; the one live record is left "before versions were kept" because its frozen copy differs; live guardrails 0 violations)):
  - **Versions (migration, SQL test `supabase/tests/audit_versions.test.sql`, fixture `fixtures/20251218100000_ncr_category_source.sql` for the backfill):** `audit_versions` (number, **reason**, content fingerprint, header copy), `audit_version_questions`, `audit_version_targets` (all functions-only writes, no user INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE, audited, RLS `audits.read`). A version is an immutable snapshot of **what a record freezes: title, type, purpose, procedure, pass mark, the checklist and the "relevant to" list**; the schedule, expected auditor and retiring are **not** versioned. `app.audit_content_hash` fingerprints the live content; `app.audit_content_changed(audit)`; `app.save_audit_version(audit, reason)` (`audits.manage`; version 1 needs no reason, every later one does (1–1000 characters), a change that alters nothing is refused `QM018`); `app.ensure_audit_version` (used by `app.start_audit`: makes version 1, or a "Changed outside the app; recorded when the audit was started" version, so a record's version always matches what it froze); `app.snapshot_audit_version` internal. **Enforcement:** deferred constraint triggers on `audit_definitions` (content columns), `audit_questions` and `audit_targets` refuse, at the end of the transaction, a **signed-in user's** content change that is not saved as a version (SQL run by the operator is not blocked). `audit_records.audit_version_id/audit_version` say which version a record was started against (null for records before versions; the backfill links an existing record to version 1 only when its frozen copy equals it). **SQL tests that change an audit as a user must save a version, and use `set constraints all immediate` (see `pg_temp.settle()` in the test) to check.**
  - **API:** `POST /audits` makes version 1; `PATCH /audits/{id}` takes `changeReason` (3–1000 characters) and saves a new version **only when the content differs** (a schedule-only change needs none; a content change without a reason is `409 audit_rule`); audits carry `version`; `GET /audits/{id}/versions` (newest first: reason, who, when, summary of what changed, records done against it, `current`) and `GET /audits/{id}/versions/{n}` (whole version, `changes` against the previous one, up to 25 records); records carry `version` and `currentVersion`, `GET /audit-records?version=`. The comparison is pure code in `api/src/audits/audit-versions.ts` (questions matched by wording, then by position; moved, changed with the fields named, added, removed; targets by kind and reference, operation codes ignoring case).
  - **UI:** the audit form asks **"Why is this changing?"** (required once the content differs from what was loaded, says which version it will become; none needed for the schedule or the auditor), the audit page shows the version and a **Version history** card with a dialog per version (`AuditVersions.jsx`: what changed, the audit as it was, records done against it), the record page says "Done against version N of the audit (the audit is now at version M)", a Version column on both registers and a `version` filter on the records register (`?version=`).
  - **Dashboard** (`/Audits`, menu "Audit dashboard", `AuditsDashboard.jsx`, hook `useAuditsDashboard`; API `GET /audits-dashboard?from&to`, `audits.read`, `api/src/audits/audits-dashboard.ts`, no migration; period 30 days / 90 days (default) / 12 months / year to date in `?period=`): done as planned (the schedule's own slot states, exported `STATE_SQL`/`REC_JOIN`; skipped slots left out; completion rate = done ÷ (done + missed + overdue), on-time rate as the schedule), overdue or missed, audits completed with pass rate and change, open and overdue findings, findings raised with how many became a live non-conformance, template changes; charts planned and done per week or month, results per week or month, findings by classification; the questions that fail most often, **coverage** (every machine, operation and department named by an active audit, when a completed audit last covered it, never-covered first then oldest, "stale" after 90 days; abandoned records and retired audits do not count), and lists of overdue or missed audits, findings past due, latest failed audits and recent template changes. Tests: `audits-dashboard.e2e-spec.ts`, `AuditsDashboard.test.jsx`.
  - **Not built:** draft / publish / archive of versions and approval of a change, versioning the schedule, restoring an old version in one click (a new change with a reason does the same), a separate template library, audit programmes and risk scoring.
- **Supplier Quality** (`docs/supplier-quality-plan.md`, from the spec `fortress_supplier_quality_implementation_spec.md`; slices SQ1–SQ6, **SQ1 done, migration `20251224100000_supplier_quality_register.sql` applied live (2026-11-09)**): one supplier register on the existing `suppliers` table; shared engines are reused, never duplicated (NCs, 8D, inspection, audits). **SQ1 (register and profile):** `suppliers` gains `supplier_type`, address/`country` (two capitals)/`website`/`email`/`phone`, `purchasing_reference`, **`approval_status`** (`pending_approval | approved | conditional | suspended | disqualified | archived`, **always starts pending: a BEFORE INSERT trigger forces it, and it is not in the UPDATE grant**; separate from `active` = operational activity) and `risk_rating` (nobody can write it until SQ2's risk assessments). The status moves only through `app.change_supplier_status(supplier, status, reason, conditions?, review_on?)` (`supplier_quality.approve`; allowed moves in `app.supplier_status_allowed`; reason 3–1000 characters; `conditional` needs conditions; review date not in the past; a cross-tenant id is `P0002` → 404) and writes the append-only `supplier_status_history` (first row written by a trigger; existing suppliers got one "recorded" row and **are Pending Approval**). `supplier_classifications` (per organisation, nine seeded by `app.seed_supplier_classifications` + trigger on new organisations, `supplier_quality.configure` adds/renames/switches off, key fixed, label unique ignoring case, nothing deleted) with `supplier_classification_links` replaced as a whole by `app.set_supplier_classifications` (a switched-off one cannot be added). `supplier_contacts` (one primary per supplier, a new primary replaces the old, deactivated never deleted) and `supplier_product_links` (a part of ours, or a material/service in words; what is supplied is fixed once linked). Permissions `supplier_quality.read/.manage/.approve/.configure` (owner, admin, quality manager all; quality engineer read+manage; inspector, viewer read); suppliers can be created/edited by `master_data.manage` **or** `supplier_quality.manage`; module `supplier_quality` is `active`, **no subscription feature is mapped yet** (as for CAPA, audits, production). Errors `QM020` → `409 supplier_rule`. API `api/src/supplier-quality/` (all under `/supplier-quality`): `GET /suppliers` (page-based: `q` over code, names, company/VAT, purchasing ref, city, country, website, email, phone, contacts and classification labels; `status|type|classificationId|risk` comma lists; `active=active|inactive|all`, default active; `sort` code|name|supplierType|approvalStatus|riskRating|country|openNcs|createdAt; `page`, `pageSize`), `POST /suppliers` (`.manage`, strict body: no status or risk accepted), `GET|PATCH /suppliers/{id}` (profile with contacts, products, classifications, counts of open/all NCs and received lots), `POST …/status` (`.approve`), `GET …/history`, `PUT …/classifications`, `POST …/contacts`, `PATCH /supplier-contacts/{id}`, `POST …/products`, `PATCH /supplier-products/{id}`, `GET|POST /classifications`, `PATCH /classifications/{id}`; the old `/suppliers` pickers endpoint is unchanged. UI `src/components/supplier-quality/`: **register at `/Suppliers`** (`SuppliersRegisterPage.jsx`: same table, column menu, header search, filter drawer `SupplierFilterPane.jsx`, chips, CSV and **saved filters** (scope `supplier_register`, `SavedFilterBar` now takes a `describe` prop), `SupplierDialog`), **profile `/Suppliers/:id`** (`SupplierProfilePage.jsx`, tabs via `?tab=`: Overview, Products & services, History are live; Performance, Qualification, Incoming quality, Audits, Documents say they are not available yet; Nonconformities links the NC register filtered by the supplier), `StatusChangeDialog.jsx` (only the allowed moves, reason, conditions), `ContactProductDialogs.jsx`, Settings → Supplier quality (`Settings/SettingsSupplierQuality.jsx`), menu group "Supplier Quality", hooks `useSupplierQuality.js`, helpers `lib/supplierQuality.js`, filter keys `SUPPLIER_FILTER_KEYS`. Tests: `supabase/tests/supplier_quality_register.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-quality.e2e-spec.ts` (+ both sweeps), `SupplierQuality.test.jsx`, `refresh.test.js`. **Not built (later slices):** assessments and approval decisions (until then an approver changes the status directly with a reason; SQ2 will route approvals through decisions and apply preparer ≠ approver), certifications, risk assessments, documents, SCARs, receipts and incoming inspection, performance scorecards, supplier audits, notifications, a journey step. Pushing the API before the migration breaks the supplier endpoints.
- **Supplier Quality SQ2a — certifications and documents** (`docs/supplier-quality-plan.md` SQ2 breakdown: SQ2a this, SQ2b risk assessments, SQ2c assessments and approval decisions; migration `20251226100000_supplier_certifications_documents.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the supplier qualification endpoints): `supplier_documents` (type, title, revision, document date, optional expiry, **one stored file** in the private `supplier-files` bucket at `<org>/supplier/<supplier id>/<uuid>-<name>`, uploads/reads via `app.supplier_file_allowed`; status current / superseded / removed; `verified_at/by/note`) and `supplier_certifications` (type iso_9001 … other, number, issuing body, scope, issued/expiry dates, optional evidence `document_id`, status current / superseded / withdrawn, verification). **No table writes at all — SECURITY DEFINER functions only** (`supplier_quality.manage`; read needs `.read`; a cross-tenant id is `P0002` → 404, a rule `QM020` → `409 supplier_rule`): `app.add_supplier_document(… , replaces?)` (a new version supersedes the old), `update_supplier_document` / `update_supplier_certification` (**refused once verified**), `verify_*` (once, records who and when), `remove_supplier_document(id, reason)` (refused while a current certification uses it as evidence), `add_ / renew_supplier_certification` (renewal keeps the type, supersedes the old, needs verifying afresh), `withdraw_supplier_certification(id, reason)`; nothing is ever deleted. **Expiry is worked out on read** (`api/src/supplier-quality/expiry.ts`: valid / expiring within 60 days / expired / no expiry; valid through the expiry date itself) **and never changes a supplier's approval status** (decision D3); only current records have an expiry state. API `supplier-qualification.*` under `/supplier-quality`: `GET|POST suppliers/{id}/documents|certifications`, `PATCH supplier-documents|supplier-certifications/{id}`, `POST …/verify`, `…/remove` (documents), `…/renew` and `…/withdraw` (certifications), `GET expiry-watchlist?days&kind` (current records already expired or expiring within `days`, soonest first, active suppliers only). UI: `supplier-quality/Qualification.jsx` (`CertificationsCard` on the Qualification tab, `DocumentsCard` on the Documents tab: upload via `uploadFileToSupabase(file, "supplier-files", org, supplierId, "supplier")` then record, new version, verify, edit until verified, renew, withdraw/remove with reasons, past records folded away, an expired-certification `Callout`), `ExpiryWatchlistPage.jsx` at `/Supplier-Quality/Expiry` ("Expiry watchlist" in the menu), `lib/supplierQualification.js` (labels, `expiryBadge`), hooks in `useSupplierQuality.js` (`useQualificationMutation`). Tests: `supabase/tests/supplier_certifications_documents.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-qualification.e2e-spec.ts`, `expiry.spec.ts`, `SupplierQualification.test.jsx` (+ both sweeps). **Not built:** email/push for expiry, a profile-wide expiry banner, material certificates (SQ4).
- **Supplier Quality SQ2b — risk assessments** (migration `20251227100000_supplier_risk_assessments.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the supplier risk endpoints): `supplier_risk_models` (the organisation's scoring as **versions**: factors `{key, label, description, weight 1–10, escalates}`, band thresholds `medium_from < high_from < critical_from ≤ 4`, review interval 1–60 months, a reason; default model seeded for every organisation by `app.seed_supplier_risk_model` + trigger; changed only by `app.save_supplier_risk_model(org, factors, thresholds, interval, reason)` (`supplier_quality.configure`; unchanged refused)) and `supplier_risk_assessments` (**append-only**, written only by `app.record_supplier_risk_assessment(supplier, scores, rationale, review_on?)` (`supplier_quality.manage`, rationale required, review date after today, default = today + the model's interval); freezes the model id/version, every factor with weight, score and note, `risk_index`, `band`, `rating`, `escalated_by`, `previous_rating`; supersedes the previous current one; **the only writer of `suppliers.risk_rating`**; event `supplier.risk_assessed`). **The database scores** (`app.score_supplier_risk(model, scores)`: each factor 1 low … 4 critical, every factor of the model exactly once, index = Σ weight×score ÷ Σ weight, band by thresholds, a factor with `escalates` scored 4 lifts the rating to at least high) so the preview and the saved record cannot disagree. Overdue review is computed on read (`reviewOverdue`), nothing is auto-changed. API `supplier-risk.*` under `/supplier-quality`: `GET|PUT risk-model` (`{ …, versions }`; PUT takes `thresholds {mediumFrom, highFrom, criticalFrom}`), `GET suppliers/{id}/risk-assessments`, `POST …/risk-assessments/preview` (nothing saved), `POST …/risk-assessments`. UI: `RiskCard` + assess dialog (a 1–4 `SlidingToggle` per factor, live database preview, rationale, optional next review) on the profile's Qualification tab (`supplier-quality/RiskAssessment.jsx`), `RiskModelCard` + `RiskModelDialog` in Settings → Supplier quality (new version with a reason; existing factor keys kept, new ones from `factorKey` in `lib/supplierRisk.js`), hooks `useRiskModel|useRiskAssessments|usePreviewRisk|useRiskMutation`. Tests: `supabase/tests/supplier_risk_assessments.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-risk.e2e-spec.ts`, `SupplierRisk.test.jsx` (+ both sweeps). **Not built:** risk review reminders / watchlist entries, risk-driven rules (e.g. inspection levels, SQ4), risk on the register beyond the existing filter and column.
- **Supplier Quality SQ2c — qualification assessments and approval decisions** (migration `20251228100000_supplier_qualification.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the supplier qualification endpoints, and **it changes SQ1: a direct move to approved / conditional is now refused**): **`app.change_supplier_status` refuses `approved` / `conditional` unless a transaction-local setting `app.supplier_approval_decision = 'on'` is set, which only `app.decide_supplier_approval` does** (suspend, disqualify, archive and re-open stay direct; SQL tests that need an approved supplier set that setting, e2e tests approve through `approveVia` with a second approver). **Templates:** `supplier_assessment_templates` + `supplier_assessment_template_versions` (questions jsonb `{key, section, text, answerType yes_no|rating|text, weight 1–10, critical, evidenceRequired, passRating 2–5}`, pass mark 1–100, reason; validated by `app.supplier_assessment_questions_problem`; a default "Supplier qualification questionnaire" (11 questions, pass mark 75) seeded per organisation; functions `create_supplier_assessment_template`, `save_supplier_assessment_template_version` (unchanged refused), `update_supplier_assessment_template` (rename, describe, switch on/off), all `supplier_quality.configure`). **Assessments:** `supplier_assessments` (`SA-YY-NNNN` via `app.supplier_assessment_counters`; **freezes** template name, version, questions and pass mark; in_progress → completed | abandoned; one in progress per supplier; only the current version of an active template can be started) and `supplier_assessment_responses` (functions only, `app.record_supplier_assessment_response(assessment, key, answer yes|no|na, rating, note, document)`: **the database works out pass / fail / n/a / unscored**; evidence must be a current document of the supplier); `app.supplier_assessment_progress(id)` = the "still needed" list (unanswered scored questions, a note on every failure, a document on evidence-required answers) and the provisional score; `app.complete_supplier_assessment` needs no problems, score = Σ weight×points ÷ Σ weight over answered non-n/a scored questions (yes = full, rating = (r−1)/4), **result fail if any critical question failed or the score is under the pass mark**, then frozen; `abandon_supplier_assessment(id, reason)` (`supplier_quality.manage`). **Approval decisions:** `supplier_approval_decisions` (proposed → approved | rejected | withdrawn; one open proposal per supplier): `app.propose_supplier_approval(supplier, target approved|conditional, rationale, assessment?, conditions?, review_on?, exception_reason?)` (`.manage`; the transition must be allowed from the current status; **approval needs a passing assessment, otherwise an exception reason (10+ characters) is required and kept on the decision**; conditional needs conditions), `app.decide_supplier_approval(decision, approve, note)` (`.approve`, **never the person who proposed it**, refuses when the supplier's status has moved since; approving goes through `app.change_supplier_status`, so the status history stays the one record and the decision links its row), `app.withdraw_supplier_approval(decision, reason)` (proposer or an approver). API `supplier-assessment.*` under `/supplier-quality`: `GET|POST assessment-templates`, `PATCH assessment-templates/{id}`, `POST …/versions`, `GET|POST suppliers/{id}/assessments`, `GET supplier-assessments/{id}`, `PUT …/responses`, `POST …/complete|abandon`, `GET|POST suppliers/{id}/approval-decisions` (each carries `canDecide`), `POST supplier-approval-decisions/{id}/decide|withdraw`. UI: `ApprovalCard` and `AssessmentsCard` (`supplier-quality/Approvals.jsx`) at the top of the Qualification tab, the run screen `AssessmentPage.jsx` at `/Suppliers/:supplierId/assessments/:id` (answers saved as given, evidence combo, still-needed list, Complete/Abandon dialogs, read-only once done), `AssessmentTemplatesCard` + question editor in Settings → Supplier quality (`AssessmentTemplates.jsx`), the status dialog no longer offers approve / conditional and links to the Qualification tab; hooks `useAssessmentTemplates|useSupplierAssessments|useSupplierAssessment|useApprovalDecisions|useAssessmentMutation`. Tests: `supabase/tests/supplier_qualification.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-assessment.e2e-spec.ts`, `SupplierAssessment.test.jsx` (+ both sweeps, `supplier-quality.e2e-spec.ts` and `SupplierQuality.test.jsx` updated). **Not built:** email/push to approvers, re-qualification schedules (a review date is stored, nothing reminds), assessments of types other than questionnaires, supplier-completed questionnaires (portal, D5), approval of a supplier by an assessment automatically.
- **Supplier Quality SQ3 — supplier non-conformances and SCARs** (migration `20251229100000_supplier_scars.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; the spec arrived after this was built and its ten statuses differ slightly from the ones below — SQ3e closed the field gaps, not the status names; pushing the API before the migration breaks the SCAR endpoints): **SQ3a** (no migration): the profile's Nonconformities tab is `supplier-quality/SupplierNcs.jsx` — the central NCs that name the supplier as **responsible** (`GET /ncrs?supplierId`, Open / All, `OPEN_NC_STAGES` in `lib/supplierQuality.js`), each row showing its **source** (where it was found), which is independent of who is responsible; no separate register (the NC register already filters by supplier and source). **SCAR** = supplier corrective action request: it asks an *external* party to investigate and fix and tracks what comes back; it is not the internal 8D (a SCAR may link one via `capa_id`). Tables `supplier_scars` (`SCAR-YY-NNNN` from `app.supplier_scar_counters`; one supplier; severity minor/major/critical; `response_due_on` and the kept `original_response_due_on`; `client_key` unique per organisation for idempotent creation), `supplier_scar_ncrs` (many NCs, **only the supplier's own live NCs**, ended with a reason never deleted), `supplier_scar_responses` (seq 1, 2 …, frozen), `supplier_scar_reviews` (one per response), `supplier_scar_implementations`, `supplier_scar_verifications`, `supplier_scar_extensions`, `supplier_scar_status_history` — the last six are **append-only and frozen by trigger** (`app.scar_frozen`). **No table writes at all: SECURITY DEFINER functions only**, `supplier_quality.manage` unless noted, read `.read`; cross-tenant ids `P0002` → 404, rules `QM020` → `409 supplier_rule`. **Ten statuses** (`app.scar_transition_allowed`, guarded again by trigger `supplier_scars_status_guard` so even SQL cannot make an impossible move): draft → issued → acknowledged → response_received → under_review → revision_requested | accepted → verifying → closed, plus cancelled from any running status. Functions: `create_supplier_scar(supplier, title, description, severity, response_due_on, contact, ncrs[], capa, client_key)` (retry with the same key returns the same SCAR, `created: false`), `update_supplier_scar` (a draft changes anything; once issued only contact and 8D link), `link_scar_ncr` / `unlink_scar_ncr(link, reason)` (an issued SCAR keeps ≥ 1 NC), `issue_supplier_scar` (needs a future due date and ≥ 1 live NC), `acknowledge_supplier_scar`, `record_scar_response` (root cause and corrective action required; **receiving a response never closes it**), `start_scar_review`, `review_scar_response` (accepted | revision_requested, note required, reviews the latest response), `record_scar_implementation` (the first moves accepted → verifying), `record_scar_verification` (**`supplier_quality.approve`**; effective → closed, not effective → revision_requested), `extend_scar_due_date` (replaced in SQ3e, below), `cancel_supplier_scar` (reason). **Overdue is worked out on read, never stored**: a response is awaited (issued / acknowledged / revision_requested) and `response_due_on` is past. API `api/src/supplier-quality/supplier-scar.*` under `/supplier-quality`: `GET scars` (page-based: `q` over reference, title, problem, supplier and live NC references; `status` running (default) | all | statuses; `severity`, `supplierId`, `ncrId`, `overdue=true`, `sort` reference|title|supplier|status|severity|responseDueOn|ncrCount|createdAt|issuedAt, `order`, `page`, `pageSize`), `POST scars` (strict body, `ncrIds`, `clientKey`), `GET|PATCH scars/{id}`, `POST scars/{id}/issue|acknowledge|responses|start-review|review|implementations|verifications|extensions|cancel|ncrs`, `POST scar-ncrs/{id}/unlink`; every command answers with the whole SCAR (`ScarDetail`: NC links, responses each with its review, implementations, verifications, extensions, history). UI `supplier-quality/`: register `/Supplier-Quality/SCARs` (`ScarsPage.jsx`, Running / Overdue / Closed / All, search, "Supplier corrective actions" in the menu), `ScarPage.jsx` at `/Supplier-Quality/SCARs/:id` (a five-stage rail — issued, supplier response, internal review, implementation, effectiveness check — with a **Next** card offering only what the status and the person's rights allow, separate cards for the request, NCs, responses with their reviews, implementation, verification, extensions and history), `ScarDialogs.jsx` (Raise with a client key and the supplier's open NCs ticked, Edit, Acknowledge, Response, Review, Implementation, Verification, Extend, Cancel/Unlink with a reason, Link), `ScarsList.jsx` (shared table), `SupplierScars.jsx` (the profile's **SCARs** tab), `NcrScarCard.jsx` (on the NC page when it names a supplier: its SCARs and **Raise a SCAR** with the supplier and NC preselected), hooks `useScars|useScar|useScarMutation`, `lib/supplierScars.js`. Tests: `supabase/tests/supplier_scars.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-scar.e2e-spec.ts` (+ both sweeps), `SupplierScar.test.jsx`, `SupplierNcs.test.jsx`. **SQ3e (spec gaps; migration `20251230100000_scar_spec_fields.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the SCAR endpoints):** `supplier_scars` gains `requirement_breached`, `objective_evidence`, `containment_requirements` and `owner_user_id` (the **internal owner**: an active member, defaults to whoever raised it, backfilled from `created_by`, can be handed over while the SCAR runs; the text fields are draft-only). `supplier_scar_items` = what is affected (part of ours, a material in words, a lot, a typed receipt reference until receiving exists, quantity, notes; a lot brings its part and must agree with a named one; added/removed with `app.add_scar_item` / `remove_scar_item(id, reason)` while the SCAR is not closed or cancelled; ended never deleted; functions only). `supplier_scar_responses` gains `extent_of_condition`, `escape_point`, `responsible_name` and `root_cause_method` (5 whys | fishbone | other, optional, never mandatory); frozen like the rest. **Due-date extensions are requested, then decided:** `app.request_scar_extension` (manage; one waiting per SCAR; the date does NOT move), `app.decide_scar_extension(id, approve, note)` (**`supplier_quality.approve`, never the person who asked**, a note; approving moves `response_due_on`, the original stays), `app.withdraw_scar_extension` (the requester or an approver), a recorded response or a cancellation withdraws a waiting request (`app.scar_settle_pending_extension`); the extensions table now carries `status` pending|approved|rejected|withdrawn, `decided_*`, `decision_note` and may change **once, only from pending** (`app.scar_extension_guard`); rows from before are approved. `create_supplier_scar` takes `p_extra jsonb` (`requirementBreached`, `objectiveEvidence`, `containmentRequirements`, `ownerId`, `items[]`; the old 9-argument call still works), `record_scar_response` four optional trailing arguments. API: create/update bodies take the new fields and `items`, `POST scars/{id}/items`, `POST scar-items/{id}/remove`, `POST scars/{id}/extensions` now **requests**, `POST scar-extensions/{id}/decide|withdraw`, `GET scar-people` (owner picker); rows carry `owner`, the detail `requirementBreached`/`objectiveEvidence`/`containmentRequirements`/`items`, responses the four new fields, extensions `status`/`decidedBy`/`decisionNote`/`canDecide`. UI: Raise/Edit dialogs ask for the requirement, evidence, containment and owner, the SCAR page has an **Affected items** card (add / remove with a reason), the extra response fields, **Request more time** and a **Decide** button for an approver who did not ask, Owner column in the lists. Fixture `supabase/tests/fixtures/20251229100000_supplier_scars.sql` exercises the backfill. **Not built:** notifications (due, overdue, response received, extension waiting), a SCAR dashboard or PPM link, files on a SCAR or a response, supplier-completed responses (portal, D5), automatic SCARs from rules, reopening a closed SCAR, editing a recorded response (a revision is a new response), the spec's separate "Awaiting supplier response" status (issued / acknowledged cover it), email.
- **Supplier Quality SQ4a — purchasing and goods receiving** (`docs/supplier-quality-plan.md` D1 option (a); SQ4 is split: SQ4a this, SQ4b inspection requirements and incoming inspection through the shared engine + rejection → NC with a receipt link, SQ4c material certificates; migration `20251231100000_purchasing_receiving.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the new endpoints): the spec says reuse an existing goods-receipt model; Fortress had none, so this is the smallest one that makes on-time delivery, PPM and incoming inspection possible. Permissions **`receiving.read` / `.manage`** (manage: owner, admin, quality manager, quality engineer, inspector; read: everyone who can see supplier quality, plus viewers). `purchase_orders` (number unique per organisation ignoring case; open | closed | cancelled with a reason), `purchase_order_lines` (a part of ours **or** a material in words, quantity, unit, **`requested_on` = the original requested date, never changes**, **`agreed_on` = the latest agreed date**, changed with `app.set_po_line_agreed_date` and audited — an on-time policy can use either; no prices, approvals or ERP sync), `goods_receipts` (`GR-YY-NNNN`, supplier, optional order and site (the organisation\'s only site by default), received date not in the future, delivery note, received | cancelled with a reason, `client_key` for idempotent retries) and `goods_receipt_lines` (optional order line, part or material, quantity, **the lot it became**; frozen: a receipt is cancelled as a whole). **A receipt line that names a lot number makes the lot** (`lots.origin = 'received'`, the supplier, site, quantity and received date filled in; needs a part; refuses a lot number already used for that part), so traceability, affected lots and the trace page work for received material. Cancelling a receipt keeps the lots it made. **No table writes: SECURITY DEFINER functions only** (`create_purchase_order`, `set_po_line_agreed_date`, `close_purchase_order`, `cancel_purchase_order`, `record_goods_receipt`, `cancel_goods_receipt`), errors **`QM021` → `409 receiving_rule`**, cross-tenant `P0002` → 404. **Received, outstanding and late are worked out on read** (a line is late when it is open, goods are outstanding and `coalesce(agreed_on, requested_on)` is before today). API `api/src/receiving/` (no `/supplier-quality` prefix): `GET|POST /purchase-orders` (list: `q`, `supplierId`, `status` open (default) | closed | cancelled | all, `late=true`, `sort`, page), `GET /purchase-orders/lookup?supplierId&q` (open orders, declared before `:id`), `GET /purchase-orders/{id}` (lines with `quantityReceived/Outstanding`, `late`, `daysLate`, first and last received; its receipts), `POST …/close|cancel`, `PATCH /purchase-order-lines/{id} {agreedOn}`, `GET|POST /goods-receipts` (list: `q` over reference, delivery note, order, supplier, lot, part, material; `supplierId`, `purchaseOrderId`, `lotId`, `status`, `from|to`; POST takes `clientKey`), `GET /goods-receipts/{id}`, `POST …/cancel`. UI `supplier-quality/`: `PurchaseOrdersPage` (`/Supplier-Quality/Purchase-Orders`: Open / Late / Closed / All, search), `PurchaseOrderPage` (lines with received / outstanding / late, **Agreed date**, **Record a delivery** with the outstanding quantities ready and a lot number per line, Close, Cancel), `GoodsReceiptsPage`, `GoodsReceiptPage` (lots link to their trace), `ReceivingDialogs.jsx`, `SupplierReceiving.jsx` (the profile\'s **Incoming quality** tab: open orders and the latest ten deliveries with both actions), menu entries "Purchase orders" and "Goods receipts", hooks `src/hooks/useReceiving.js`, `lib/receiving.js`. Tests: `supabase/tests/purchasing_receiving.test.sql`, `api/test/receiving.e2e-spec.ts` (+ both sweeps), `Receiving.test.jsx`. **Not built:** the NC ↔ receipt link, inspection requirements and incoming inspection (SQ4b), material certificates (SQ4c), receipt quarantine / hold on arrival, partial-line splitting, over-receipt warnings, prices, ERP sync (`external_system/external_id` are not reserved here), email.
- **Supplier Quality SQ4b — inspection requirements and incoming inspection** (migration `20260101100000_incoming_inspection.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the receipt and requirement endpoints): `supplier_inspection_requirements` (what to inspect on arrival; scope = supplier, part, part family, supplier classification, risk; strategy full / fixed / percentage / plan (bands of lot size) / first_article / skip_lot / none; level increased / normal / reduced / skip; generated `reduces_controls`; **draft → approved → frozen, approved only by a different person with `supplier_quality.approve`** (`app.approve_inspection_requirement`, a reducing rule needs a 10+ character note); a replacement supersedes; retire with a reason; written only by `app.save_inspection_requirement` / approve / retire). Most specific approved rule wins (part 100, family 50, supplier 30, classification 20, risk 10; newest on a tie). **A part with a critical characteristic at the rule's operation, or no resolvable plan, never gets reduced controls** (`app.part_controlled_for_incoming`: inspected in full). `app.record_goods_receipt` now stores a frozen `incoming_inspection_determinations` row per line and puts the lot `on_hold` when inspection is required; `app.start_incoming_inspection` uses the shared `app.start_inspection` with trigger `incoming`; `app.incoming_inspection_assessment` (samples, failures, outcome); `app.disposition_receipt_line` (append-only `receipt_line_dispositions` held / accepted / rejected / conditional; accepting needs an acceptable assessment where required; conditional and leaving rejected need approval rights); `app.raise_ncr_from_receipt_line` (central NC, source goods_receiving, idempotent, `receipt_line_ncrs`). API `api/src/receiving/requirements.*` (`/inspection-requirements`, `/incoming-inspection/preview`, `GET /ncrs/{id}/receipt-source`) and `POST /goods-receipt-lines/{id}/start-inspection|disposition|raise-ncr`; receipt lines carry status, determination, assessment, dispositions, NCs. UI: `RequirementsPage` (`/Supplier-Quality/Inspection-Requirements`) + `RequirementDialog`, `LineInspection.jsx` on the receipt page, `NcrReceiptSource` on the NC page, hooks `useIncoming.js`, `lib/incoming.js`. Tests: `supabase/tests/incoming_inspection.test.sql`, `api/test/incoming-inspection.e2e-spec.ts`, `IncomingInspection.test.jsx`. **Not built:** material certificates (SQ4c), scheduled re-evaluation of skip-lot levels, receipt quarantine locations, ERP sync, email.
- **Supplier Quality SQ4c — material certificates** (migration `20260102100000_material_certificates.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the receipt endpoints (lines select `app.receipt_line_certificates`) and the new ones): `supplier_material_certificates` = what a supplier sent with material (kind mill test report / certificate of conformance / certificate of analysis / test report / other; number, specification, heat number, issued date, optional file = a **current `supplier_documents` row of the same supplier**, notes). It covers a part of ours or a material in words and must be **traceable**: a receipt line, a lot and/or a heat number (a receipt line brings its lot and part; receipt line, lot, part and supplier must agree; a cancelled receipt is refused; the same number + heat is not recorded twice while it stands, any case). Status received → accepted | rejected (**reviewed once, frozen**; a rejection needs a note) | withdrawn (reason, any status; frees the number). **No table writes: SECURITY DEFINER functions only** — `app.record_material_certificate` / `update_material_certificate` (until reviewed) / `withdraw_material_certificate` need `receiving.manage` (so inspectors record), `app.review_material_certificate` needs `supplier_quality.manage`; read `receiving.read`; `app.receipt_line_certificates(line)` counts live ones by status (**information only: it never blocks a disposition**). Errors `QM020` → `409 supplier_rule`, cross-tenant `P0002` → 404. API `api/src/receiving/material-certificates.*`: `GET /material-certificates` (page-based: `q`, `supplierId`, `lotId`, `receiptLineId`, `partId`, `status` live (default) | review | all | statuses; `reviewCount`), `POST` (strict body with `supplierId`), `GET|PATCH /material-certificates/{id}`, `POST …/review {accept, note}|withdraw {reason}`; receipt lines carry `certificates {received, accepted, rejected}`. UI: `MaterialCertificatesPage` (`/Supplier-Quality/Material-Certificates`, "Material certificates" in the menu: Current / To review / All, search, review / edit / withdraw by rights), `CertificateDialogs.jsx` (`RecordCertificateDialog` from the register or from a receipt line with **Add certificate**, `CertificateDecisionDialog`), a certificates line under each receipt line in `LineInspection.jsx`, hooks `useMaterialCertificates.js`, `lib/materialCertificates.js`. Tests: `supabase/tests/material_certificates.test.sql`, `api/test/material-certificates.e2e-spec.ts` (+ both sweeps), `MaterialCertificates.test.jsx`. **Not built:** a requirement that a certificate exists (or is accepted) before a line can be accepted, chemistry / mechanical values, a certificates section on the trace page, certificate expiry, email.
- **Supplier Quality SQ5 — performance scorecards** (migration `20260103100000_supplier_performance.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the performance endpoints): **the database works everything out, once, so the live view, a saved scorecard and the watchlist cannot disagree** (this replaces the plan's `metrics.ts`: a client or the API never sends a figure). `supplier_scorecard_models` (the organisation's scoring as **versions**: `config` = OTD date policy `agreed` | `original`, weights for otd / ppm / ncs / scar / certs, `ppm_good` / `ppm_zero`, per-severity NC penalties, `good_from` / `watch_from`, `min_lines`; valid per `app.scorecard_config_problem`; default seeded per organisation by trigger; new version only via `app.save_supplier_scorecard_model(org, config, reason)`, `supplier_quality.configure`, unchanged refused). `app.supplier_performance_metrics(supplier, from, to, policy)` (SECURITY DEFINER; caller needs `supplier_quality.read` **and** `receiving.read`; cancelled receipts left out): **PPM** = units on lines whose latest decision is rejected ÷ units received × 1,000,000 (a rejected line counts whole: dispositions carry no quantity); **lot rejection** = rejected lines ÷ lines (kept distinct); **on-time** = receipt lines against an order line received on or before the due date (original requested date or latest agreed date by policy; lines with no order line are "unmatched" and left out; open order lines already past due are counted as a warning only); **NCs** = live NCs naming the supplier detected in the period by severity, repeats = NCs on a part with ≥ 2; **SCARs** issued in the period answered by the original due date (unanswered and not yet due left out); **certifications** current and not past expiry (as at today). Each carries the records behind it (`sources`, ≤ 10). `app.score_supplier_performance(config, metrics)` (pure): each metric 0–100 (OTD %, PPM linear between the targets, NCs 100 − penalties, SCAR % on time, certs % in date); **a metric without enough data is left out; when under half the weight has data there is no score and the band is `insufficient`**; band good / watch / poor; warnings (`info` / `warning` / `critical`) with `sourceType` and `sources`. `app.supplier_performance(supplier, from, to)` = live (current model + metrics + scoring); `app.save_supplier_scorecard(supplier, from, to, note)` (`supplier_quality.manage`; period ends today or earlier, ≤ 366 days) writes `supplier_scorecards` (**append-only, frozen by trigger even for SQL; no table writes**; keeps model version, config, metrics with their sources, components, score, band, warnings); `app.supplier_watchlist(org, days 7–366)` scores every active, non-archived supplier (≤ 300) and returns those that are poor / watch or carry a warning or critical flag, worst first. Nothing here changes a supplier's approval status. Errors `QM020` → `409 supplier_rule`. API `api/src/supplier-quality/supplier-performance.*` under `/supplier-quality`: `GET|PUT performance/model`, `GET performance/watchlist?days`, `GET suppliers/{id}/performance?from&to` (default last 90 days), `GET|POST suppliers/{id}/scorecards`, `GET scorecards/{id}`. UI: the profile's **Performance** tab (`SupplierPerformance.jsx`: 30 days / 90 days / 12 months, score, band, metric cards, warnings linking to the receipt, order, NC, SCAR or certification behind them, figures table, **Save as a scorecard**, saved scorecards with a trend line and a dialog showing the frozen record), `WatchlistPage.jsx` (`/Supplier-Quality/Watchlist`, "Supplier watchlist" in the menu), `ScorecardModel.jsx` (Settings → Supplier quality: current model, versions, **Change the model** dialog), `PerformanceParts.jsx`, hooks `useSupplierPerformance.js`, `lib/supplierPerformance.js`. Tests: `supabase/tests/supplier_performance.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-performance.e2e-spec.ts` (+ both sweeps), `SupplierPerformance.test.jsx`. **Not built:** scheduled scorecards (no worker: a person saves them), email / push for the watchlist, partial-quantity rejection (needs quantities on dispositions), the agreed date as it stood on the day (only the latest is kept), document completeness as a metric, per-part or per-site scoring, PPM by customer.
- **Supplier Quality SQ6 — supplier audits, inspection recommendation and reports** (migration `20260104100000_supplier_audit_targets.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09 (0 existing audit fingerprints changed); pushing the API before it breaks the audit endpoints that select `supplier_id`): **SQ6a:** a supplier is now a fourth thing an audit can be **relevant to** (`kind = 'supplier'`, `supplier_id`) on `audit_targets`, `audit_version_targets` and `audit_record_targets` (CHECKs replaced; unique per audit / record; own-organisation composite FK). `app.set_audit_targets`, `app.set_audit_record_targets` (only the audit's own suppliers on a record) and `app.snapshot_audit_version` learned it; `app.audit_content_hash` only **appends** the supplier to its coalesce, so **no existing audit's fingerprint changes** (SQL test compares against the old formula). Everything else about audits is unchanged: versions need a reason, records freeze their targets, the dashboard coverage lists suppliers too (`kind: supplier`). API: target bodies take `{ kind: 'supplier', supplierId }`; `GET /audits`, `GET /audit-records` and `GET /audit-findings` take `supplierId`. UI: a **Suppliers** list under Relevant to in the audit form (the audit page, record page, version history and dashboard show them). **SQ6b:** the profile's **Audits** tab (`SupplierAudits.jsx`): audits listing the supplier (last done, next due), records carried out on it, findings with the NC each became, and **Start an audit** (`StartAuditDialog supplier=`: only audits that list the supplier, carried out on it). No tab says "not available yet" any more. **SQ6c:** `GET /supplier-quality/suppliers/{id}/inspection-recommendation?days` — **information only, nothing is written**: the rules are one pure function (`api/src/supplier-quality/inspection-recommendation.ts`, unit-tested): INCREASE for high/critical risk, conditional/suspended, poor band, a critical NC, an overdue SCAR response or ≥ 10% of lines rejected; REDUCE only when approved, risk assessed and low, band good on enough lines, no major or critical NC, no overdue SCAR, no expired certification (every unmet condition is listed); else NORMAL. It is set beside the supplier-wide approved requirement (`current`, or the default) and says raise / lower / none. UI `InspectionRecommendation.jsx` on the **Incoming quality** tab (needs `supplier_quality.read` + `receiving.read`), with a link to write a requirement (which someone else approves; a reducing requirement is still never applied to a part with a critical characteristic). **SQ6d:** read-only reports `GET /supplier-quality/reports/performance|audits?from&to` (default last 90 days, ≤ 366 days; performance needs `receiving.read` too, audits needs `audits.read`) answer `{ meta { title, from, to, generatedAt, definitions[], basis[] (scoring model version, date policy, weights, bands, minimum lines), rowCount }, columns, rows }`; the watchlist page's **Export a report** menu turns one into a CSV whose first lines say what it is, the period, when it was generated, what it was scored with and every definition (`lib/reportCsv.js`: quotes, and a cell that starts with `= + - @` is kept as text so a spreadsheet will not run it). Performance report = every active supplier (≤ 500) scored live; audits report = one row per record and supplier (abandoned left out). Tests: `supabase/tests/supplier_audit_targets.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-audit-targets.e2e-spec.ts`, `supplier-performance.e2e-spec.ts` (recommendation, reports), `inspection-recommendation.spec.ts`, `SupplierAudits.test.jsx`, `InspectionRecommendation.test.jsx`, `reportCsv.test.js`. **Not built:** supplier coverage as a gap on the audits schedule, email / scheduled reports, PDF reports, portal readiness beyond keeping response tables free of internal-only fields, per-part inspection recommendations, applying a recommendation in one click.
- **Supplier quality dashboard** (no migration; `/Supplier-Quality`, menu Supplier Quality → Dashboard, now the group's default page; `supplier-quality/SupplierDashboard.jsx`, hook `useSupplierDashboard` in `useSupplierPerformance.js`): API `GET /supplier-quality/dashboard?from&to` (`supplier_quality.read`, ≤ 3 years, `api/src/supplier-quality/supplier-dashboard.ts`, read as the caller). **As of today** (suppliers in use = active and not archived): suppliers, approved, pending approval, approval proposals waiting, high / critical risk, not assessed, risk reviews overdue, current certifications and documents expired / expiring within 60 days, running SCARs and those with a response overdue; `byStatus`, `byRisk`, `scarsByStatus`; lists `overdueScarList`, `approvalList`, `expiryList` (≤ 10), `riskReviewList` (≤ 8 each). **For the period:** `ncs` (live NCs naming a supplier: open now, raised, per week or month by severity, top 8 suppliers; `available: false` without `ncr.read`), `receiving` (delivered lines, rejected lines, lot rejection %, per-bucket accepted / rejected / undecided; as of today lines waiting for a decision, open order lines past their date, material certificates to review; `available: false` without `receiving.read`) and `performance` (needs `receiving.read`): **every supplier in use (≤ 300, `truncated`) is scored by `app.supplier_performance`, exactly as on its Performance tab, and the dashboard only adds the results up** — bands, on-time % (on-time ÷ matched lines), PPM (rejected ÷ received units), in full %, and `attention` (poor / watch or carrying a warning, worst first, top 8, with the first warning as the reason) — so it cannot disagree with scorecards or the watchlist. A section the caller may not read says so; it never shows zeros. Periods use the shared `useDashboardPeriod("90d")` (`?period=`). Tests: `api/test/supplier-dashboard.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep query), `SupplierDashboard.test.jsx`. **Look (redesigned, frontend only):** a hero card (supplier count over a theme-coloured wash with the approval split), a Delivery performance card (half-circle gauges for on-time and in full, meters for PPM and lines rejected, two link tiles), five stat tiles with tinted icons, split bars above each trend chart that double as its legend, score rings in the attention table, the corrective action requests as steps in order, initials and chips in the lists. The drawn pieces are shared by the dashboards in `dashboard/DashboardGraphics.jsx` (`Tile` with `data-tile=<label>` for tests, `HeroCard`, `StatTile` (takes `change` on the previous period), `ChangeNote`, `ArcGauge`, `Meter`, `SplitBar`, `RankList`, `ScoreRing`, `Pipeline` (`layout` four / six / eight steps a row, `to(step)` links), `Initial`, `Chip`, `Empty`, `cardLink`, `headerLink`); every one keeps its figures in words (a list, a note or a table cell), colours are the `--chart-*` status colours and the theme colour, and a gauge or meter is only used for one ratio (splits are bars, not donuts). **Not built:** PPM / on-time trend lines per bucket (the scoring is per supplier and period), change on the previous period, a journey step, drill-down by site or part. In a test, `beforeEach(() => mock.mockReset())` returns the mock and Vitest calls it as a cleanup: use braces.
- **Integrations (Phase 5, `docs/connectors-plan.md`; decisions D1–D10 agreed — D9 import settings (add new only / add and update / update only, match existing, field ownership, schedule, disappeared records; NCs always add new only), D10 references by the source id from an earlier import through the same connection or by Fortress code, and file connections need no lifecycle and are self-approved by the Controller who imports (§6.7–§6.10); slices IN0–IN9, all done; exit report `docs/connectors-exit-report.md`)**: **IN1** (migration `20260107100000_integrations_core.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09, live guardrails 0 violations): `integration_providers` (global catalogue: `csv` and `rest_api` available), `integration_connections` (status draft → ready → active ⇄ paused → retired; `external_system` = fixed short code naming where records came from; non-secret `config` validated by `app.integration_config_problem`: https only, no localhost / IP literals / metadata address / userinfo / non-443 ports / internal suffixes), `integration_credentials` (**the secret lives in Supabase Vault; the table holds only the secret id**; written by `app.set_integration_credential` / `revoke_integration_credential`, read back only by `app.integration_credential_secret`, executable by the `qms_api` login alone; never returned, audited or logged) and append-only `integration_connection_events`. **No table writes: SECURITY DEFINER functions only**; permissions `integrations.read/.manage/.run/.approve`; marking ready needs no activation problems (`app.integration_activation_problems`), **activation needs a different person from whoever marked it ready** (`QM022` → `409 integration_rule`); editing after ready resets to draft; an active connection is paused before it can be changed. The REST adapter makes **no outbound calls until IN7** (SSRF defence). API `api/src/integrations/` (`core/connector.interface.ts`, `connector.registry.ts`, `providers/csv`, `providers/rest-api`): `GET /integrations/providers|connections`, `POST /integrations/connections`, `GET|PATCH …/{id}`, `PUT …/{id}/credential`, `POST …/{id}/credential/revoke|test|ready|activate|pause|resume|retire`. UI: Settings → Integrations (`Settings/SettingsIntegrations.jsx`, `integrations/ConnectionForm.jsx`, `integrations/ConnectionPage.jsx` at `/Settings/Integrations/:id`: settings, credential (write-only), test result, what is still needed, status steps by rights, history), hooks `useIntegrations.js`, `lib/integrations.js`, `PlugIcon`. Tests: `supabase/tests/integrations_core.test.sql`, `api/test/integrations.e2e-spec.ts` (+ both sweeps), `Integrations.test.jsx`. **IN2 (sync engine; migration `20260108100000_integration_sync_engine.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09):** `integration_sync_runs` (the queue and the record in one: kind preview/full/incremental/retry, status queued → running → succeeded | partial | failed | cancelled, counts, attempts, `next_attempt_at`, lease; **one queued or running run per connection and entity**), `integration_inbox` (staging, one row per source record per run: payload, mapped `canonical`, `content_hash` worked out by the database, status staged/previewed/unchanged/applied/rejected/failed/superseded, field-level errors; not audited; payloads cleared after 90 days except failed rows, `app.system_purge_integration_payloads`), `integration_external_records` (the map, PK (organisation, connection, entity, external id) → internal id + hash; **written only on create or when the hash changes, so an unchanged record writes nothing**), `integration_sync_cursors` (moved only when a full/incremental run finishes), `integration_run_errors` (append-only). People: `app.request_integration_run` (preview allowed before activation, real imports need `active`), `app.retry_integration_rows`, `app.cancel_integration_run(reason)` (`integrations.run`). **Runner functions `app.system_*` are API-login only**, take the organisation and re-check the run and the lease on every call: lease, stage (rejects missing/duplicate ids and rows with validation errors; same hash = unchanged), rows to apply, apply row (map + inbox in the importer's savepoint; one QMS record per source record), fail row, finish, run error (transient: back off 1, 2, 4… minutes or Retry-After, five attempts), release, runs due. A newer applied/unchanged copy of a record supersedes older failed copies; a retry applies only the newest. **`audit.record_change` now records `app.initiating_user` as the actor when `app.source = 'integration'`** (a signed-in person is always their own actor). API: `core/entity-importer.ts` (importer contract + `EntityImporterRegistry`, empty until IN4), `core/sync.engine.ts` (`SyncEngine.drain`: lease, fetch pages through the adapter's `fetchRecords` (`ConnectorError` for transient/permanent source errors), stage, apply in batches of 200 with a savepoint per row, finish; works ~20 s per call), `core/defaults.ts`; `ConnectorRegistry` and `EntityImporterRegistry` are Nest providers (tests override them); `GET /integrations/runs[?connectionId&status]`, `GET /integrations/runs/{id}` (`rowsNow`, `errors`), `GET …/{id}/rows[?status]`, `POST /integrations/connections/{id}/runs {entityType, kind}` (works it in the request; answer has `drain.state` finished/paused/waiting/failed/blocked), `POST …/connections/{id}/retry {entityType, rowIds?}`, `POST /integrations/runs/{id}/continue|cancel`, `GET /integrations/connections/{id}/external-records`; connections carry `runnable` (entities with a fetching adapter and an importer; `[]` in production until IN3/IN4); cron `GET|POST /internal/cron/integration-runs?limit` (not scheduled yet: IN8). UI: `integrations/ImportsCard.jsx` on the connection page (Run an import dialog, latest runs with a summary), `integrations/RunPage.jsx` at `/Settings/Integrations/:id/runs/:runId` (figures, waiting/failed/cancelled notices, source errors, rows by status with errors and the record as it arrived, Continue / Cancel with a reason / Retry failed rows). Tests: `supabase/tests/integrations_sync.test.sql`, `api/test/integration-runs.e2e-spec.ts` (fake adapter + fake importer; + both sweeps), `Integrations.test.jsx`. **Not built:** entity imports (IN4–IN6), the REST calls (IN7), schedules, payload purge cron, rejected-row edit/ignore (IN8), no subscription feature mapped. **IN3 (files, mappings, preview; migration `20260110100000_integration_files_mappings.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09):** a file connection (`csv`) imports **uploaded files**: the browser uploads to the private `integration-files` bucket at `<org>/integration/<connection>/<uuid>-<name>` (`uploadFileToSupabase(file, "integration-files", org, connectionId, "integration")`; uploads need `integrations.run`, reads `integrations.read`, via `app.integration_file_allowed`), then `POST /integrations/connections/{id}/files {path, fileName, size}`; the API downloads it with the service role (`IntegrationFileStore` / `SupabaseIntegrationFileStore`, **needs `SUPABASE_URL` + `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, else `503 file_storage_unavailable`**), parses it (`core/file-parsers.ts`: own RFC 4180 CSV with the connection's delimiter, encoding and heading setting; `.xlsx` first worksheet via `fflate`; ≤ 25 MB) and stores the rows as `integration_file_chunks` (≤ 1,000 rows) through `app.register_integration_file` / `add_integration_file_chunk` / `finish_integration_file` (`integration_files`: uploaded / parsed / failed with the reason / purged; the purge clears old chunks). **Mappings** (`integration_mappings`, versioned, frozen, one current per connection and kind of record; `app.save_integration_mapping(conn, entity, key column, fields, reason)` needs `integrations.manage`, a reason from version 2, unchanged refused): a key column and per Fortress field a closed transform (`none|upper|lower|map|constant|concat` + `dateFormat iso|dmy|mdy|excel`, `decimal`, `default`, `ownership`; `core/mapping.ts`, no expressions), checked against the field catalogue (`core/catalogue.ts`: suppliers, customers, parts, **machines, employees, gauge types, gauges, non-conformances** — the last five added by migration `20260111100000_integration_more_entities.sql`, which only widens `app.integration_entity_types()`; links to other records are codes in the file (site, department, part, machine, gauge type, unit, category, employee number), resolved when imported; gauge calibration dates/status are never imported; NCs arrive as drafts) and the file's columns before saving (`409` with `details.problems`). `app.request_integration_run(conn, entity, kind, file)`: a file connection needs a parsed file and a current mapping, no incremental; runs freeze `file_id` + `mapping_id`; the lease returns them and `app.system_integration_file_chunk` feeds the CSV adapter. **A preview needs only the mapping and catalogue; a real import needs an importer (none until IN4)** — connections carry `runnable` (catalogue ∪ importers) and `importable` (importers). API `integration-files.*`: `GET /integrations/entities`, `GET|POST /integrations/connections/{id}/files`, `GET /integrations/files/{id}` (20-row sample), `GET /integrations/connections/{id}/mappings?entityType`, `PUT /integrations/connections/{id}/mapping {entityType, keyField, fields, reason?, fileId?}`; runs carry `file` and `mappingVersion`, `POST …/runs` takes `fileId`. UI: `integrations/FilesCard.jsx` on a file connection's page (drop zone, files with status, **Map columns**), `integrations/MappingPage.jsx` at `/Settings/Integrations/:id/mapping?entity&file` (records and file combos, first rows, key column, per field Use / How / Column / When empty / translate lines / date format / decimal mark, suggestions from headings (`suggestColumns`), "Why is the mapping changing?" from version 2, server problems listed, **Preview this file**, versions), the Run dialog asks for the file and needs a mapping (Full disabled until the kind is `importable`, no Incremental for files), the run page names the file and mapping version; helpers `mappingToRows`/`rowsToFields`/`parseValueMap`/`fileProblem` in `lib/integrations.js`, hooks `useIntegrationEntities|Files|File|Mappings`, actions `registerFile`/`saveMapping`. Tests: `supabase/tests/integrations_files.test.sql`, `core/file-parsers.spec.ts`, `core/mapping.spec.ts`, `api/test/integration-files.e2e-spec.ts` (+ both sweeps), `IntegrationFiles.test.jsx`. **IN4 (real imports; migration `20260112100000_integration_imports.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09):** **file connections** are created `active` (no test / ready / activate / pause / resume; edit while active; retire in the page's "…" menu), and **whoever imports a file approves it**. **Import settings** per connection and kind of record (`integration_import_settings`, `app.set_integration_import_settings`; `GET|PUT /integrations/connections/{id}/settings` → `writeMode add_only|add_update|update_only`, `matchExisting`, `onMissing nothing|list|deactivate` (stored; acted on in IN8), `schedule manual|daily` (never for a file), `saved`, `fixed`, `canDeactivate`); **NCs are always add only, never matched**; runs freeze `settings`. Staging marks rows `skipped` with the reason (already imported under add only / not in Fortress under update only), matches hand-made records by code when asked, rejects a hand-made record already linked to another source record. **A real import or retry needs the write permission** for that kind (`app.integration_may_write`; connection detail `writable`, `fileConnection`). **References** (`ref` on catalogue fields; mapping field `match: code|source`): resolved before staging by `core/references.ts` through `app.system_integration_lookup` (source id → this connection's map; code → Fortress code, ignoring case; units resolve to their code; a department code at two sites is ambiguous) — an unresolved link rejects the row in the preview too. **Importers:** `core/record-importer.ts` (`RecordImporter`, registered for supplier, customer, part, machine, employee, gauge_type, gauge, nonconformance in `defaultImporterRegistry`) calls `app.system_import_record` (API login only), which re-checks run/lease/row and the frozen settings, **sets the requester's claims for the transaction** (created_by, table rules, audit actor) and writes through `app.import_<kind>`: suppliers pending approval, part revisions as drafts, gauges without calibration dates/status, NCs as drafts (detected today when no date) and never updated; an update changes only the mapped fields. UI: `ImportSettingsCard.jsx` on the mapping page (saved as chosen, optimistic: `useIntegrationSettings`, `useSaveImportSettings`), **Match by** per link field, file connection page without lifecycle buttons (history folded away), Run dialog "Import" (needs `importable` and `writable`; "You approve this import yourself"), run page Skipped tile and the settings used.
