Tests `supabase/tests/competence_core.test.sql` (+ tenancy sweep seeds), `api/test/competence.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep), `SettingsCompetence.test.jsx`, `CompetenceHome.test.jsx`, `competence.test.js`. **CO2 done** (migration `20260128100000_competence_assessments.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the new endpoints): `competence_employee_positions` (employee in the Employees register + position + `started_on`/`ended_on`; ended, never deleted; one open row per employee and position, no overlapping stints; written only by `app.assign_employee_position` / `app.end_employee_position`, `competence.manage`; a position someone holds cannot be retired) and `competence_assessments` (**append-only**: level 0–4, `assessed_on`, assessor, `expires_on`, note, `current`/`superseded`, `supersedes_id`; the trigger allows only current → superseded even for SQL, nothing deleted; written only by `app.record_competence_assessment`: `competence.assess`, **never the person's own** (`Employees.auth_id = auth.uid()`), level 4 also needs `competence.manage`, level 1–4 needs an active skill, a lower level needs a note, not dated in the future or before the one it replaces, expiry defaults from the skill's `refresh_months`, none for level 0). **Own record:** `app.competence_is_me` / `competence_has_own_record`; a member with a linked employee reads their own positions and assessments and the skills / positions lists whatever their role, and `app.my_visible_permissions` adds the menu-only flag `competence.own`. Gap, expiry (60-day warning, valid through the expiry date) and status are **worked out on read** (`api/src/competence/competence-state.ts`: `gap` > `not_assessed` > `expired` > `expiring` > `ok`; `extra` = assessed but nothing requires it; expiry never lowers a level). API (`competence-people.service.ts`): `GET /competence/matrix` (`positionId`, `departmentId`, `siteId`, `categoryId`, `skillId`, `status` all|attention|gap|expiring|expired, `q`, `page`, `pageSize`; ≤ 40 skill columns and ≤ 1,000 people, `…Truncated` flags), `GET /competence/me` (any member; `{ linked: false }` without a linked employee), `GET /competence/people/{employeeId}`, `POST …/positions {positionId, startedOn}`, `POST /competence/employee-positions/{id}/end {endedOn?}`, `POST …/assessments {skillId, level, assessedOn?, expiresOn?, note?}` (`competence.assess`); every command answers with the whole profile. UI `src/components/competence/`: `MatrixPage` (`/Competence/Matrix`, URL filters, a grid of people by skills tinted against the requirement), `PersonPage` (`/Competence/People/:employeeId`), `MyCompetencePage` (`/Competence/Mine`), shared `ProfileView` (positions with dates, skills needing attention first with history, `AssessDialog`, give / end a position), hooks `useCompetenceMatrix|Person|MyCompetence`, helpers `STATUS`, `statusLabel`, `statusClass`, `describeCell`; menu "Skills matrix" / "My competence" / "Skills and positions". Tests `supabase/tests/competence_assessments.test.sql` (+ sweep seeds), `competence-state.spec.ts`, `api/test/competence-people.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep), `CompetencePeople.test.jsx`, `competence.test.js`. **Not built:** training and evidence (CO3), the dashboard (CO4), department managers seeing their own department.
  **CO3 done** (migration `20260129100000_competence_training.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the training endpoints and the person profile, which now selects the new tables): `competence_training_sessions` (`TS-YY-NNNN` per organisation and year from `app.competence_session_counters`; kind course / on_the_job / external / induction / other; **`provider_type` internal names a trainer from the Employees register, external names the provider as text, exactly one of the two** (CHECK; the function clears the other side); status open → closed | cancelled (reason), a closed one is reopened with a reason; content editable only while open; never deleted), `competence_session_skills` (skills supported: information only, **training creates no assessment**), `competence_session_attendees` (enrolled → completed | failed | absent, or removed (reason); **completed / failed carry the person's own `completed_on`**, not in the future, not before the session started, a failure needs a note; a recorded result is frozen and corrected by **voiding** it with a reason and adding the person again while the session is open; unique per session and person while live; trigger holds the allowed moves even for SQL) and `competence_attachments` (files on a session or on one attendee, functions only, removed with a reason, never deleted; private `competence-files` bucket at `<org>/competence/session/<id>/…` and `<org>/competence/attendee/<id>/…`, `app.competence_file_allowed`). **No table writes: SECURITY DEFINER functions only, all need `competence.assess`** (`create_training_session(org, jsonb, employees[])`, `update_training_session`, `add_session_attendee`, `remove_session_attendee`, `record_session_attendees(session, attendees[], completed|failed|absent, on, note)` — several people on one day, call again on another day —, `void_session_attendee`, `close_training_session` (nobody still enrolled), `reopen_training_session`, `cancel_training_session` (no recorded result), `add_/remove_/restore_competence_attachment`). Reading: `competence.read` (Controller-seat holders too) or being a live attendee yourself (`app.competence_on_session`, `competence_is_me`). API (`competence-training.service.ts`, under `/competence`): `GET training-sessions` (page-based: `q`, `status` all|open|closed|cancelled, `skillId`, `employeeId`, `from`, `to`, `sort`, `order`; **membership only: RLS decides what is shown**), `POST training-sessions` (with `employeeIds`, `skillIds`), `GET|PATCH training-sessions/{id}`, `POST …/attendees|record|close|reopen|cancel|attachments`, `POST session-attendees/{id}/remove|void`, `POST competence-attachments/{id}/delete|restore`; every command answers with the whole session (attendees with files, files carry `sizeBytes`/`deletedAt`/`createdBy` for `AttachmentDrop`); the person profile gains `training`. UI: `TrainingPage` (`/Competence/Training`), `TrainingSessionPage` (`/Competence/Training/:id`: facts, attendee list with tick boxes, **Record a result** for the ticked people, void / remove with reasons, files and a per-person certificate dialog, Close / Reopen / Cancel), `SessionDialogs.jsx`, a Training card on the profile, menu "Training sessions"; hooks `useTrainingSessions|useTrainingSession`, helpers `SESSION_KINDS`, `providerLine`, `sessionNextStep`. Tests `supabase/tests/competence_training.test.sql` (+ sweep seeds), `api/test/competence-training.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep), `CompetenceTraining.test.jsx`. **Not built:** evidence files on an assessment, notifications to attendees, a session calendar.
  **CO4 done** (migration `20260130100000_competence_notice.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the notification preference list for everyone: it selects the new column): **the person assessed is told.** `notification_event_types.own_record` (new): such an event is visible to, and offered to, every member (the notice is addressed to one person, `notifications.recipient_user_id`), because the person assessed may hold no competence permission; `app.notification_can_see`, `list_notification_preferences`, `set_notification_preference` and `emit_notification` (the CAPA6 version plus a branch) were replaced. Event `competence_assessments.created` ("You were assessed", body "CMM operation — Supervised (2)") goes to the employee's **linked sign-in only**: no sign-in, no notice; the assessor is never told of their own action (actor excluded); superseding the earlier assessment announces nothing; can be switched off in Settings → Notifications; links to `/Competence/Mine` (`notificationLink`). **Dashboard:** `GET /competence-dashboard?from&to` (`competence.read`, `api/src/competence/competence-dashboard.ts`, no other migration): as of today — people with requirements, people with gaps and the pairs short (required level from the positions held now, `level` 0 when not assessed), current assessments expired / expiring in 30, 60 and 90 days (active people and skills, level 1+), people never assessed, gaps by skill (top 10), levels held, worst gaps (top 10 people), next expiries (≤ 90 days, soonest first), never-assessed people; for the period — sessions started, completed and failed results per week or month (by the person's own date), latest sessions. UI `CompetenceDashboard.jsx` is **now `/Competence`** (menu "Dashboard"; someone without `competence.read` is sent to `/Competence/Mine`; shared period picker, `?period=`), the old read-only skills and positions list moved to `/Competence/Skills` ("Skills and positions"); hook `useCompetenceDashboard`. **Matrix CSV export:** "Export CSV" on `/Competence/Matrix` fetches every page for the current filters (`useMatrixExport`, ≤ 1,000 people) and `matrixReport` (`lib/competence.js`) builds a report for `downloadReport` (per skill: level, needs, standing in words, expires; the file's first lines say what it is, as at, who it shows and what levels, needs and standing mean). Tests `supabase/tests/competence_notice.test.sql`, `api/test/competence-dashboard.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep query), `CompetenceDashboard.test.jsx`, matrix export test in `CompetencePeople.test.jsx`, `competence.test.js`, `notifications.test.js`, `MenuStructure.test.js`. **Not built:** a time per scheduled reminder, email/push for expiry, department-scoped dashboards.
  **CO5 done (exit check, `docs/competence-exit-report.md`, no migration):** live guardrails 0 violations (`supabase/checks/guardrails.sql` now lists the ten Competence tables in its audit rule), advisors show nothing new, and **`npm run journey` has a Competence step** (`competence()` in `scripts/journey/journey.cjs`: skill and position set up in Settings, Ada given the position and assessed one level short, a training session created, completed and closed, the dashboard and matrix show the gap, the matrix exports a CSV, and Ada's own sign-in (no Competence permission) sees only her record and has one "you were assessed" notice). The browser found two real faults, both fixed: **a page that redirects on a permission must wait for `usePermissions().isLoading`** (opening `/Competence` directly sent the owner to My competence), and the Competence pages had no outer padding (the page root is `flex min-h-0 w-full min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto bg-primary-bg p-4`, as on the dashboards). The Competence module is complete; not built: email/push, department-scoped reading, evidence files on an assessment, notices to attendees, a session calendar, a subscription feature.
- **Document control** (`docs/document-control-plan.md`, decisions DC1–DC16 agreed; slices DC1–DC6, **all done**, migration `20260202100000_document_control_core.sql` (applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the documents endpoints)): controlled documents in the Documentation module, an identity (`SOP-001`) separate from its revisions (rev A, B …); information only, never blocks work. **DC1 = types, documents and drafts:** `document_types` (seeded per organisation by `app.seed_document_types` + trigger: Procedure SOP, Work instruction WI, Form FRM, Specification SPEC, Policy POL, External EXT; prefix `^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9-]{0,9}$` unique per organisation and **fixed once a document uses it**; own sequence in `app.document_type_counters`; `review_months` 1–120, `content_hint` file|text, on/off, never deleted), `documents` (number from the type (`app.next_document_number`, `<prefix>-NNN`, skipping typed numbers) **or typed with its current letter when bringing a document across** (`number_typed`, `start_letter`; a typed `<prefix>-<n>` moves the sequence past it); unique ignoring case; `content_kind` file|text fixed; owner = active member; status `draft` (never in force) | active | withdrawn | discarded; `last_letter`), `document_revisions` (sequence; **letter null until submitted** (DC2) and never reused, `app.document_letter_after` A…Z, AA…; status draft → in_review → awaiting_approval → approved → effective → superseded | withdrawn, or discarded with a reason; **one in progress per document**; content (change summary, `body`) changes only while draft, trigger; `body` = **TipTap JSON only** (type "doc", ≤ 500 kB) plus `body_text` for search worked out by `app.document_body_text`, **no stored HTML**) and `document_revision_files` (main | attachment, **one live main**, a new main replaces the old ("Replaced by …"), only while draft, removed with a reason, never deleted; private `document-files` bucket at `<org>/document/<revision id>/<uuid>-<name>`, `app.document_file_allowed`). **No table writes: SECURITY DEFINER functions only**, `documentation.manage`: `app.save_document_type(org, jsonb, id?)`, `create_document(org, jsonb)` (+ first draft), `update_document(id, jsonb)` (title, description, owner), `update_document_revision(id, jsonb)` (changeSummary, body), `discard_document_revision(id, reason)` (the only draft of a never-effective document discards the document), `add_ / remove_ / restore_document_file`. Reading: `app.document_readable` / `document_revision_readable` (documentation.manage, the author, the owner; others with `documentation.read` once a revision is approved). Errors **`QM025` → `409 document_rule`**, other organisation `P0002` → 404. API `api/src/documents/`: `GET|POST /document-types` (`all=true`), `PATCH /document-types/{id}`, `GET /documents` (page-based: `q` over number, title, description, text and file names; `type` (ids), `status` (discarded only when asked), `kind`, `owner=me`, `sort` number|title|type|status|owner|updatedAt), `POST /documents`, `GET|PATCH /documents/{id}` (with `revisions`, `inProgress`, `nextLetter`, `canManage`), `GET|PATCH /document-revisions/{id}` (`plannedLetter`, `body`, `files`, `canEdit`), `POST …/discard`, `GET|POST …/files`, `POST /document-files/{id}/delete|restore`. UI `src/components/documents/`: register `/Documentation/Documents` (`DocumentsPage`, "Documents" first under Documentation), `NewDocumentDialog` (type, content kind, owner, "bringing an existing document across"), `DocumentPage` (`/Documentation/Documents/:id`: details, draft-in-progress callout, revisions, Edit details), `RevisionPage` (`…/revisions/:revisionId`: **`DocumentEditor.jsx`** (TipTap StarterKit + TableKit: headings, bold, italic, lists, tables, links, undo; saved as typing pauses via the optimistic `useSaveDraft`) or the main-file `AttachmentDrop`, attachments, change summary saved on leaving, "still needed" list, Discard), Settings → Data Management → **Documents** (`Settings/SettingsDocuments.jsx`, managers only; optimistic on/off via `useToggleDocumentType`), hooks `useDocuments.js`, helpers `lib/documents.js` (`REVISION_STATUS`, `revisionLabel`, `draftProblems`, `documentPath`, `revisionPath`), CSS `.document-text` in `index.css`. Tests `supabase/tests/document_control_core.test.sql` (+ tenancy sweep seeds, guardrails list), `api/test/documents.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep), `documents/Documents.test.jsx`. **DC2 done** (migration `20260203100000_document_control_review.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the documents endpoints, which settle and select the new columns): `document_revision_reviewers` (named on a draft, replaced as a whole) + `document_revisions.approver_id` via `app.set_document_signers` (none the author; a reviewer may approve); `app.submit_document_revision` (empty "still needed" list `app.document_revision_problems`; gives the letter the first time; new `review_round` with a pending `document_signoffs` row per reviewer); `app.review_document_revision(rev, accept, comment)` (pending reviewer of the round; sending back needs a comment, returns it to draft and closes the round; all accepted → `awaiting_approval` + approver sign-off); `app.approve_document_revision(rev, effective_from, comment)` (the named approver, `documentation.issue`, never the author, today or later) and `app.send_back_document_revision`; **settling without a worker**: `app.settle_document` / `settle_documents(org)` (API runs it on every documents read) / `system_settle_documents()` (API login; cron `/internal/cron/settle-documents`, 00:15 UTC) make an approved revision `effective` on its date, supersede the previous (`superseded_by_id`) and set `documents.current_revision_id` / status active; `app.withdraw_document(doc, reason)` (`documentation.issue`; the revision in force and any approved one withdrawn, a draft left alone); `app.start_document_revision(doc)` (copies text and live files of the revision in force, `previous_revision_id`); status moves of §3 held by trigger; decided sign-offs frozen; `app.document_signer_candidates(org)`; reviewers and the approver read the revision and its document (`app.document_signer_of`). Notice `document_signoffs.created` ("A document is waiting for you", own_record, addressed to the person; `emit_notification` = the 20260201100000 merge + this branch; link to the revision). API: `GET /document-signers`, `GET /document-reviews/mine`, `PUT /document-revisions/{id}/signers`, `POST …/submit|review|approve|send-back`, `GET …/compare?with=` (`document-compare.ts`: blocks and files added/removed/kept), `POST /documents/{id}/revisions|withdraw`; revisions carry `problems`, `reviewers`, `approver`, `signoffs`, `reviewRound`, `myTurn`, `effectiveFrom`, …; documents `currentRevision`, `canIssue`, `canStartRevision`, `withdrawReason`. UI `documents/ReviewPanels.jsx` (`SignersCard` optimistic via `useSetSigners`, `SignoffsCard`, `MyTurnCard` with Accept / Approve (effective date) / Send back, `CompareCard`, `SubmitButton`), status callouts on the revision page, "in force" / withdrawn callouts, **Start a new revision** and **Withdraw** on the document page, "waiting for you" on the register and an In force column. Tests `supabase/tests/document_control_review.test.sql`, `api/test/document-review.e2e-spec.ts`, `document-compare.spec.ts`, `Documents.test.jsx`. **DC3 done** (migration `20260204100000_document_control_readers.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the documents and competence profile endpoints): `document_targets` (department / role / named person, replaced as a whole by `app.set_document_targets`), `document_recipients` **fixed when a revision takes effect** (`app.settle_document` resolves `app.resolve_document_targets`; `reached_via`, `due_on` = effective date + `document_revisions.acknowledge_within_days` (1–365, on the draft), first view, acknowledgement + note; frozen once recorded; functions only: `app.mark_document_viewed` (the API calls it on every open), `app.acknowledge_document(rev, note)` (the revision in force, once), `app.add_document_recipient(rev, user)` (manage, "Added")), `documents.restricted` (only readers (`app.document_is_reader`: targets now or recipients of any revision), managers, owner, authors, signers), `document_skills` (`app.set_document_skills`) and `app.competence_document_evidence(employee)` ("Documents read" on the competence profile, evidence only). Notice `document_recipients.created` ("A document for you to read", no actor, emailed by default). API: `PUT /documents/{id}/readers|skills`, `POST /document-revisions/{id}/acknowledge`, `GET|POST …/recipients`, `GET /my-documents`, `GET /my-documents/count` (`{ toRead, overdue, reviews, total }`); documents carry `restricted`, `targets`, `skills`, `readers`, `toAcknowledge`; revisions `acknowledgeWithinDays`, `myReading`, `readers`; the competence profile `documentsRead`. UI `documents/ReadersPanels.jsx` (`ReadersCard` with the Restricted switch (optimistic `useSetRestricted`), `SkillsCard`, `AcknowledgeDaysField`, `AcknowledgeCard`, `RecipientsCard`), `MyDocumentsPage` at `/Documentation/My-Documents` ("My documents" first under Documentation, badge `my-documents`; the Documentation badge is now `documentation` = alerts + documents), "Documents read" in competence `ProfileView`. Tests `supabase/tests/document_control_readers.test.sql`, `api/test/document-readers.e2e-spec.ts`, `Documents.test.jsx`. **DC4 done** (migration `20260205100000_document_control_periodic_links.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the documents endpoints, which select the new columns): `documents.review_months` (own interval, null = the type's; `app.document_review_months`) and `next_review_on` (set by `app.settle_document` = effective date + interval, by a no-change review = today + interval, by changing the interval; cleared when withdrawn; backfilled), `document_periodic_reviews` (append-only, frozen by trigger; `app.record_document_review(doc, no_change|revision_started, note)`, manage; no change needs a note, revision started calls `app.start_document_revision`), `document_change_requests` (`DCR-YY-NNNN` from `app.document_change_request_counters`; `app.raise_document_change_request` (anyone who may read the document), `accept_` (manage; a new draft or the draft in progress, refused while in review / approved), `decline_(id, reason)`, `withdraw_` (the person who asked); readable by the person who asked and managers; final once decided; notice `document_change_requests.updated` to the person who asked, only on accept / decline), `document_links` (part / part family / machine / operation code, replaced as a whole by `app.set_document_links`). `emit_notification` = the 20260204100000 version + the change-request branch. API: `POST /documents/{id}/reviews`, `PUT /documents/{id}/links`, `GET /document-change-requests` (`status` open default | accepted | declined | withdrawn | all, `documentId`, `mine`), `POST /documents/{id}/change-requests`, `POST /document-change-requests/{id}/accept|decline|withdraw`, `GET /documents-in-force?partId|partFamilyId|machineId|operationCode` (a part matches its family too; `toAcknowledge`); documents carry `reviewMonths`, `reviewMonthsEffective`, `nextReviewOn`, `reviewOverdue`, `reviews`, `links`, `changeRequests`; the register takes `reviewOverdue=true` and `sort=nextReview`. UI `documents/LifecyclePanels.jsx` (`PeriodicReviewCard`, `ChangeRequestsCard` + `ChangeRequestRow`, `LinksCard`), `ChangeRequestsPage` at `/Documentation/Change-Requests` (menu "Change requests"), `DocumentsInForce.jsx` (banner beside `ActiveAlertsBanner` on the part, part family, machine and Start inspection pages; `DocumentsInForceList` in a "Docs" pane on the job hub), Next review column and "Review overdue" filter on the register. Tests `supabase/tests/document_control_periodic_links.test.sql`, `api/test/document-periodic-links.e2e-spec.ts`, `Documents.test.jsx`. **DC5 done** (migration `20260206200000_document_control_external.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the documents endpoints, which select `origin` / `issuer_revision`): **external documents** (`documents.origin` internal|external fixed once created, `issuer`, `external_reference`; always a file; revisions carry `issuer_revision`; never reviewed here — `app.issue_external_revision(revision, issuer_revision, effective_from, summary?)` (`documentation.issue`) gives our letter, approves and settles; `app.create_external_document`, `app.update_external_details`; `app.document_revision_problems` asks an external draft only for its file and, after the first issue, what changed), **controlled copies** (`GET /document-files/{id}/controlled-copy`: the API reads the PDF with the service key through `DocumentFileStore` (`503 file_storage_unavailable` without it) and stamps every page with pdf-lib (`api/src/documents/controlled-copy.ts`; controlled copy in force, or NOT FOR USE for drafts, superseded and withdrawn); PDFs only, `409 document_rule` otherwise; written documents print with the same stamp from the browser (`copyStamp`), other files say "Uncontrolled when printed or saved"; `apiFetch(path, { as: "blob" })`) and the **documents dashboard** (`GET /documents-dashboard?from&to`, `documents-dashboard.ts`; page `DocumentsDashboard.jsx` at `/Documentation`, menu "Dashboard"). API: `POST /documents` takes `origin`/`issuer`/`externalReference`, `PATCH` takes `issuer`/`externalReference`, `POST /document-revisions/{id}/issue-external`; revisions carry `issuerRevision`, `canRecordIssue`. UI: External switch in `NewDocumentDialog`, **Record this issue** dialog on `RevisionPage`, `CopiesCard` (`ControlledCopy.jsx`), hooks `useControlledCopy`, `useDocumentsDashboard`. Tests `supabase/tests/document_control_external.test.sql`, `controlled-copy.spec.ts`, `api/test/document-external.e2e-spec.ts`, `Documents.test.jsx`. Plan §16. **DC6 done (exit check, `docs/document-control-exit-report.md`, no migration):** live guardrails 0 violations, advisors show nothing new, and **`npm run journey` has a document-control step** (`documentControl()` in `scripts/journey/journey.cjs`: write in the editor → reviewer, approver, readers → send back → fix → accept → approve for today → in force → the reader acknowledges → rev B started → dashboard). The browser found and fixed: **typing just before Send for review lost the last words** (the editor saves on a 0.8 s pause; `DocumentEditor` now takes `flushRef` / `onPendingChange`, the revision page saves what is waiting and waits for it before submitting or recording an issue, and says "unsaved changes"), **switching the editor to read-only fired a save** (`setEditable(editable, false)`, no saves while read-only), and `RequireModule` **asked for the plan catalogue for everyone** (403 for anyone without `subscription.read`; now only when the module is locked and the person may read subscriptions, `usePlanCatalogue({ enabled })`). The Document control module is complete. **Not built:** page watermarks, paper copy registers, stamping Word/Excel, images inside the text, a subscription feature.
- **Risk register** (`docs/risk-management-plan.md`, decisions RM1–RM14 agreed; slices RK1–RK4 then management review MR1–MR3 and exit check RX; **RK1 done**, migration `20260208200000_risk_register_core.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12 (both organisations got the default matrix and ten categories); pushing the API before it leaves the risk endpoints failing): one register of **risks and opportunities**, information only. Permissions `risk.read` / `.manage` / `.approve` (owner, admin, quality manager all; quality engineer read + manage; inspector, viewer read). **Matrix:** `risk_models` = the organisation's 5 × 5 scoring as **versions** (`config`: five `likelihood` and five `consequence` labels, `bands[likelihood-1][consequence-1]` low|medium|high|critical, `acceptanceBand` low|medium|high, `reviewMonths` per band; `app.risk_model_problem`; frozen by trigger; default seeded per organisation by `app.seed_risk_model` + trigger: score 1–4 low, 5–9 medium, 10–16 high, 17–25 critical); a new version only via `app.save_risk_model(org, config, reason)` (**`risk.approve`**, unchanged refused); `app.score_risk(org, likelihood, consequence)` (score, band, labels, `acceptable`, model id / version). `risk_categories` (ten seeded, RLS insert / update under `risk.manage`, key fixed, switched off never deleted). `risks` (`RSK-001` … from `app.risk_counters`; kind risk | opportunity; category, title, description, cause, effect, owner = active member, optional site / department; status draft → open (first assessment), retired with a reason and brought back; CHECK already allows treating / monitoring / closed for RK2) and `risk_assessments` (**append-only, frozen by trigger even for SQL**; phase inherent; likelihood, consequence, score, band, the model version it was scored with, rationale; a new one supersedes). **No table writes on risks or assessments — SECURITY DEFINER functions only** (`risk.manage`): `app.create_risk(org, jsonb)`, `update_risk(id, jsonb)` (the kind only while a draft; nothing once closed or retired), `assess_risk(risk, likelihood, consequence, rationale)` (the database scores it), `retire_risk(id, reason)`, `reactivate_risk(id)`. Errors **`QM026` → `409 risk_rule`**, other organisation `P0002` → 404. API `api/src/risk/`: `GET|PUT /risk-model` (`canChange`, `versions`), `GET /risk-score?likelihood&consequence` (nothing saved), `GET|POST /risk-categories` (`all=true`), `PATCH /risk-categories/{id}`, `GET /risks` (page-based: `q`, `kind`, `status` default `live` (not retired) | `all` | statuses, `band` incl. `unassessed`, `categoryId`, `owner=me`, `sort` reference|title|category|owner|status|score|assessedOn|createdAt), `POST /risks`, `GET|PATCH /risks/{id}` (`inherent`, `assessments`, `canManage`, `canEdit`), `POST /risks/{id}/assessments|retire|reactivate`. UI `src/components/risk/`: menu **Risk** (`ShieldAlertIcon`) → register `/Risk/Register` (`RiskRegisterPage`; `/Risk` redirects there until the dashboard of RK4), risk page `/Risk/Register/:id` (`RiskPage`), `RiskDialogs.jsx` (`RiskDialog` raise / edit, `AssessDialog` = choose a cell + why, `RetireDialog`), **`RiskMatrix.jsx`** (the reusable 5 × 5 grid: every cell says its score and band in words, `onPick` makes cells buttons, `selected`, `marks`, `compact`), Settings → Data Management → **Risk** (`Settings/SettingsRisk.jsx`: matrix, **Change the matrix** for approvers (labels, click a cell to move its band on, acceptable level, review months, reason → a new version), categories with an optimistic on/off via `useToggleRiskCategory`), hooks `useRisk.js`, helpers `lib/risk.js` (`BAND_CELL`, `BAND_PILL`, `cellBand`, `isAcceptable`, `modelProblems`, `riskPath`). Tests `supabase/tests/risk_register_core.test.sql` (+ sweep seed, guardrails list), `api/test/risk.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep), `risk/Risk.test.jsx`. Plan §11. RK2 (treatment, actions, residual assessments, acceptance, reviews) is below. **Not built yet (RK3–RK4):** links and raise-from, heat map, dashboard, notices; not browser-checked.
- **Risk register RK2 — treatment, acceptance, reviews** (`docs/risk-management-plan.md` §12; migration `20260210100000_risk_treatment.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12; pushing the API before it breaks every risk endpoint: they select the new columns and call the new functions): `risks` gains `strategy` (mitigate / avoid / transfer / accept; an opportunity: exploit / accept = leave), `treatment_plan`, `next_review_on`, `outcome` (treated / accepted / no_longer_applies), `close_note`, `closed_at/by`, `reopen_reason`. New tables `risk_actions` (owner = active member, due date; open → done with a note | cancelled with a reason, reopened; never deleted), `risk_reviews` (**append-only, frozen even for SQL**: `no_change` with a note, or `reassessed` written by every assessment after the first) and `risk_acceptances` (proposed → approved | rejected | withdrawn, decided once, the band and the assessment it names). **No table writes: SECURITY DEFINER functions only** (`risk.manage` unless said): `app.assess_risk(risk, l, c, rationale, phase default 'inherent')` (replaces RK1's; **`residual` = what is left after treatment**, never before an inherent one; withdraws a waiting acceptance; moves the review date), `set_risk_treatment(risk, strategy, plan)`, `add_ / update_ / cancel_ / reopen_risk_action`, `complete_risk_action(id, note)` (**a manager, or the person it was given to with only `risk.read`**), `propose_risk_acceptance(risk, rationale ≥ 10)`, `decide_risk_acceptance(id, approve, note)` (**`risk.approve`, never the proposer**; also held by the frozen trigger), `withdraw_risk_acceptance` (proposer or approver), `record_risk_review(risk, note, next?)`, `close_risk(risk, outcome, note)` (cancels open actions), `reopen_risk(risk, reason)` (due for review today); `retire_risk` now refuses open actions. **The status is worked out, never set** (`app.risk_recompute_status`): draft → open (assessed) → treating (a strategy) → monitoring when `app.risk_treatment_problems(risk)` (the "still needed" list, `risk.read`) is empty → closed. **The assessment in force** = residual when there is one, else inherent (`app.risk_effective_assessment`); a risk whose band in force is above the matrix's `acceptanceBand` reaches monitoring (or closes as accepted) only with an **approved acceptance of that very assessment** (`app.risk_acceptance_state` → above / accepted / waiting; assessing again asks afresh); within the band no acceptance exists (proposing is refused); opportunities never need one. Non-accept strategies need a residual assessment before monitoring. **Review dates:** `next_review_on` = day of the assessment or review + `reviewMonths[band in force]` of the current matrix (or the date given, after today, ≤ 5 years); null when closed or retired; **overdue is worked out on read**. API (every command answers with the whole risk): `POST /risks/{id}/assessments` takes `phase`, `PUT /risks/{id}/treatment {strategy, plan}`, `POST /risks/{id}/actions`, `PATCH /risk-actions/{id}`, `POST /risk-actions/{id}/complete {note}` (route needs only `risk.read`; the database decides) `|cancel {reason}|reopen`, `POST /risks/{id}/acceptances {rationale}`, `POST /risk-acceptances/{id}/decide {approve, note}|withdraw {reason}`, `POST /risks/{id}/reviews {note, nextReviewOn?}`, `POST /risks/{id}/close {outcome, note}|reopen {reason}`; risks carry `residual`, `band` (in force), `strategy`, `treatmentPlan`, `openActions`, `nextReviewOn`, `reviewOverdue`, `outcome`, `closeNote`, `closedAt/By`, `reopenReason`, and the detail `actions` (`mine`), `reviews`, `acceptances` (`inForce`, `canDecide`, `canWithdraw`), `problems`, `acceptance {acceptanceBand, above, accepted, waiting}`, `canApprove`; `GET /risks` takes `strategy` (incl. `none`), `reviewOverdue=true`, `sort=residual|nextReview`, and **`band` now filters on the band in force**. UI: `risk/RiskTreatment.jsx` (`TreatmentCard` with the actions, `AcceptanceCard`, `ReviewsCard`, dialogs `TreatmentDialog`, `ActionDialog`, `DecideDialog`, `ReviewDialog`, `CloseDialog` and the generic `TextDialog` for one note or reason), `RiskPage` (a **Still needed** callout from `problems`, Before / After treatment cards (`AssessmentView`), Close / Reopen, closed and overdue callouts), `AssessDialog phase="residual"`, register columns After treatment and Next review with a **Review overdue** toggle (`?overdue=true`) and the new statuses; helpers `strategyOptions(kind)`, `outcomeOptions(kind)`, `reviewWords`, `actionOverdue`, `ACTION_STATUS`, `ACCEPTANCE_STATUS` in `lib/risk.js`. Tests `supabase/tests/risk_treatment.test.sql` (+ sweep seeds, guardrails list), `api/test/risk.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep), `risk/Risk.test.jsx`. **Not built yet:** links and raise-from (RK3); heat map, dashboard, notice to the person an action is given to, menu badge (RK4); not browser-checked (RX).
- **Risk register RK3 — links and raising a risk from a record** (`docs/risk-management-plan.md` §13; migration `20260214100000_risk_links.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12; pushing the API before it breaks the risk detail and the new endpoints): `risk_links` (one target per row: `ncr` / `capa` / `audit_finding` / `supplier` / `part` / `part_family` / `machine` / `document` (typed columns with own-organisation composite FKs) or `operation_code` (text); unique per risk and target; audited; RLS `risk.read`; **no table writes**), replaced as a whole by `app.set_risk_links(risk, jsonb)` (`risk.manage`, while the risk can still change; duplicates dropped, codes trimmed; another organisation's record is refused `QM026` "That part is not in this organisation"). **Raising from a record:** `risks.origin_kind` (`ncr` | `capa` | `audit_finding`) + `origin_id`, set only by `app.raise_risk_from(kind, id, data, links)` (calls `app.create_risk`, links the source first) and **never changed afterwards** (trigger `risks_origin_fixed`, even for SQL); **the link to the origin cannot be removed**. `app.risk_draft_from(kind, id)` (needs read of the source — `ncr.read` at its site, `capa.read`, `audits.read` — and `risk.read`; `P0002` otherwise) suggests: from an NC its title and description, category supplier (supplier / goods receiving source or a supplier named), customer (customer source) or product, and links to its part, supplier and causal machine; from an 8D its title, problem statement, category process and its **verified** root causes as the cause; from an audit finding its title, what was found, the requirement as the cause, category compliance and the suppliers and machines the audit was carried out on. API: `GET /risk-draft?kind&id` (nothing saved; suggested links with labels), `POST /risks` takes `raisedFrom {kind, id}` and `links`, `PUT /risks/{id}/links {links: [{kind, id} | {kind: 'operation_code', code}]}`, `GET /risks/linked?kind&id` (risks linked to one record, not retired, closed last, plus `canRaise`; declared before `risks/:id`); the risk detail carries `links` (`kind`, `id`, `code`, `reference`, `name`, `readable` — **a record the caller may not read under its own RLS has no name**) and `origin`. UI: `risk/RiskLinks.jsx` (`LinksCard` on the risk page with **Change links** → `LinksDialog`: the origin marked "Raised from this" and not removable, Add a link to (kind combo) + the shared NC / part / supplier / machine pickers or a search of 8Ds, audit findings, documents, part families, or an operation code typed in; `LinkList` chips to each record's page via `linkPath`), "Raised from …" under the risk's title, `risk/LinkedRisks.jsx` (the **Risks** card: risks linked to a record with level, status and overdue review, and **Raise a risk** on an NC, an 8D or an audit finding for `risk.manage` → `RiskDialog draft=…` prefilled, the source named and its suggested links listed and removable; hidden without `risk.read`) on the NC page (own section), 8D step D7, the audit finding page, the supplier profile's Overview and the machine dialog; helpers `LINK_KINDS`, `linkKindLabel`, `linkPath`, `linkWords`, `linksBody`, `sameLink` in `lib/risk.js`, hooks `useLinkedRisks`, `useRiskDraft`, mutation `setLinks`. Tests `supabase/tests/risk_links.test.sql` (+ sweep seed, guardrails list), `api/test/risk.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep: B gets its own NC for the draft check), `risk/Risk.test.jsx`. **Not built yet:** heat map, dashboard, notices, menu badge (RK4); risks on the part, part family and document pages; not browser-checked (RX).
- **Risk register RK4 — heat map, dashboard, my work, notices** (`docs/risk-management-plan.md` §14; migration `20260215100000_risk_notices.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12; pushing the API before it only leaves the notices unsent): notices `risk_actions.created|updated` ("A risk action was given to you") and `risks.created|updated` ("A risk was given to you"), addressed to the action's / risk's person (`recipient_user_id`, `risk.read`), only when added with someone on it or handed over, **never written when that person did it**, emailed by default; `app.emit_notification` = the `20260205100000` version + these branches (`risk_id` in details; links `/Risk/Register/:id` in `notificationLink` and the email `notificationPath`). API `api/src/risk/risk-dashboard.ts` (read only, RLS): `GET /risk-heatmap?phase=inherent|residual|effective&kind&categoryId&owner=me` (live risks per cell, ≤ 50 listed each, `placed` / `notPlaced`), `GET /risks-dashboard?from&to` (as of today: live risks / opportunities, unassessed, high or critical in force and untreated, reviews overdue, open / overdue actions, acceptances waiting, by band / status / category / strategy, top risks and lists; for the period: raised, from a record, closed, assessments, trend), `GET /risk-work/mine` (my reviews due within 30 days or overdue, my open actions, acceptances I may decide) and `GET /risk-work/count` (badge `total` = overdue reviews + open actions + acceptances). UI: `/Risk` is now the dashboard (`RiskDashboard.jsx`, shared period picker), `/Risk/Heat-map` (`RiskHeatMapPage.jsx`: In force / Before / After treatment, kind, mine, category, a chosen cell lists its risks; all in the address), `/Risk/My-Work` (`MyRiskWorkPage.jsx`: mark an action done, reviews due, acceptances to decide), menu badge `my-risk-work` (`useMyRiskCount` in `Navbar.jsx`), hooks `useRiskHeatmap|useRiskDashboard|useMyRiskWork|useMyRiskCount`, icons `HandshakeIcon`, `SparklesIcon`. Tests `supabase/tests/risk_notices.test.sql`, `api/test/risk.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep query), `risk/Risk.test.jsx`, `notifications.test.js`, `email.spec.ts`. **Not built:** due-date reminders, risks on the home page, heat map over time; not browser-checked (RX).
- **Management review MR1 — quality objectives** (`docs/risk-management-plan.md` §15; migration `20260216100000_quality_objectives.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12; pushing the API before it leaves only the quality objective endpoints failing): permissions **`management_review.read` / `.manage`** (owner, admin, quality manager both; quality engineer read), errors **`QM027` → `409 management_review_rule`**. `quality_objectives` (`QO-001`; owner = active member; **measure** `manual` or a fixed system measure `ncr_count` / `supplier_ppm` / `capa_on_time` / `audit_pass_rate`, each with its own unit and direction; target; frequency monthly / quarterly / yearly; first period aligned; measure, frequency and first period fixed once created, even for SQL; retired with a reason) and `quality_objective_values` (manual only, one current per period, append-only, a correction supersedes and needs a note). Functions only: `app.save_quality_objective(org, jsonb, id?)`, `retire_ / reactivate_quality_objective`, `record_quality_objective_value(objective, day, value, note)` (a manager **or the objective's owner**). Read with `management_review.read` or as the owner (RLS). **System measures are worked out by the API on read, as the person** (`api/src/management-review/quality-objectives.service.ts` `systemValues`; "not available" without the source module's read permission, never zero). API: `GET /quality-objective-measures`, `GET|POST /quality-objectives`, `GET|PATCH /quality-objectives/{id}?periods`, `POST …/retire|reactivate|values` (list and detail and values need membership only: RLS decides). UI: menu **Management review** (`PresentationIcon`, `/Management-Review` → Objectives), `management-review/ObjectivesPage.jsx`, `ObjectivePage.jsx`, `ObjectiveDialogs.jsx`, `ObjectiveChart.jsx`; hooks `useManagementReview.js`, helpers `lib/qualityObjectives.js`. Tests `supabase/tests/quality_objectives.test.sql` (+ sweep seed, guardrails list), `api/test/quality-objectives.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep), `QualityObjectives.test.jsx`. **Next:** MR2 (reviews), MR3, RX.
- **Management review MR2 — the reviews** (`docs/risk-management-plan.md` §16; migration `20260217100000_management_reviews.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12; pushing the API before it leaves only the review endpoints failing): `management_reviews` (`MR-YY-NN`, period ≤ 3 years, chair, minutes TipTap JSON; planned → in_progress → completed | cancelled), `management_review_inputs` (twelve fixed sections from `app.management_review_sections()`, two commentary-only), `management_review_attendees`, `management_review_decisions`, `management_review_actions` (readable by their owner without the permission; an owner or a manager closes them). Functions only (`management_review.manage`): `app.plan_ / update_ / start_ / complete_ / cancel_management_review`, `set_management_review_snapshot` (the API's gathering), `set_management_review_input(input, commentary, reviewed)`, `set_management_review_attendees`, `set_management_review_minutes`, `add_ / update_ / remove_management_review_decision`, `add_ / update_ / complete_ / cancel_ / reopen_management_review_action`, `management_review_problems` (the still-needed list). **A completed or cancelled review, its inputs, attendees and decisions never change (triggers, even for SQL).** **Gathering:** `api/src/management-review/review-inputs.ts` (`ReviewInputsService`) calls each module's dashboard service **as the person** for the review's period and stores one snapshot shape `{ figures, lists, notes }`; a source the person may not read → "not available", never zeros. API `management-reviews.*` (list, plan, detail, update, start (gathers), gather, inputs, attendees, minutes, decisions, actions, complete, cancel; every command answers with the whole review). UI `management-review/ReviewsPage.jsx`, `ReviewPage.jsx`, `ReviewParts.jsx` (`SnapshotView`, `InputCard`), `ReviewDialogs.jsx`, `ReviewReportPage.jsx` (print); hooks `useManagementReviews|useManagementReview|useReviewMutation|useReviewInput` (optimistic commentary / reviewed). Tests `supabase/tests/management_reviews.test.sql` (+ sweep seed, guardrails list), `api/test/management-reviews.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep), `ManagementReviews.test.jsx`. **Next:** MR3 (action owner notice, dashboard card), RX.
- **Management review MR3 — notices, my actions, home card** (`docs/risk-management-plan.md` §17; migration `20260218100000_management_review_notices.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12; pushing the API before it breaks My review actions): notice `management_review_actions.created|updated` (own_record, addressed to the action's person, never to the giver, emailed by default; link `/Management-Review/My-Actions`; `emit_notification` = the `20260215100000` version + this branch). `app.my_review_action_reviews(org)` names the reviews I hold an action from without reading them. API `GET /management-review-actions/mine?status` (membership only; `{ data, openCount, overdueCount }`), `GET /management-review-overview` (`management_review.read`). UI `MyReviewActionsPage.jsx` (`/Management-Review/My-Actions`, Mark done; menu "My actions", badge `my-review-actions`), `ManagementReviewCard.jsx` on the home page (next review, last completed, open / overdue actions, objectives), **For you** tiles for risk work and review actions (`useHomeDashboard`). Tests `supabase/tests/management_review_notices.test.sql`, `management-reviews.e2e-spec.ts`, `ManagementReviews.test.jsx`, `notifications.test.js`, `email.spec.ts`. The management review module is complete. **RX done (exit check, `docs/risk-management-exit-report.md`, no migration):** live guardrails 0 violations, advisors show nothing new, and **`npm run journey` has a risk and management review step** (`riskAndReview()` in `scripts/journey/journey.cjs`: risk raised from the NC, assessed, treated, residual still high, accepted by Colin, reviewed, heat map; a quality objective; a review planned, started, every section reviewed, a decision and an action for Colin, completed; Colin closes the action; the report). The browser found two real faults, both fixed: **the "Reviewed" tick did not show at once** (`InputCard` keeps its own `ticked` state, put back on error) and **two overlapping saves of one section could undo each other** (`useReviewInput` writes the server's answer to the cache only when it is the last save in flight). Journey notes: a required field's accessible name includes the CSS `*` (match `/^To\b/`, not exact), and match textboxes by role. Both modules are complete.
- **OAuth client credentials for REST connections** (`docs/email-oauth-plan.md` OA1, decision O1; migration `20260131100000_integration_oauth_client.sql` (written as `20260124100000_…` and renamed after the parallel audit-fix and competence migrations took that number; same content), applied live 2026-11-11, live guardrails 0 violations): sign-in type `oauth_client` with `oauthTokenUrl` (required; same rules as the API address: https, 443, a public host name — it may be another host, e.g. Microsoft Entra, Auth0, Okta), optional `oauthScope`, `oauthAudience`, `oauthClientAuth` (`body` default | `basic`, RFC 6749 §2.3.1 encoding). The credential is one Vault secret, JSON `{clientId, clientSecret}` (checked by `app.set_integration_credential`). **The token host must be on the organisation's allow-list too** (`app.integration_activation_problems`; `SafeHttpClient` refuses it at run time). `SafeHttpClient.postForm` (same SSRF rules, form body ≤ 16 kB); `providers/rest-api/oauth.ts` (`OAuthTokens`): token asked for with `grant_type=client_credentials`, **kept in memory only** until a minute before `expires_in` (default 300 s, ≤ 24 h), keyed by a hash of address/scope/audience/client, never stored or logged; a 401 from the API drops it and asks once more; the sign-in server's `error`/`error_description` are reported; 429/5xx/timeouts are transient. The connection test says "Signed in at <host>; The API answered …". UI: "OAuth client credentials" in How we sign in (token address, scope, audience, how the client is sent), the credential dialog asks Client id + Client secret. **Fixed at the same time:** the credential dialog sent a basic sign-in as `user:password` while the database needs JSON `{username, password}` (`credentialSecret` in `lib/integrations.js`). Tests: `rest-api.adapter.spec.ts` (OAuth), `safe-http.spec.ts` (form POST), `supabase/tests/integration_oauth_client.test.sql`, `api/test/integration-oauth.e2e-spec.ts` (fake sign-in server + fake API on two hosts), `Integrations.test.jsx`, `RestConnector.test.jsx`. Not built: private-key JWT client assertions, authorisation-code (user sign-in) flows.
- **Email notifications** (`docs/email-oauth-plan.md` EM1–EM3, decisions E1–E4; migration `20260131200000_email_notifications.sql` (written as `20260125100000_…`, renamed like the OAuth one), applied live 2026-11-11 (the 164 existing notices were marked as considered, so none is emailed); **it replaced three functions the competence notice migration had changed, so `20260201100000_notifications_merge.sql` puts both together** (competence `own_record` + the assessment branch, and email; "You were assessed" emailed by default) — applied live 2026-11-11 (live checks: both branches present, guardrails 0 violations). Two parallel lines of work both replacing `emit_notification` / the preference functions is what caused this: **before replacing a shared function, check the latest migration that defines it, including ones pulled since**): **every in-app notification can also be emailed, per person.** `notification_preferences.email` (null = the event's default; `enabled` may now be null too, a row lives while either choice is overridden), `notification_event_types.email_default` (on only for notices addressed to one person — quality alert addressed to you / withdrawn, 8D action assigned to you — and import alerts); `app.notification_email_wanted` (= shown in the app AND email chosen), `app.set_notification_email`, `list_notification_preferences` returns `email`, `email_default`, `email_overridden`. **`email_outbox`** (one row per notification and person, `UNIQUE (notification_id, user_id)`, never twice; RLS: a person reads their own; **no table writes**) written only by API-login functions: `app.system_queue_notification_emails(limit)` (for each notice not yet considered — `notifications.email_queued_at` — every active member with a **confirmed** address who would see it in the app, evaluated **as that person** with `aal2` claims, and wants it by email; a notice over a day old is marked, never mailed late; notices from before the migration were marked), `system_lease_emails(limit, lease seconds)` (returns what the API needs to render: organisation, recipient name, event, title, body, details), `system_finish_email(id, sent|unsent|failed|refused, …)` (failed backs off 1, 4, 16, 64 minutes, final after five; refused and unsent are final). **`integration_alerts`** (audited; one per finished non-preview import that failed or left rejected/failed rows, values to review or records that disappeared; written by the deferred constraint trigger `integration_sync_runs_alert` at commit) → event `integration_alerts.created` (`integrations.read`, on by default for `integrations.run`, emailed by default; **its notice has no actor**, so whoever started the import is told too; `emit_notification` now copies `connection_id`, `run_id`, `kind` into details). `app.platform_email_outbox(hours)` (operators: counts, waiting, last sent, providers, by event, problems with errors — never subjects, bodies or addresses). **API** `api/src/email/`: `MailTransport` chosen by env **`MAIL_PROVIDER`** (`none` default = **outbox only**, marked not sent; `resend` = Resend HTTPS API with `RESEND_API_KEY` + `MAIL_FROM`, idempotency key = outbox id, 429/5xx retried, other refusals final). Supabase's mail service only sends its own auth templates, so it cannot carry these. `email-content.ts` (`renderNotificationEmail`: subject = title — body on one line ≤ 180, text + simple HTML with everything escaped, link via `notificationPath` = a mirror of `src/lib/notifications.js` built only from UUIDs, `APP_BASE_URL`), `EmailService.drain()` (fan-out then lease/send/finish within a time budget) and **`kick()` after every successful non-GET request** (`EmailKickInterceptor`, one drain at a time per process, registered with `waitUntil` from `@vercel/functions` so it finishes after the response on Vercel; switched off under `NODE_ENV=test`, tests set `email.autoKick = true`), cron `GET|POST /internal/cron/email` (daily in `api/vercel.json` — a safety net), `GET /platform/email`, `PUT /notification-preferences` takes `enabled` and/or `email`, preference responses carry `email: { sending }`. **UI:** Settings → Notifications has an envelope toggle beside each switch (only while the event is on in the app; optimistic via `applyPreference` in `useNotifications.jsx`; a Callout says when the server sends no email), import alerts link to their run, the operator panel has an **Email** tab (`platform/EmailPanel.jsx`). Tests: `supabase/tests/email_notifications.test.sql`, `api/src/email/email.spec.ts`, `api/test/email.e2e-spec.ts`, `SettingsNotifications.test.jsx`, `notifications.test.js`, `PlatformPanels.test.jsx`. **Not built:** digests, push, per-organisation senders, bounce handling / suppression, unsubscribe links in the mail, a person's own sent-email list in the UI.
- **Integrations page and file import wizard** (UX1, no migration): Settings → Integrations is a **two-column grid of integration cards** (`SettingsIntegrations.jsx`; list `INTEGRATION_CATALOGUE` in `lib/integrations.js`: CSV or Excel file (available), REST API, Dynamics 365 Business Central, SAP, Sage, MySQL, PostgreSQL, SQL Server, Odoo, Google Sheets, Xero, QuickBooks (**Coming soon**)), logos from `integrations/ProviderLogo.jsx` + `brandMarks.js` (Simple Icons paths, CC0, bundled; Microsoft's four squares drawn; your Excel, REST API and SQL Server pictures in `src/assets/integrations/`), then Recent imports (`useRecentIntegrationRuns`) and every connection. **CSV is a one-time wizard** at `/Settings/Integrations/csv?connection&entity&file&step` (`CsvImportWizard.jsx`; `wizardPath()` builds the address): (1) choose the kind of record (tiles; ones without an importer or write permission disabled; "Mapping saved" badge); the organisation's file connection ("File imports", code from `freeSystemCode`) is made on first use; (2) how to read the file (headings switch, character set, separator — sent per upload as `options` on `POST /integrations/connections/{id}/files`, over the connection's settings; "Read again with these settings"), drop zone or an earlier file, first rows, and `ImportSettingsCard` (for files only write mode and matching); (3) **`MappingStep.jsx`, the reusable mapping editor**: key column, one row per Fortress field — field → column combo = example from the file (`exampleValue`/`readDate` mirror the server's reading; arrows step through the sample rows), required fields without a source in red with a banner, each row expands for How / fixed value / joined columns / when empty / translation / date format / decimal mark / Match by; a first mapping takes Code from the key column. A sticky footer says what is missing and offers **Preview** and **Import N rows**: the mapping is saved first only when it changed (`mappingChanged`; a reason dialog from version 2), then the run starts and the run page opens, where a file preview offers **Import these records** and **Change the mapping**. The old `/Settings/Integrations/:id/mapping` redirects to step 3. `RHFComboBox`/`ComboField` take `ariaLabel` for a combo without a visible label.
- **Import templates and steps** (`docs/import-templates-plan.md`, decisions T1–T5; slices UI2a–UI2d, **all done**, migration `20260119100000_integration_templates.sql`, applied live 2026-11-10 (the 3 existing mappings became 2 templates with 3 versions); pushing the API before it breaks the runs endpoints, which join `template_version_id`): `integration_templates` (organisation-wide, one kind of record, name unique per organisation and kind ignoring case, description, `current_version`, archived with a reason, never deleted; audited; RLS `integrations.read`; SELECT only) and `integration_template_versions` (**frozen by trigger**, even for SQL: source settings, expected columns, key column, field rows (1–100), import settings `{writeMode, matchExisting}` (NCs always add only, never matched), reason, `carried_from_mapping_id`); `integration_sync_runs.template_version_id`; `organisation_settings.advanced_imports` (default off, for the formula editor and template manager in UI2c). **No table writes:** `app.create_integration_template(org, entity, name, description, body)`, `save_integration_template_version(id, body, reason)` (reason ≥ 3 characters, unchanged refused, not while archived), `update_integration_template` (name, description), `archive_ / restore_integration_template`, `duplicate_integration_template(id, name?)` ("Copy of …"), `restore_integration_template_version(id, version, reason)` (saves N's content as a new version), all `integrations.manage` (so a Controller seat, T4); `QM022` → `409 integration_rule`, other organisation `P0002` → 404. `app.request_integration_run(conn, entity, kind, file, template)` pins the template's **current version** and merges its settings over the connection's; `app.system_lease_integration_run` hands that version to the engine as the mapping (so the engine is unchanged). `app.carry_integration_mappings_to_templates()` (run by the migration, idempotent) made every connection's mappings into a template "<connection> — <kind>" with the versions in order and the current import settings on the live version; `integration_mappings` stays for old runs and the old mapping endpoints (the wizard moves to templates in UI2c). **Steps** (`api/src/integrations/core/steps.ts`, schema `integration-steps.schemas.ts`): a field row takes `steps[]` (≤ 30) from a closed set — text `trim upper lower title collapse_spaces replace remove left right split prefix suffix`, number `number round multiply divide convert` (fixed units mm/cm/m/in/ft, g/kg/lb/oz, c/f; T5), date `date` (iso/dmy/mdy/excel or a pattern of yyyy yy MM M dd d) `add_days`, logic `map if coalesce join default`; applied after the old transforms, stop at the first error (the row is rejected with "<Field>: <message>"), checked before saving by `stepProblems`; `mapping-dates.ts` holds the date reading. The old transforms give the same values as their steps (unit-tested). API `api/src/integrations/integration-templates.*`: `GET /integrations/templates?entityType&archived=exclude|include|only`, `POST`, `GET|PATCH /integrations/templates/{id}`, `GET|POST …/{id}/versions`, `GET …/{id}/versions/{n}`, `POST …/{id}/duplicate|archive|restore|restore-version`; content checked by `mappingProblems` against the catalogue (incl. custom fields) and the expected columns; templates carry `runCount`, `lastUsedAt`, detail `current` and `canManage`, versions `runCount`. `POST /integrations/connections/{id}/runs` takes `templateId`; runs carry `template {id, name, version}`. Tests: `supabase/tests/integration_templates.test.sql` (fixture `fixtures/20260118100000_custom_field_imports.sql` for the carry-over), `core/steps.spec.ts`, `api/test/integration-templates.e2e-spec.ts` (+ both sweeps). **UI2b done (formulas and the preview, no migration):** `api/src/integrations/core/formula/` — `parser.ts` (hand-written lexer + recursive-descent parser: `[Column]` with `]]` for `]`, `"text"` with `""`, numbers with `.`, `TRUE`/`FALSE`, `& + - * / = <> < <= > >= AND OR NOT`, brackets; ≤ 2,000 characters, nesting ≤ 32; `FormulaSyntaxError` with a 1-based position and what was expected), `evaluate.ts` (tree-walker over values empty / text / number / true-false / date, ≤ 20,000 steps and ≤ 10,000-character text per row, arguments evaluated lazily so `IF(TRUE, x, 1/0)` works; `FUNCTIONS` = CONCAT LEFT RIGHT MID LEN UPPER LOWER PROPER TRIM SUBSTITUTE FIND SPLIT · IF IFS IFEMPTY COALESCE ISEMPTY IN SWITCH · NUMBER ROUND ABS MIN MAX CONVERT · DATE TODAY ADDDAYS YEAR MONTH DAY, reusing the step implementations for title case, rounding, units and date reading so steps and formulas agree; empty values pass through arithmetic as empty; text compares ignoring case), `index.ts` (`checkFormula(src, columns?)` = syntax, unknown function with "did you mean", arity "IF needs 2 or 3 values, got 4", unknown column "Column [Stauts] is not in the file — did you mean [Status]?"; `runFormula` with a parse cache; `FUNCTION_LIST`). No variables, loops, user functions, network, database or file access. A field row takes `formula` (≤ 2,000) **instead of** its column and original transforms; the steps still follow; a formula that fails rejects the row with "<Field>: <message>"; `mappingProblems` checks it (and its columns against the file); `fieldColumns(m)` lists every column a row reads. **Rows are read with `cell(record, column)` (own properties only), so a column named `constructor` or `__proto__` can never read inherited properties** (found by the hostile-input tests). API: `POST /integrations/templates/preview {entityType, fileId, keyField, fields, rows?}` (`integrations.run`; nothing written; per row and field the value taken, the value after each step, the imported value and the error, plus the row's errors; `columnCheck` = missing columns (blocking for the key column and required fields) and unused ones; `problems` = the save check; links are shown as written), `POST /integrations/formulas/check {formula, columns?, sample?}` (`integrations.read`; first problem with position, columns, functions, sample result), `GET /integrations/formulas/functions`. Tests `core/formula/formula.spec.ts` (incl. hostile input), `api/test/integration-templates.e2e-spec.ts` (formulas and the preview). **UI2c done (screens, no migration):** `organisation_settings.advanced_imports` is now in the organisation API (`settings.advancedImports`, `PATCH /organisations/current`); Settings → Integrations has an **Advanced imports** card (switch for `organisation.manage`, optimistic; **Manage templates** link when on). **The file import wizard works on templates:** step 3 has a **Template** card (`Start from` = the organisation's templates for the kind of record, defaulting to the one used most recently (`defaultTemplate`), or "Start a new mapping"; URL `?template=` (`new` for none); status "Version N, unchanged" / "Changed from version N"; **Save as template / Save a new version** for `integrations.manage`), a **What importing may do** card (write mode and matching, kept **on the template**; the connection-level `ImportSettingsCard` is gone) and the mapping editor. Preview / Import run with `templateId`: an unchanged template runs as it is; a changed one (or none) opens `SaveTemplateDialog` (next version of the chosen template with a reason, or a new template with a name — default "<Kind> from <file name>"), saves `{keyField, fields, settings, sourceSettings (the step-2 read options), expectedColumns (the file's headings)}`, then runs. People with `integrations.run` but not `.manage` may change the mapping to see the preview, but importing waits until someone who manages integrations saves it (footer says so, buttons disabled). **T3: the browser no longer reads values** — `exampleValue` / `readDate` are gone; `useTemplatePreview(body)` (debounced 300 ms, previous answer kept) asks `POST /integrations/templates/preview` and the editor shows its per-field value, the value after each step, row errors (arrows step through the preview rows) and the **column check** (missing columns, red when a key or required field needs them and the import is then blocked; unused columns in blue). `MappingStep` rows open to: **Use a formula instead of a column** (only with advanced imports; `FormulaEditor.jsx`: monospace box, suggestions for functions while typing a name and for columns after `[` (`tokenAt`/`suggestionsFor`), the server's message (`useFormulaCheck`, `POST /integrations/formulas/check` with the file's columns and the example row) and the example result; function list from `useFormulaFunctions`), the original transforms otherwise, and the **steps** (`StepEditor.jsx`: add from a combo of every step kind, move up/down, remove, each step's settings by type — text, number, select, switch, date format or pattern, translate lines, one-per-line list, columns, join pieces — the value after each step for the example row, and what is missing (`stepProblem`), which also blocks importing). Rows carry `steps`, `formula`, `useFormula` (`mappingToRows` / `rowsToFields`, steps cleaned by `cleanSteps`); `rowHasSource` counts a formula and a join / coalesce / default step. Helpers `src/lib/importTemplates.js` (`STEP_KINDS` metadata, `newStep`, `describeStep`, `stepProblem`, `cleanSteps`, `templateSettings`, `templateChanged`, `defaultTemplate`, `versionChanges`, formula suggestions); hooks in `useIntegrations.js` (`useImportTemplates`, `useImportTemplate`, `useTemplateVersions`, `useTemplateVersion`, `useTemplateMutation`, `useTemplatePreview`, `useFormulaFunctions`, `useFormulaCheck`). **Template manager** (`integrations/TemplatesPage.jsx`, routes `/Settings/Integrations/Templates` and `/Settings/Integrations/Templates/:templateId`; shown when advanced imports are on): list by kind (combo) with In use / Archived tabs, version, imports, last used; "…" Rename / Duplicate (name, opens the copy) / Archive (reason) / Bring back; a template's page shows its current version (key column, write mode, each field's column / formula and steps), the versions with reason, who, when, field and import counts, and a dialog per version with **what changed** from the one before (`versionChanges`) and **Bring this version back** (reason). The run page names the template and version, and "Import these records" / "Change the mapping" reuse the run's template; the connection page's Run dialog picks a template for a file connection. Tests `ImportTemplates.test.jsx`, `IntegrationFiles.test.jsx` (wizard rewritten for templates and the server preview), `Integrations.test.jsx`, `refresh.test.js`. **UI2d done (exit check, `docs/import-templates-exit-report.md`):** the browser journey imports machines from a CSV with a step and a formula, saves the template and opens it in the manager (the journey's fake Supabase keeps uploads in memory and the API's file store reads them back, `scripts/journey/stack.cjs`); the whole journey passes again (it had fallen behind the quality-alert `actions` and the disposition Lot combo). **Rows shown while they save (optimistic, id `pending-…`) offer no actions** — NC dispositions and containment actions say "Saving…", saved filters still saving cannot be renamed or deleted — because a quick click sent the placeholder id. UI2 is complete.
- **Custom fields** (`docs/custom-fields-plan.md`, decisions CF-D1–D6; slices CF1–CF4; **all done**, migration `20260115100000_custom_fields.sql`, applied live 2026-11-10): organisation-defined fields on 13 kinds of record (`app.custom_field_entities()`: part, part_family, machine, gauge_type, gauge, supplier, customer, employee, nonconformance, capa, inspection, production_order, lot). Permission **`custom_fields.manage`** (owner, admin, quality manager). `custom_field_definitions` (key `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,39}$` and type fixed once created; label unique ignoring case among active; types text ≤ 500 / long_text ≤ 5000 / number (decimals 0–6, unit, min, max) / date / boolean / choice / multi_choice (options `{key, label, active}`, ≤ 100, switched off never removed) / person (active member); `required_at` never / save / a stage of the record (`app.custom_field_stages`); at most **20 active per kind**; retired with a reason, reactivated within the limit; read by every member) and `custom_field_values` (**one row per record, primary key = the record id**, `values` jsonb keyed by field key; audited with the record id as entity id; read under the record's own read permission, `app.custom_values_readable`). **No table writes:** `app.save_custom_field(org, entity, jsonb, id?)`, `retire_custom_field(id, reason)`, `reactivate_custom_field`, `reorder_custom_fields(org, entity, ids[])` (every field once), `app.set_custom_values(entity, record, values)` (merge; null / "" / [] clears; the record's own edit permission (`app.custom_values_writable`); a locked record — closed/cancelled/deleted NC, closed/cancelled 8D, finished inspection, closed/cancelled order, closed lot, deleted gauge — accepts only a save that changes nothing; retired fields and switched-off options / departed people can only be kept or cleared; a field required on save is never cleared) and `app.write_custom_values` (internal, for importers in CF4). **Required checks:** deferred constraint trigger `custom_values_required` AFTER INSERT on the 13 tables (save fields, plus stage fields when inserted at or past the stage — e.g. an NC inserted as open), and `custom_values_stage` BEFORE UPDATE of `stage`/`status` on NCs, 8Ds, inspections, orders, lots (moving from before a required stage to it or later; cancelled / abandoned / on hold never block; new orders and lots are always inserted planned / open). Errors `QM023` (→ `409 custom_field_rule` in CF2). Tests `supabase/tests/custom_fields.test.sql` (mutants caught); **tests that create one of the 13 records in an organisation with a required custom field must give its values in the same transaction.** **CF2 done (API, no further migration; `app.get_custom_values(entity, record)` is in its own migration `20260116100000_custom_values_read.sql`, applied live 2026-11-10):** `api/src/custom-fields/` — `GET /custom-fields?entityType&includeRetired` (every member: `{ data, limit: 20, entities: [{ entityType, label, stages, activeCount }] }`), `POST /custom-fields`, `PATCH /custom-fields/{id}` (key / type never), `POST …/{id}/retire {reason}|reactivate`, `PUT /custom-fields/order {entityType, ids}` (all `custom_fields.manage`); `GET /custom-values?entityType&ids=a,b` (batch, `{ entityType, data: {id: values} }`), `GET /custom-values/{id}?entityType` (404 when the record cannot be read), `PUT /custom-values/{id} {entityType, values}` (merge; 403 without the record's edit permission). Errors `QM023` → `409 custom_field_rule`. **Records:** the 13 kinds' create bodies take `customValues` (written in the same transaction, so a field required on save is satisfied and a bad value refuses the whole create): `POST /parts`, `/part-families`, `/machines`, `/gauge-types`, `/gauges`, `/suppliers`, `/supplier-quality/suppliers`, `/customers`, `/employees`, `/ncrs`, `/capas`, `/inspections`, `/jobs`, `/production-orders` (+ `/inline`), `/lots`, plus the routes that create NCs or inspections (`/inspections/{id}/raise-ncr`, `/audit-findings/{id}/create-ncr`, `/goods-receipt-lines/{id}/raise-ncr` and `/start-inspection`, `/calibrations/{id}/raise-ncr`; written only when a new record was made). Detail responses and list rows carry `customValues` (helpers `api/src/custom-fields/custom-values.ts`: `writeCustomValues`, `attachCustomValues`, `customValuesOf`). **Register filters** `cf_<field key>=` on every one of those lists (`pickCustomFilters` + `customFilterSql`): text contains, choice / person one of a comma list, several choices any of, yes / no `true|false`, number / date equal or `from..to`, `-` = no value; an unknown key or bad value is 400. Edits go through `PUT /custom-values/{id}`, not the records' PATCH bodies. Not yet: inline orders by inspectors when a field is required on save (they cannot write production values). Tests `api/test/custom-fields.e2e-spec.ts` (+ tenancy sweep). **CF3 done (screens; migration `20260117100000_custom_field_people.sql`, applied live 2026-11-10):** `app.custom_field_people(org)` (any member; active members' user id + name, because RLS on memberships shows people without `members.read` only themselves) → `GET /custom-fields/people`. Frontend: hooks `src/hooks/useCustomFields.js` (`useCustomFields(entity, { includeRetired })`, `useActiveCustomFields`, `useCustomFieldMutation` create/update/retire/reactivate/reorder (reorder optimistic), `useSetCustomValues(entity, id)` — optimistic: patches `customValues` of every cached object with that id, rolls back on error — and `useCustomFieldPeople`), helpers `src/lib/customFields.js` (types, `requiredChoices`, `fieldBody`/`fieldToForm`/`fieldProblems`, `formatCustomValue`, `withCustomValues`). **Settings → Data Management → Custom fields** (`custom-fields/CustomFieldsSettings.jsx`; tab shown to `custom_fields.manage` only): kind-of-record combo with counts, "N of 20 in use", list with move up/down, type, section, required badge, "…" Edit / Retire (reason), Retired fields folded with Bring back; field dialog (label, type fixed once created, section, required never/save/before a stage, help text, number decimals/unit/limits, options editor: saved options switch off, new ones removable). **Records:** `custom-fields/CustomFieldsCard.jsx` default export = the **More details** card (`entityType`, `record`, `editable`): sections, one control per type (text/long text/number save on leave, yes/no switch, date picker, choice and person combos, several choices as toggle chips), switched-off options / departed people kept but never offered, retired fields only when holding a value, read-only words otherwise, toast on refusal; named `CustomFieldInputs` for **create forms** (only fields required on save, values in the form's state, sent as `customValues`). On every record page / edit dialog (parts, part families, machines and customers/suppliers edit dialogs, employees dialog, supplier profile Overview, gauge types (Settings), gauges, production orders, lots dialog, NCs, 8D D0, inspections, jobs) and every create form incl. raise NC from an inspection / audit finding / receipt line; **not** the one-click calibration Raise NC and the NC page's Start a new 8D (no dialog: a field required on save on NCs / 8Ds refuses those). Edit dialogs take the current row from the list (not the copy taken on open) so the card shows what was just saved. **Registers:** `custom-fields/customColumns.jsx` (`useCustomColumns(entity)` → columns `cf_<key>`, hidden at first, not sortable; `useCustomFilterChips`, `useCustomFilterParams`, `describeCustomFilter`, `customCsvValues`) and `CustomFieldFilters.jsx` (filter controls per type writing `cf_<key>` to the address straight away, `CustomFilterChips`): NC, employees, suppliers (supplier quality), gauges, 8D and inspection registers (columns, filter drawer section, chips, CSV), production orders and lots (`RegisterPage entityType`: columns, "More filters", chips, CSV) and the master-data lists (`DataList entityType`: columns only). Saved filters do not keep custom field filters yet. Tests `CustomFields.test.jsx`, `CustomFieldsCard.test.jsx`, `CustomFieldFilters.test.jsx`, `refresh.test.js`, `SettingsDataManagement.test.jsx`. **CF4 done (imports; migration `20260118100000_custom_field_imports.sql`, applied live 2026-11-10):** `app.custom_field_catalogue(org, entity)` (API login only) → `CatalogueService.get(entity, organisationId)` / `all(organisationId)` add the organisation's active custom fields as catalogue fields `cf_<key>` (`custom: true`; required = required on save; text 500 / long text 5000 / number with min-max / date / yes-no / choice = enum of its active options / several choices = text "A, B" / person = text, email or full name; `customField()` in `core/catalogue.service.ts`), read each time (they change without a deployment); `GET /integrations/entities`, mapping validation and the sync engine pass the organisation; fields carry `custom`. `app.system_import_record` (replaced) calls `app.import_custom_values(org, entity, record, data)` after writing the record: takes the `cf_` keys, turns a choice's key or label into the key, splits several choices on commas / semicolons, finds a person by email address (or a unique full name) among active members, **skips empty values (an import never clears a custom value)**, writes through `app.write_custom_values` (every value checked, `QM023` fails that row only) and checks required-on-save for that record at once (`SET CONSTRAINTS public.custom_values_required IMMEDIATE`). The content hash includes the custom values, so a changed value updates and an unchanged file writes nothing. Mapping editor shows "custom field" in the field's hint. Tests `api/test/custom-field-imports.e2e-spec.ts`. The import catalogue covers only the eight importable kinds (no part families, 8Ds, inspections, orders, lots).
- **Gauge type codes** (migration `20260113100000_gauge_type_codes.sql`, applied live 2026-11-10; pushing the API before it breaks the gauge type endpoints, which select `code`): `gauge_types.code` = what identifies a gauge type in the app and in imports instead of its uuid or name — required, trimmed, ≤ 40 characters with no spaces at either end (the machine-code rule), **unique per organisation ignoring case** (`gauge_types_org_code_key`, `409` "A gauge type with this code already exists"), editable by `calibration.manage` (column UPDATE grant), audited. Existing types got a code made from their name by `app.gauge_type_code_from_name` (capitals, other characters → "-", ≤ 36, "-2", "-3" … on a clash, `TYPE` when nothing is left; backfill with row triggers off), and trigger `gauge_types_default_code` gives an insert without a code one the same way (older SQL, seeds, tests). API: types carry `code`, `POST /gauge-types` requires it, `PATCH` takes it, the gauge register search matches it. UI: Settings → Data Management → Calibration asks for a **Code** (suggested from the name until typed, `gaugeTypeCodeFromName` in `lib/calibration.js`) and shows it beside each type; type pickers show it as the hint. Imports: the gauge type catalogue has a required **Code** field (key hint "The gauge type code"), the importer writes it, and a gauge's **Gauge type** link is matched by code, then by name for files from before codes (`app.integration_find_by_code`, `integration_record_code`). Tests: `supabase/tests/gauge_type_codes.test.sql` (fixture `fixtures/20260112100000_integration_imports.sql` exercises the backfill), `calibration.e2e-spec.ts`, `Calibration.test.jsx`.
- **Background worker (WK1)** (`docs/workflow-engine-plan.md`, decisions W1–W10 agreed; migration `20260219100000_worker_foundation.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12; **the worker only runs once the runbook `supabase/runbooks/worker-schedule.sql` has been applied live** — pg_cron + pg_net, the tick address and the cron secret in Vault): **pg_cron calls `POST /internal/worker/tick?source=pg_cron` every minute** (`CronGuard`, `Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>`; one Supabase project, so it calls one deployment: the Dev alias; production now exists at `api.fortress-qms.com`, but the pg_cron job still calls whichever address its runbook was applied with — check it before launch). `WorkerService.tick({ source, budgetMs ≤ 50 s })` (`api/src/worker/`): **one tick at a time** (`app.system_worker_begin` takes a lease in `app.worker_state`;
