Decorated: ncrs, capas, risks, documents, document-revisions, management-reviews (+ minutes), quality-objectives, audits, audit-findings, audit-records, quality-alerts, suppliers (both controllers), customers, parts, part-families, machines, gauges, gauge-types, calibrations, control-plans, control-plan-revisions, employees, production-orders, lots, scars, workflow-rules. **A new editable record gets `@Versioned` on its GET and PATCH.** Tests `api/test/edit-conflicts.e2e-spec.ts`, `versioned.spec.ts`. **EC3 done** (screens; plan §7): `apiFetch` sends any PATCH/PUT whose body has `rowVersion` through `lib/editConflicts.js` (`versionedSave`: one save per record at a time, the person's own saves chained so they never clash with themselves, a conflict on other fields saved again with a "both changes kept" note, a clash on the same field asked in **`ui/EditConflictDialog.jsx`** — Keep yours / Take theirs, rich text Overwrite with mine / Load theirs, Cancel leaves it unsaved — mounted once as `EditConflictHost` in `main.jsx`). **A screen that edits a versioned record sends its version:** `withVersion(record, body)` for forms that reset on refetch, `changesSince(opened, body)` for dialogs that send every field (`const [opened] = useState(record)`), `useEditBase(record, dirty)` for forms that keep their own state, and a base ref + `tookTheirs` for autosaved rich text (`useSaveDraft().reloads`, review minutes). API `@VersionedRows(table, key?)` puts `rowVersion` on register rows (machines, part families, employees, customers/suppliers, production orders, lots, gauge types, a plan's revisions) so row dialogs have one; **a new register whose rows open an edit dialog gets it too.** Not covered: custom fields card, review inputs, job details, child lines (actions, contacts, containment, dispositions). **EC4 done** (exit report `docs/edit-conflicts-exit-report.md`: live guardrails 0 violations, advisors nothing new, **`npm run journey` has an edit-conflicts step** (`editConflicts()`: the owner and Colin on one NC — different fields merged with the note, the same field asked and kept); the browser found that sending the version as an `ETag` made browsers show stale records after child rows changed, fixed). The edit conflicts plan is complete. **Then (plan §8, migration `20260325100000_edit_conflicts_custom_values.sql`, applied live 2026-11-17):** the More details card and action lines are checked too — `custom_field_values` is keyed by `record_id` (`@Versioned(table, keyColumn)`) and conflicts are reported per custom field as `values.<key>` (different custom fields merged, the same one asked, labelled from the definitions on screen); `useSetCustomValues` reads the values' version from `GET /custom-values/:id` (now without `entityType`); 8D, risk, review and NC containment actions carry versions through `@VersionedRows(table, 'actions')` (now on any method, so a PATCH answer has fresh versions) and their dialogs send `changesSince`.
- **Public website** (`site/`, `docs/website.md`; agreed 2026-11-17): a separate static Astro project (own Vercel project, root directory `site`, no framework in the browser) for the landing page, about, FAQ, contact, help guides (`site/src/content/docs`) and legal texts (`site/src/content/legal`). It is **not** part of the app: root lint ignores it, Tailwind scans only `src/`, it has its own `package.json` and lockfile. Look: "drawing sheet" (blueprint sections on drafting paper, sheet border, title-block footer, red-pen rings, an interactive measuring demo in the hero). Where it sends people comes from `site/src/config.ts` / `PUBLIC_APP_URL` (default `https://app.fortress-qms.com`; navbar **Log in** → `/login`, trial buttons → `/signup`). **Plan: marketing on the bare domain, the app on `app.fortress-qms.com`**; **done 2026-11-17**: the website is the Vercel project `qms-site` (root directory `site`, `fortress-qms.com`, `www` redirects to it, `/login`, `/signup` and `/forgot-password` redirect to the app), the app (`qms-web`) is on `app.fortress-qms.com`, the API (`api.fortress-qms.com`) allows only the app's origin and its `APP_BASE_URL` / `INVITE_REDIRECT_URL` are the app's address, Supabase's Site URL is the app's address, and the app's terms links (`VITE_TERMS_URL`/`VITE_PRIVACY_URL`) go to the website. Steps and what was changed are in `docs/website.md`. Copy rules: say only what the product does today; legal texts are drafts until `PUBLIC_LEGAL_REVIEWED=true`; SAML SSO is mentioned but not live-tested (needs Supabase Pro). The site sets no cookies and has no analytics, which the cookie page states: adding any means updating it.
- **Suspended screen** (migration `20260326100000_suspended_screen.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); pushing the API before it breaks `/me`, which calls `app.my_suspensions()`): a member suspended from their only organisation used to land on "Start your organisation". `app.my_suspensions()` (own memberships only, status `suspended`, active organisations; the person and time come from the latest audit event that made the membership suspended, else `updated_by`/`updated_at`) → `/me` `suspended [{id, name, suspendedAt, suspendedBy}]`. `App.jsx` shows `onboarding/Suspended.jsx` ("You have been suspended from X", "Suspended from X by Y on <date> at <time>", **Check again**, **Sign out**, **Use a different organisation** which falls through to `NoOrganisation` for a waiting invitation) when there is no active organisation and `suspended` is not empty; `suspensionWords` / `suspendedFrom` in `lib/memberOnboarding.js`. Someone who is suspended **while using the app** sees it on the next load of `/me` (a request refused mid-session is not turned into this screen yet). No reason is stored with a suspension. Tests `supabase/tests/suspended_screen.test.sql`, `api/test/suspended.e2e-spec.ts`, `App.test.jsx`, `lib/memberOnboarding.test.js`.
- **Seats on the Users page, and a function missing live** (migration `20260327100000_restore_seat_assignments.sql`, **applied live 2026-11-17**): the live database did not have `app.list_seat_assignments(uuid)` (created by `20251110100000_subscriptions.sql`), so `GET /subscription/assignments` and `PUT /subscription/assignments/{id}` (which reads the list first) answered 500: Settings → Users & permissions showed no one's seat and could not set one. Seats chosen on the invite form had been reserved all along (`set_invitation_details`); only the list was broken. The migration is `CREATE OR REPLACE` of the original definition. **The page now says "Seats could not be loaded" (and offers no seat controls) when the seat list fails, instead of showing "No seat" for everyone.** A migration written long ago can be missing live: when an endpoint answers 500 with nothing else wrong, compare `pg_proc` with the migrations (names per group hashed, as done here).
- **External portal PT1 — foundation** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md`, decisions X1–X18, PT0 threat model in §8; migration `20260328100000_external_portal_foundation.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); pushing the API before it breaks `/me`, which calls `app.my_portal_access()`): suppliers' and customers' people sign in to a portal of an organisation **without being members** (no `organisation_users` row, role or seat, so `app.is_member` is false and every existing RLS rule and function already refuses them; nothing existing was widened). Permissions `external_access.read` / `.manage` (owner, admin, quality manager; **unpriced**, in `app.unpriced_permissions()`). `organisation_settings.portal_enabled` (default off), `portal_ranking_enabled` (used later), `portal_require_mfa` (default on) — changed only by `app.set_portal_settings`. `portal_parties` (one per supplier or customer: `enabled`, `tiles`, `contact_name` shown to them, reserved nullable `linked_organisation_id` for X13, unused; `app.set_portal_party`) and `external_access` (one row per person per party: invited → active → suspended | removed, role admin | member; unique per party by address and by account; never deleted; **functions only**, audited, read with `external_access.read` or by the person's own row). Functions: `app.invite_external_user(party, email, role, message)` (staff, or an active **party admin** inviting ordinary members only; refuses an address that belongs to one of the organisation's own people, a party or portal that is off, duplicates; 7-day expiry), `resend_external_invitation` (not within 60 s, ≤ 10), `remove_external_access(id, reason)` (a party admin cannot remove themselves or another admin), `set_external_access_suspended`, `my_portal_invitations()` (matched by the **confirmed** address), `accept_portal_invitation`, `my_portal_access()`, `portal_my_access(org)` (live only while organisation, portal and party are all on), `portal_mfa_blocked`, `portal_max_session_hours`, `portal_record_sign_in` (≤ once an hour), `list_external_access(org, party?)`. Errors `QM032` → `409 portal_rule`. `app.user_reference_columns()` classifies `external_access.user_id` as history. **API** `api/src/portal/`: **`PortalGuard` + `@PortalScoped({ admin? })`** (X-Organisation-Id; no live access → 404; then `403 mfa_required` per the portal's own setting and `401 reauthentication_required` per the organisation's session age; sets `req.portal` / `ctx.portal`) — **the only place the network allow-list, "company sign-in required" and seat rules are not applied, and only for external people** (tests prove a member outside the allow-list is still blocked and an external person outside it is not). Staff `GET /external-access`, `PUT …/settings|parties`, `GET|POST …/people`, `POST …/people/{id}/resend|remove|suspend|restore` (404 for another organisation's ids); the person's `GET /portal/me`, `GET /portal/invitations`, `POST /portal/invitations/{id}/accept` (unscoped, on the sweep allowlist), `GET /portal/home`, and a party admin's `GET|POST /portal/people`, `POST /portal/people/{id}/resend|remove` (the party comes from the person's access row, never the request). `/me` gains `portal` and `portalInvitations`. The invitation email reuses `InvitationMailer` (`InviteEmailDetails.portalParty` switches the copy to "invited you to the … portal"). **UI:** Settings → **External access** (`Settings/SettingsExternalAccess.jsx`: portal switch, two-factor for external people, every supplier and customer with an optimistic on/off, a People dialog (invite as admin first, resend, suspend, remove with a reason, the contact name they see) and an access report with last sign-in), and the portal at **`/portal`** (`portal/PortalApp.jsx`, outside `<App />`: own layout with no staff menu, organisation chooser when the person has several, pending invitations to accept, the party's home with who to contact and "nothing shared yet", an admin's `PortalPeople.jsx`); `App.jsx` sends someone with portal access and no organisation of their own to `/portal`, and `AcceptInvite` hands portal-only invitations over. Hooks `useExternalAccess|useExternalPeople|useExternalAccessMutation` (optimistic switches) and `usePortalAccess|usePortalInvitations|usePortalHome|usePortalPeople|usePortalMutation`, helpers `lib/portal.js`. Tests `supabase/tests/external_portal_foundation.test.sql` (+ sweep seed), `api/test/external-portal.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep: three unscoped routes, `{id}` sample bodies, portal routes left out of the owner's list check), `SettingsExternalAccess.test.jsx`, `portal/Portal.test.jsx`, `lib/portal.test.js`, `refresh.test.js`. **Not built (PT2+):** sharing records, comments, viewed tracking, responses, tasks, the scorecard, the customer side, notices and email to external people other than the invitation, a browser journey step.
- **External portal PT2 — sharing and conversation** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6, decisions X2, X9, X11, X12, X15; migration `20260329100000_portal_sharing.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); needs PT1's migration first; pushing the API before it breaks the NC and 8D pages' share card routes only): staff share an **NC or an 8D** with a party; the party sees the curated view and talks to staff on the record. Permission `external_access.share` (owner, admin, quality manager, **quality engineer**; unpriced). `portal_shares` (one live share per party and record; an NC only with the supplier or customer **it names**, an 8D only with a party named by one of its **live linked NCs**; a draft or cancelled NC and a cancelled 8D cannot be shared; withdrawn with a reason, never deleted, and withdrawing hides it from the party at once), `portal_comments` (both sides in one thread; **a comment is withdrawn by its author within ten minutes, otherwise permanent**; a withdrawn one keeps its place and loses its words; staff show to the party as **the organisation, never by name** (PT0 T13); to staff an external comment shows "Sam (Acme Castings)") and `portal_views` (first / last view per person: the staff card says "Seen by N people, last …"). **No table writes** — functions only: `app.share_with_portal(kind, record, party)`, `withdraw_portal_share`, `portal_eligible_parties`, `list_portal_shares(kind, record)`, `add_portal_comment` (staff or external decided by the caller; an external person gets 404 for a withdrawn share), `withdraw_portal_comment`, `portal_share_comments`, `portal_share_recipients` (the party's active people with a confirmed address, for email), and for the party `portal_list_shares(org)`, `portal_mark_viewed`, **`portal_get_ncr(share)` / `portal_get_capa(share)` = the curated external views: one function each returning NAMED fields only** (NC: reference, title, description, requirement, actual result, severity, stage, detected date, quantities, part, revision label, lot, serial, purchase order, customer reference, containment actions (cancelled left out; result note only when done), **approved** dispositions only, contact; 8D: problem and 5W2H, containment, **verified** root causes, corrective and preventive actions, latest verification (effective, method, date), lessons learned, closed date, team **size** only). **A column added to a table adds nothing to the portal; the SQL test and `portal-sharing.e2e-spec.ts` list the exact keys and fail on a change.** Costs, internal notes, people, other parties, root-cause workings and proposed dispositions are never returned. `app.user_reference_columns()` classifies the new user columns as history. API (`api/src/portal/`): staff `GET|POST /external-access/shares` (`?kind=ncr|capa&recordId`; the answer carries `eligible` parties; another organisation's record is 404), `POST …/shares/{id}/withdraw {reason}`, `GET|POST …/shares/{id}/comments`, `POST /external-access/comments/{id}/withdraw`; portal (`PortalScoped`) `GET /portal/shares`, `GET /portal/shares/{id}` (the record, the conversation, and **records that the person has seen it**), `POST …/shares/{id}/comments`, `POST /portal/comments/{id}/withdraw`; `GET /portal/home` now returns `attention` (shares not yet opened or with replies since the person last looked) and `shared`. **Email** to the party's people on a share and on a staff comment through `PortalMailer` (the notification mail transport, `MAIL_PROVIDER`; **best effort, straight after the action, never retried, never undoes it**; link `/portal/shared/<id>?org=<org>`). UI: `portal/PortalShareCard.jsx` (**Shared with suppliers and customers** on the NC page, in its own section, and under the step of an 8D; shows nothing when nobody could be given it; share with a named party, Conversation dialog, Stop sharing with a reason, earlier shares folded), shared `portal/CommentThread.jsx`, the portal is now a layout (`PortalApp` + `Outlet`): `PortalHome` (needs attention, shared with you) and **`/portal/shared/:shareId`** (`PortalSharedPage.jsx`: the record in blocks and the conversation); hooks `useShares|useShareComments|useShareMutation` and `usePortalShares|usePortalShare|usePortalShareMutation`. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_sharing.test.sql` (+ sweep seeds), `api/test/portal-sharing.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep: share/comment `{id}` routes, a list query), `portal/PortalShared.test.jsx`, `portal/PortalShareCard.test.jsx`, `portal/Portal.test.jsx`, `refresh.test.js`. **Not built:** documents shared (PT3 with acknowledgements), **files on a shared record** (PT3, with a portal file rule), notices to staff when the party replies (they see replies on the record), email retries, SCARs and tasks (PT3), the scorecard (PT4).
- **External portal PT3a — corrective action requests (SCARs)** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6, decisions X2, X3, X9, X11, X12; migration `20260330100000_portal_scars.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); needs PT2's migration first (applied); pushing the API before it breaks only the new `/portal/scars` routes and the staff SCAR emails): a supplier's people see the SCARs raised against their company and answer them in the portal. **Visibility is automatic (X2): a SCAR that has been issued, for the party whose supplier it names, whatever its status afterwards; a draft is never shown.** **The supplier's side is functions only, each checking the caller's own live portal access to the SCAR's supplier (`app.portal_scar_for_external`; the party comes from the SCAR, never the request; anything else is `P0002` → 404):** `app.portal_list_scars(org)`, `portal_get_scar(scar)` (**named fields only**: the request, the requirement breached, evidence, containment wanted, what is affected, their responses with our review decision and note, requests for more time with the decision note, `canAcknowledge` / `canRespond` / `canRequestExtension`, the contact; no owner, 8D, linked NCs, implementation or verification, no staff names; the SQL test and `portal-scars.e2e-spec.ts` list the exact keys), `portal_acknowledge_scar`, `portal_respond_scar` (root cause and corrective action needed; dated today; names the person and `(Supplier)`; **never closes the SCAR: it waits for our review**, a waiting extension request is settled as for staff), `portal_request_scar_extension` (**the date does not move**; staff decide with `supplier_quality.approve` as ever), `portal_withdraw_scar_extension` (only the person who asked, while pending; answers with the SCAR id). They write the **same rows** the staff functions write (`supplier_scar_responses`, `supplier_scar_extensions`, status via `app.scar_set_status` and its history), so the SCAR page, internal review and extension decisions work unchanged. **Staff are told:** `portal_activity` (audited, frozen, functions only via `app.portal_note_activity`; RLS `external_access.share`; event `portal_activity.created`, group "External portal", on by default for `external_access.share`, not emailed) is written when a party acknowledges, responds, asks for or takes back more time, **or comments on a shared NC / 8D** (`app.add_portal_comment` replaced); the general notification path carries it (no change to `emit_notification`), links via `notificationLink` / `notificationPath` (`portal_activity` → the SCAR, 8D or NC). **Email to the supplier's people** (best effort, `PortalMailer`, link `/portal/scars/<id>?org=<org>`; `app.portal_scar_recipients`, `supplier_quality.manage`): when staff **issue** a SCAR, **review** a response (accepted / revision asked, with the note) and **decide** an extension (`SupplierScarService.tellSupplier`; never undoes the step). API (`api/src/portal/`, all `@PortalScoped`): `GET /portal/scars`, `GET /portal/scars/{id}`, `POST …/acknowledge|respond|extensions`, `POST /portal/scar-extensions/{id}/withdraw` (every command answers `{ id, scar }`); `GET /portal/home` also returns `scarsAwaiting` and `scars`. UI: `portal/PortalScarPage.jsx` at `/portal/scars/:scarId` (what we ask, what is affected, responses with our review, requests for more time with Take back, **Acknowledge**, **Respond** / **Send a revised response**, **Ask for more time**; buttons follow `can…`), home sections "Waiting for your response" and "Corrective action requests" (`PortalApp.jsx`), hooks `usePortalScars|usePortalScar|usePortalScarMutation` (wait for the server's answer, which replaces the cached SCAR), words in `lib/portal.js` (`scarStatus`, `dueWords`, `scarPath`). Tests `supabase/tests/portal_scars.test.sql` (+ sweep seed), `api/test/portal-scars.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep: five `{id}` routes in both lists), `portal/PortalScar.test.jsx`, `Portal.test.jsx`, `lib/portal.test.js`, `notifications.test.js`, `email.spec.ts`. **Not built (PT3b/PT3c):** files on a response (needs the portal file rule), tasks assigned to them, acknowledging documents and quality alerts, the SCAR implementation / verification being shown or answered, a portal journey step; not browser-checked.
- **External portal PT3b — evidence files on a SCAR response** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6; migration `20260331100000_portal_scar_files.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); needs PT3a's migration first; pushing the API before it breaks only the new portal file routes and the staff SCAR page, whose detail now selects `supplier_scar_files`): the supplier adds evidence while a response is awaited. `supplier_scar_files` (organisation, SCAR, optional `response_id`, path, name, type, size ≤ 25 MB, who uploaded, removed marks; **functions only, audited, never deleted**; staff read with `supplier_quality.read`): a file is **unsent** (`response_id` empty: the supplier may take it back, at most **ten** wait) until `app.portal_respond_scar` (replaced) attaches every unsent file of that SCAR to the response, and from then on it is frozen (trigger, even for SQL). Files live in the private **`scar-files`** bucket at `<org>/scar/<scar id>/<uuid>-<name>` (`app.scar_file_allowed`: staff read, **nobody writes directly**; `app.storage_file_allowed` and the storage policies know the bucket; `scar-files` is in `ORGANISATION_BUCKETS`, so staff open files through the ordinary `/files/download-url`). **Portal rule for uploads:** `app.portal_scar_upload_allowed(scar, path)` (their own live portal access to that SCAR, a response awaited, the SCAR's own folder, under ten unsent) is checked by the API before it signs with the service key; **the API chooses the path** (`PortalService.scarUploadLink`: a safe file name, never the browser's), then `app.portal_add_scar_file` records it, `portal_remove_scar_file` takes back an unsent one (answers the SCAR's id), `portal_scar_file_path` gives a file of a SCAR they may see (not removed) for a ten-minute link. `portal_get_scar` (replaced) adds `draftFiles` (unsent, `mine`) and `files` on each response — still named fields only (the SQL test and e2e list the keys). API (`@PortalScoped`): `POST /portal/scars/{id}/files/upload-url {fileName}`, `POST /portal/scars/{id}/files {path, fileName, contentType?, sizeBytes}`, `POST /portal/scar-files/{id}/remove`, `POST /portal/scar-files/{id}/download-url`; the staff SCAR detail's responses carry `files` (with `storagePath`). UI: **Evidence to send with your response** block on `PortalScarPage` (Add a file, Take back, shown while a response is awaited or files wait), files with each response (open on a short link, `openPortalScarFile`), `uploadPortalScarFile` in `src/api/supabaseApi.js` (the one place that touches storage), staff `ScarPage` lists each response's files. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_scars.test.sql` (+ sweep seed, `storage_through_api.test.sql` now counts twelve buckets), `api/test/portal-scar-files.e2e-spec.ts` (fake signer; + API sweep: four `{id}` routes), `PortalScar.test.jsx`, `supabaseApi.test.js`. **Not built:** tasks assigned to suppliers (needs a decision: 8D team contacts are not linked to portal sign-ins and SCARs have no actions), files by staff on a SCAR, files on an extension request or the implementation, a virus scan, a portal journey step; not browser-checked.
- **External portal PT3c — documents and quality alerts shared and acknowledged** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6; migration `20260401100000_portal_acknowledgements.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23) — it contains `ALTER TABLE … DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS` lines, so if the Supabase tool waits on a confirmation prompt, run the statements another way; needs PT3b's migration first; pushing the API before it breaks the staff share routes only): staff with `external_access.share` share **a document in force** or **an alert in force** with **any party that has the portal on** (not only one a record names); the party reads the curated view, opens files, **acknowledges once per person and per revision** and talks to staff on the record. `portal_shares` gains `document_id` / `quality_alert_id` (live-unique per party; `app.portal_share_rules` and `share_with_portal` take the new kinds, the NC / 8D error texts unchanged); a document must be `active` with a revision in force and the sharer needs `documentation.read`; an alert must be `active`. **`portal_acknowledgements`** (frozen, audited, functions only, RLS `external_access.share`; unique per share, person and subject = **document revision id** or alert id; note ≤ 1,000) is written only by `app.portal_acknowledge_share(share, note)` (the caller's own live access; an alert no longer in force is refused `QM032`); a **new revision asks again** (the party sees "acknowledged" only for the revision in force). **Curated views, named fields only (tests list the exact keys):** `app.portal_get_document(share)` (number, title, type, revision, effective date, what changed, description, text for written documents as TipTap JSON, files of the revision in force, whether acknowledged, contact) and `app.portal_get_quality_alert(share)` (reference, revision, title, severity, category, description, effective / expiry / acknowledge-by dates, the actions' wording only — never departments, machines, recipients, attachments or who issued it). Document files open through `app.portal_document_file_path(share, file)` + the API's `FileLinkSigner` (bucket `document-files`, ten minutes, only files of the revision in force). Staff: `app.portal_ack_summary(kind, record)` (acknowledged / people / subject per share), `app.portal_share_acknowledgements(share)`; the party's `app.portal_pending_acknowledgements(org)` drives the home page. Staff are told by `portal_activity` kind `acknowledged` (record kinds `document`, `quality_alert`; links to the document / alert page). API: staff share routes take `kind=document|quality_alert` (cards carry `acknowledgement {acknowledged, people, subject}`), `GET /external-access/shares/{id}/acknowledgements`; portal `POST /portal/shares/{id}/acknowledge {note?}`, `POST /portal/shares/{id}/file-url {fileId}`, `GET /portal/home` adds `toAcknowledge` and `needsAcknowledgement` on attention items; the share email says document / quality alert. UI: `PortalShareCard` on the **document page** (while active) and the **quality alert page** (while active), showing "Acknowledged by N of M" and a **Who** dialog; the portal's shared page renders `DocumentRecord` (read-only `DocumentEditor` for written text, files with **Open**) and `AlertRecord` with an `AcknowledgeBlock` (note + Acknowledge, or "You have acknowledged this"); the portal home marks items "Please acknowledge". Tests `supabase/tests/portal_acknowledgements.test.sql` (+ sweep seed, `storage_through_api` unchanged), `api/test/portal-acknowledgements.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep: three `{id}` routes in both lists), `PortalShared.test.jsx`, `PortalShareCard.test.jsx`, `notifications.test.js`, `email.spec.ts`. **Not built:** quality alert attachments in the portal, reminders for unacknowledged items, a portal journey step, tasks for suppliers; not browser-checked.
- **External portal PT3d — actions for the supplier on a SCAR** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6; migration `20260402100000_portal_scar_actions.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); needs PT3c's migration first (applied); pushing the API before it breaks the staff SCAR page (its detail selects `supplier_scar_actions`) and the portal SCAR view): staff ask the supplier to **do** something on an issued SCAR, and the supplier's people complete it with a note. `supplier_scar_actions` (description 3–1000, optional due date, `open | done | cancelled`; **functions only, audited, never deleted**, RLS `supplier_quality.read`; a trigger holds the moves: open → done / cancelled, done → open only by staff reopening; a cancelled action stays cancelled; a SCAR that is closed or cancelled keeps its actions as they were). Staff (`supplier_quality.manage`, through `app.scar_for_write`): `app.add_scar_action(scar, description, due_on)` (even on a draft, so they can be prepared; the supplier sees them once the SCAR is issued), `update_scar_action` (open only), `cancel_scar_action(id, reason)`, `reopen_scar_action(id, reason)` (clears the supplier's note). Supplier: **`app.portal_complete_scar_action(action, note)`** checks the caller's own live portal access to the SCAR's supplier (`app.portal_scar_for_external`; the party comes from the SCAR; anything else `P0002` → 404; refused on a closed or cancelled SCAR and on an action already done, `QM032`), stamps who and when from the database, answers with the SCAR id and tells staff (`portal_activity` kind `scar_action_done`). **Actions never move the SCAR's status** (the response and its review still do). `app.portal_get_scar` (replaced; **the exact keys are listed in `portal_scars.test.sql`, `portal_scar_actions.test.sql` and the e2e tests**) adds `actions` — id, description, due date, status, overdue, result note, completed at / by name, `canComplete` — cancelled ones left out, no staff names; `app.portal_scar_action_counts(org)` gives open / late per SCAR for the home page. API: staff `POST /supplier-quality/scars/{id}/actions {description, dueOn?}` (emails the supplier's people when the SCAR is issued, best effort), `PATCH /supplier-quality/scar-actions/{id}`, `POST …/cancel|reopen {reason}`; the SCAR detail carries `actions` (with who added it, the supplier's note, cancel and reopen reasons); portal `POST /portal/scar-actions/{id}/complete {note}` (answers `{ id, scar }`), `GET /portal/home` adds `scarsWithActions`, SCAR list items carry `openActions` / `lateActions`. UI: **Actions for the supplier** card on the staff SCAR page (`ScarPage.jsx`, `ActionDialog` in `ScarDialogs.jsx`: add, edit, cancel / reopen with a reason), **Actions we ask of you** block with a **Complete** button and `CompleteActionDialog` on `PortalScarPage.jsx`, "Actions for you" on the portal home and an "N actions to do" / "Action overdue" mark in the SCAR lists. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_scar_actions.test.sql` (+ sweep seed), `api/test/portal-scar-actions.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep: five `{id}` routes in both lists), `PortalScar.test.jsx`, `Portal.test.jsx`, `SupplierScar.test.jsx`. **Not built:** a reminder before an action is due, actions on 8Ds (their team contacts are not linked to portal sign-ins), files on an action's result, actions as a condition for closing the SCAR, a portal journey step; not browser-checked. With this the decision on supplier tasks is settled: they are actions on the SCAR.
- **External portal PT4 — supplier performance** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6, decisions X6, X10, X11; migration `20260404100000_portal_performance.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); needs PT3d's migration first; pushing the API before it breaks only `/portal/performance`): a supplier's people see their own scorecard on `/portal/performance` — **the same figures as the staff Performance tab, from the same function** (`app.supplier_performance`), never a second calculation. The migration replaces `app.supplier_performance_metrics` and `app.supplier_effective_config` unchanged except their gates: both let a call through when the transaction-local setting `app.portal_performance` equals that supplier's id, which only `app.portal_get_performance` / its ranking loop set (after checking the caller's own live portal access to a supplier party, `app.portal_my_access`) and clear again; staff calls are gated exactly as before. **`app.portal_get_performance(org, party, days 30|90|365)` returns named fields only** (the SQL test and `portal-performance.e2e-spec.ts` list the keys): score and band, delivery (on time, in full, OTIF), quality (PPM both measures, lot rejection), NC counts by severity, SCAR on-time and waiting, certifications, the monthly trend (the **saved monthly scorecards**, `period_kind = 'monthly'`; the portal scores no period of its own) and the ranking. **No record references, no warnings, no weights, no other supplier.** **Tiles** (`portal_parties.tiles`, on unless `false`: score, delivery, quality, ncs, scars, certs, trend, ranking) are applied **by the database**: a tile staff switched off is not in the answer. **Ranking** (X10) needs `organisation_settings.portal_ranking_enabled` and the tile; the band is a quarter of the suppliers scored over the same period (`app.portal_rank_band`: top quarter / upper half / lower half / bottom quarter, ties never push anyone down) and **only with at least five scored suppliers** (`enough` says so; the answer never carries a name or a number). API `GET /portal/performance?days=` (`@PortalScoped`, suppliers only, other kinds 404). UI: `portal/PortalPerformancePage.jsx` at `/portal/performance` (period toggle, gauges and figures with every number also in words), a link on the portal home for suppliers, staff **Settings → External access**: a switch for the ranking and a **Tiles** dialog per supplier (`TilesDialog`, optimistic via `useExternalAccessMutation` party `tiles`); helpers `PERFORMANCE_TILES`, `tileOn`, `RANK_WORDS`, `pctWords` in `lib/portal.js`, hook `usePortalPerformance`. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_performance.test.sql`, `api/test/portal-performance.e2e-spec.ts`, `PortalPerformance.test.jsx`, `SettingsExternalAccess.test.jsx`. **Not built:** the customer side (PT5: NCs and 8Ds for their parts, PPM and first-pass yield), the "how this is scored" text, open NCs by age, a portal journey step; not browser-checked.
- **External portal PT5a — customers raise complaints** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6, decisions X3, X14; migration `20260405100000_portal_complaints.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); needs PT4's migration first (applied); pushing the API before it breaks only the new complaint routes): a customer's people raise a complaint in the portal and it becomes a **draft non-conformance** (`source = customer`, `customer_id` from the party, the customer's words as title and description, optional part, quantity, lot, their reference and date found) that staff review, complete and open — **severity, category and site stay empty; nothing is opened or decided for them**. `portal_complaints` links a complaint to its NC and to who raised it (functions only, audited, never changed or deleted; staff read with `external_access.share`; unique per NC, and unique per party and `client_key` so **a retry returns the same complaint**). **`app.portal_raise_complaint(org, party, …)`** checks the caller's own live access to a CUSTOMER party (`app.portal_customer_party`; the customer comes from the access row, never the request), refuses a part that is not linked to that customer in `part_customers`, a future date, and a party whose tile `complaints` is `false` (`portal_parties.tiles`, on by default), and tells staff with `portal_activity` kind `complaint_raised` (record kind `ncr`, so notices already link to the NC page). `app.portal_my_parts` lists only that customer's parts (our number and name, their own number); **`app.portal_list_complaints`** returns named fields only with a plain status from the NC's stage (draft → received, closed → closed, cancelled or deleted → cancelled, else in progress) and the live share id once staff share the NC (the complaint then links to the shared record). API (`@PortalScoped`, customers only, suppliers 404): `GET|POST /portal/complaints` (strict body `ComplaintBody`: title, description, `partId`, `quantity`, `lot`, `customerReference`, `detectedOn`, `clientKey`), `GET /portal/complaint-parts`. UI: `portal/PortalComplaints.jsx` ("Your complaints" and **Raise a complaint** on a customer's portal home; the dialog sends a client key made once per dialog), staff **Settings → External access**: an **Options** dialog per customer with the "Allow them to raise complaints" switch (`CUSTOMER_TILES`); helpers `COMPLAINT_STATUS` in `lib/portal.js`, hooks `usePortalComplaints|usePortalComplaintParts|usePortalComplaintMutation`. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_complaints.test.sql` (+ sweep seed), `api/test/portal-complaints.e2e-spec.ts`, `PortalComplaints.test.jsx`. **PT5 is in slices: PT5a this; not built yet:** customer contacts (X17: `customer_contacts` in the shape of `supplier_contacts`), closure confirmation on shared NCs and 8Ds, quality figures for their parts (NC and 8D counts and ageing, PPM, first-pass yield), inspection and certificate-of-conformance evidence, files on a complaint, a portal journey step; not browser-checked.
- **External portal PT5b — closure confirmation** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6, decision X3 "customers confirm closure"; migration `20260406100000_portal_closure_confirmation.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); needs PT5a's migration first (applied); pushing the API before it breaks the shared-record page's closure list and the portal home): staff ask a **customer** to confirm that a shared non-conformance or 8D can be closed; the customer's people **confirm, or say what is still wrong** (a note is needed then). **Information only: nothing is closed, reopened or blocked internally** — it is the customer's recorded answer beside the record. `portal_closure_requests` (pending → confirmed | declined | withdrawn; **one pending per share**; functions only, audited, never deleted, a trigger freezes everything once answered or withdrawn; `requested_at` is `clock_timestamp()` so order within one transaction is stable). Staff (`external_access.share`): `app.request_portal_closure(share, note?)` (customer parties only, a live share, ≤ 500 characters), `app.withdraw_portal_closure(id, reason)` (a waiting one, reason 3–500). Customer: `app.portal_decide_closure(id, confirm, note?)` (own live access via `app.portal_share_for_external`; final; tells staff with `portal_activity` kinds `closure_confirmed` / `closure_declined`, so notices link to the NC or 8D). `app.portal_share_closures(share)` answers either side with named fields only — **staff are never named to the customer** (`requestedBy`, `withdrawnReason` are null for them, withdrawn requests are hidden), staff see who asked and who answered; `app.portal_pending_closures(org)` drives the home page. API: staff `GET|POST /external-access/shares/{id}/closures` (the POST emails the customer's people, best effort, `tellParty` `closure`), `POST /external-access/closure-requests/{id}/withdraw`; portal `POST /portal/closure-requests/{id}/decide {confirm, note?}`, the shared record carries `closures`, home `toConfirm` and `needsClosure` on attention items. UI: **Closure** button on a customer's share in `PortalShareCard` (`ClosureDialog`: ask with a message, history, withdraw with a reason), **Closure** block with Yes / No on the portal's shared page (`ClosureBlock`), "Please confirm closure" mark on the portal home; helpers `CLOSURE_STATUS`, hooks `useShareClosures`. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_closure_confirmation.test.sql` (+ sweep seed), `api/test/portal-closure.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep, four routes in both lists), `PortalShared.test.jsx`, `PortalShareCard.test.jsx`. **Not built (PT5 still to do):** customer contacts (X17), quality figures for their parts (NC and 8D counts and ageing, PPM, first-pass yield), inspection and certificate-of-conformance evidence, files on a complaint, a portal journey step; closure confirmation does not gate closing; not browser-checked.
- **External portal PT5c — customer contacts** (decision X17; migration `20260407100000_customer_contacts.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); pushing the API before it breaks only the new contact routes): `customer_contacts` in the shape of `supplier_contacts` — several per customer, **one primary** (trigger: a new primary replaces the old, an inactive contact cannot stay primary), deactivated never deleted (no DELETE grant, column-level UPDATE that excludes the customer), audited, RLS `master_data.read` / `.manage`. **Staff data: nothing shows it to a customer in the portal, and portal sign-ins are not linked to a contact.** API `api/src/master-data/customer-contacts.ts`: `GET|POST /customers/{id}/contacts` (answers with the customer's contacts), `PATCH /customer-contacts/{id}`. UI `master-data/CustomerContacts.jsx` (a Contacts card in the customer edit dialog: add / edit dialog, active switch). Tests `supabase/tests/customer_contacts.test.sql` (+ sweep seed), `api/test/customer-contacts.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep, three routes), `CustomerContacts.test.jsx`.
- **External portal PT5d — customer quality figures** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6 "Performance (customer)"; migration `20260408100000_portal_customer_figures.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); pushing the API before it breaks only `/portal/figures`): `app.portal_get_customer_figures(org, party, days 30|90|365)` — the customer's own live access to a CUSTOMER party decides (`app.portal_customer_party`), and it returns **counts and averages only**: non-conformances that name this customer (live, past draft, not cancelled) — raised in the period by severity, open now with the oldest and average age, closed in the period with the average days to close — and the live 8Ds linked to those NCs (started, in progress, closed in the period, average days to close). No references, costs, owners or other customers; staff switch it off per customer with the tile `figures` = false (then the answer says `available: false` and nothing else). **PPM and first-pass yield are deliberately not built: there is no shipment data to divide by (decision X10 defers delivery performance) and nothing is invented to fill the gap.** API `GET /portal/figures?days=` (`@PortalScoped`, customers only, suppliers 404). UI `PortalFigures` (in `portal/PortalComplaints.jsx`, on a customer's portal home: period toggle, two tiles, every figure in words), staff **Options** dialog per customer gains "Show them their quality figures" (`CUSTOMER_TILES`), hook `usePortalFigures`. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_customer_figures.test.sql`, `api/test/portal-complaints.e2e-spec.ts`, `PortalComplaints.test.jsx`.
- **External portal PT5e — files on a complaint** (decision X3; migration `20260409100000_portal_complaint_files.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); pushing the API before it breaks only the complaint file routes and the complaints list, which now asks for files): a customer adds photos or documents to a complaint they raised. The files are ordinary **`ncr_attachments` of the complaint's draft non-conformance** (private `nc-files` bucket, `<org>/ncr/<ncr id>/…`), so staff see them on the NC page with nothing new. **Only while the complaint is a draft ("received")**, at most ten live files from people of the party, 25 MB each (the table's limit); once staff open it the customer talks to us in the shared record's conversation. The customer sees **only files added by people of their own party, never staff's own files on the NC**, and can take back only their own. Functions only, each deciding from the caller's own live access to the CUSTOMER party that raised the complaint (`app.portal_complaint_for_external`): `portal_complaint_folder`, `portal_complaint_upload_allowed` (the API calls it before signing an upload with the service key; **the API chooses the path**), `portal_add_complaint_file`, `portal_remove_complaint_file` (marks it removed with a reason), `portal_complaint_files`, `portal_complaint_file_path`. API (`@PortalScoped`): `POST /portal/complaints/{id}/files/upload-url {fileName}` (403 `file_not_allowed`), `POST /portal/complaints/{id}/files`, `POST /portal/complaint-files/{id}/remove|download-url` (ten-minute link); `GET /portal/complaints` carries `files`. UI: `ComplaintFiles` under each complaint in `PortalComplaints.jsx`, `uploadPortalComplaintFile` in `src/api/supabaseApi.js` (the one place that touches storage), hooks `usePortalComplaintFileMutation`, `openPortalComplaintFile`. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_complaint_files.test.sql`, `api/test/portal-complaint-files.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep, four routes), `PortalComplaints.test.jsx`. **Not built:** files attached while raising (they are added right after, on the complaint), a virus scan.
- **External portal PT5f — certificates and reports shared from a lot** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md` §6, "inspection and certificate-of-conformance evidence they were shared"; migration `20260410100000_lot_evidence.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23) — it creates the private `lot-evidence` bucket and extends `app.storage_file_allowed`, so the storage test now counts **thirteen** organisation buckets; pushing the API before it breaks the new routes and file links, and `ORGANISATION_BUCKETS` in `api/src/files/files.service.ts` names the bucket): staff attach a certificate of conformance, an inspection report or another file to a **lot** and share it with **one customer the lot is for**; that customer sees and opens only those. `lot_evidence_files` (one row per file per customer; functions only, audited, never deleted — a removed file keeps its row with a reason and **vanishes from the customer at once**; path must sit in `<org>/lot/<lot id>/…`, 25 MB). **Who may share:** `external_access.share` AND `production.read` at the lot's site; **a lot is for a customer** when it came from that customer's production order or its part is supplied to them (`part_customers`), and the customer's party and the portal are on (`app.lot_evidence_customers`). Staff with `external_access.share` read and upload through the ordinary `/files` routes (`app.lot_evidence_file_allowed`); a customer never touches storage — `app.portal_evidence_file_path` then the API signs a ten-minute link. The customer's list is named fields only (lot number, part, kind, description, file, size, date): no order, site, staff names or other customer. Functions `app.lot_evidence_customers`, `add_lot_evidence`, `remove_lot_evidence`, `list_lot_evidence`, `portal_list_evidence`, `portal_evidence_file_path`. API: staff `GET|POST /external-access/lots/{id}/evidence`, `POST /external-access/lot-evidence/{id}/remove`; portal `GET /portal/evidence`, `POST /portal/evidence/{id}/download-url`. UI: `production/LotEvidence.jsx` (a **Shared with customers** card in the lot dialog: share a file with a customer and kind, stop sharing with a reason), `PortalEvidence` ("Certificates and reports" on a customer's portal home), helpers `EVIDENCE_KINDS`, hooks `usePortalEvidence`, `openPortalEvidence`. Tests `supabase/tests/lot_evidence.test.sql` (+ sweep seed, storage test), `api/test/lot-evidence.e2e-spec.ts` (+ API sweep, four routes), `LotEvidence.test.jsx`, `PortalComplaints.test.jsx`. **Not built:** sharing inspection records themselves (only files), certificates linked automatically from material certificates or inspections, notices to the customer when a file is shared.
- **External portal PT6 — the portal looks and works like the main app** (`docs/external-portal-plan.md`; migration `20260411100000_portal_registers.sql`, applied live (checked 2026-11-23); pushing the API before it breaks only `/portal/ncrs` and `/portal/capas`): `/portal` is now a shell like the main app (`PortalApp.jsx`: the main app's collapsible side menu `PortalNavbar.jsx` built on `NavItem` (a row of links under the header below `md`), a top bar with the page name, organisation chooser, Open the QMS and Sign out) and a menu **per kind of party** from `portalMenu(access)` in `lib/portal.js`: **Dashboard** (`/portal`, the default: what needs attention, then a supplier's performance (`PerformancePanel`, the old performance page; `/portal/performance` redirects here) or a customer's quality figures), **Non-conformances** (`/portal/non-conformances`), **Corrective actions** (suppliers: `/portal/scars`), **8D reports** (customers: `/portal/8ds`), **Complaints** and **Certificates and reports** (customers), **Documents and alerts** (`/portal/documents`) and **Team** (party admins). Menu items show a badge of what is waiting (from the home answer: new or replied-to records, SCARs awaiting a response or with actions, documents to acknowledge). Pages for the other kind of party or for a member redirect to the dashboard; every page but the dashboard waits for two-factor when the organisation asks (`MfaNeeded`). **Registers** (`PortalRegister.jsx`, columns and filters in `portalColumns.jsx`, search/filter/sort in `lib/portalRegister.js`): the main app's `TableWrapper`, `ColumnVisibilityMenu` (show, hide, reorder, resize, remembered per browser via `useTablePreferences`) and `DateRangeFilter`, with a search box, a **sliding filter drawer** (`PortalFilterPane.jsx`, the main app's own pattern: slides in from the right over a dimmed page, Apply / Reset all, Escape or the backdrop closes without applying; stage and severity pills, date ranges, a "Needs my attention" switch), removable chips and CSV export of selected rows; **the lists are small and already limited by the database to what is shared, so search, filter, sort and paging happen in the browser** and the address keeps `q`, `f.<key>`, `d.<key>.from|to`, `s.<key>`, `page`, `pageSize`. A new register supplies columns (each with an `accessorFn`, which is what sorts), filters, `searchText` and `csvRow`. **Sharing is still the gate** (decision X2): `app.portal_list_ncrs(org)` and `app.portal_list_capas(org)` (named fields only, the same as the shared record shows: no costs, owners, sources or people of ours; the exact columns are asserted in the SQL test and `portal-registers.e2e-spec.ts`) list only NCs and 8Ds staff have shared with the caller's own party and not withdrawn, so an unshared NC is not in the register. API `GET /portal/ncrs`, `GET /portal/capas` (`@PortalScoped`); hooks `usePortalNcrs|usePortalCapas`. Detail pages (`/portal/shared/:id`, `/portal/scars/:id`) sit in `portalContent.jsx` and their back links go to the right register. Tests `supabase/tests/portal_registers.test.sql`, `api/test/portal-registers.e2e-spec.ts`, `PortalRegisters.test.jsx`, `lib/portalRegister.test.js`, `Portal.test.jsx` (menus per party and role). **Not built:** customers seeing NCs that staff have not shared (a decision for the owner: it would widen X2), saved filters, a SCAR dashboard, a browser journey step for the portal; not browser-checked.
- **Installable app (PWA)** (frontend only, no migration): `public/manifest.webmanifest` (name Fortress QMS, standalone, dark `#05060a`, icons `pwa-192.png`, `pwa-512.png`, maskable `pwa-maskable-512.png`), `public/sw.js` registered from `src/main.jsx` **in production builds only**. The worker is deliberately small: pages network-first with `public/offline.html` when the network fails, hashed `/assets/*` cached for speed, **every cross-origin request (API, Supabase, Stripe) and every non-GET goes straight to the network, so no record, token or API answer is ever stored**. `vercel.json` serves `/sw.js` with no-cache and the manifest with its own type. A release is picked up on the next page load (existing chunk-error reload still applies). Not built: push notifications, offline records, an install button inside the app. Change the cache name `VERSION` in `sw.js` only if the caching rules change.
- **Email links and branded emails** (`docs/email-setup.md`, no migration): Microsoft Defender Safe Links (and Mimecast, Proofpoint…) open every link in an email before the person does, which **used up Supabase's one-time links** (invitations said "expired or already used"; seen in the auth logs 2026-11-17). **Every email link now lands on `/auth/confirm`** (`auth/ConfirmEmailLink.jsx`, outside `<App />`): it shows **Continue** and only then calls `supabase.auth.verifyOtp({ token_hash, type })` (`verifyEmailLink` in `authService.js`), then goes to `next` (inside the app only, `safeNext`); an older link that already signed the person in goes straight on. The app sends `emailLinkUrl(next)` as the redirect for sign-up, resend and password reset; the API (`invitation-mailer.service.ts`) uses `confirmUrl(next)` for invitations (`/set-password?mode=invite`) and existing-account sign-in links (`/accept-invite`). **With a mail provider** (`MAIL_PROVIDER=resend`) the API makes the link with Supabase's admin `generate_link` (sends nothing) and sends its **own branded email** (`email/branded-email.ts` `renderBrandedEmail`, `invitationEmail`: inviter, message, role, expiry, logo from `APP_BASE_URL/fortress-qms.png`); without one Supabase sends, and **its templates must be the ones in `supabase/templates/`** (generated by `scripts/email-templates.cjs` from the same layout; buttons go to `{{ .RedirectTo }}&token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=…`, so every redirect we pass has a `?`). Supabase's Redirect URLs must allow the app's `/**`. Tests `ConfirmEmailLink.test.jsx`, `invitation-mailer.service.spec.ts`.
- **Applying a gauge type's checks to existing gauges** (no migration): a gauge gets its type's active checks only when it is created, so gauges made or imported before a check was added have none. `POST /gauge-types/{id}/apply-characteristics` (`calibration.manage`; runs as the person under RLS) copies each active type check onto every gauge of the type that is in use (not deleted, retired or lost) and has no characteristic with that `template_id` — a check already on a gauge, even switched off there, is never added again or changed; gauges at sites where the caller does not manage calibration are left and counted (`{ gaugesUpdated, characteristicsAdded, gaugesSkipped, type }`; a switched-off type is `409 calibration_rule`). Types carry `gaugesMissingChecks`. UI: a warning `Callout` with **Apply the checks to them** on each type in Settings → Data Management → Calibration (`appliedMessage`), and `GaugeChecksNotice` in the file import wizard's mapping step for gauges (needs `calibration.read`) naming the active types with no checks. Calibrations and characteristics are still not importable. Tests: `calibration.e2e-spec.ts`, `Calibration.test.jsx`, `IntegrationFiles.test.jsx`.
- **Calibration record page and the gauge type's procedure** (migrations `20260206100000_gauge_type_procedure.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11, and `20260207100000_gauge_type_procedure_document.sql`, which replaces it, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before the second breaks the gauge type endpoints, which select `procedure_document_id`): **a gauge type's calibration procedure is a controlled document** — `gauge_types.procedure_document_id` (composite FK to `documents (id, organisation_id)`, optional, `calibration.manage` via the column UPDATE grant; the typed `procedure_ref` of the first migration is dropped, and the migration refuses to run if any type had one). API: types carry `procedureDocumentId` and `procedureDocument { id, number, title, status }` (read under the caller's RLS, so `null` when they may not read the document); create / update take `procedureDocumentId`. UI: `calibration/ProcedureDocumentPicker.jsx` (server search over `GET /documents?status=draft,active`, needs `documentation.read` to list) on the type form in Settings → Data Management → Calibration, and the type shows the document's number as a link. A **new** calibration starts with the document's number as its own `calibrations.procedure_ref` (text, editable, never rewritten later) and offers a link to open the document. Not importable (`columns.ts`). **Certificate number** shows only for an external calibration, or when the record already has one. **Certificates before saving:** a new *external* calibration has a Certificates card (`PendingCertificates`); the files upload to the new record's folder right after it is created (a failed upload is reported, the calibration stays saved). **Readings:** the as-found / as-left boxes (`ReadingInput`) carry the verdict themselves — always a 2 px border, green with a `CircleCheckIcon` inside on the left when in tolerance, red with a `CircleXIcon` when out, plain when empty, plus the words for screen readers; there are no separate In / Out columns. **No layout shift while recording:** fixed column widths, the as-left Pass / Fail always holds its space (hidden until it applies) and the outcome row a minimum height; Discard changes shows only when there is something to discard. The gauge page lists Calibrations above Characteristics.
- **Calibration procedure document type** (migration `20260208100000_calibration_procedure_document_type.sql`, applied live 2026-11-12: both organisations got the type with prefix `CALP`, no gauge type had a procedure linked; pushing the API before it breaks the document type and documents endpoints, which select `system_key`): `document_types.system_key` marks a **built-in type** the application relies on (only key: `calibration_procedure`; unique per organisation). Every organisation has one, **Calibration procedure** (prefix `CALP`, written text, review every 24 months): `app.seed_calibration_procedure_type(org)` adopts an active type already called that, else makes one with the first free prefix (`CALP`, `CALP-2` …); `app.seed_document_types` calls it for new organisations. A built-in type is **never deleted (no type is) and cannot be switched off**, and its key cannot be put on or taken off (`app.document_type_rules`, now also on insert; only the seeding sets it, through the transaction setting `app.document_type_system`; `QM025`); its name, prefix (until a document uses it), description and review interval stay the organisation's. **A gauge type's procedure must be a document of that type** (`app.gauge_type_procedure_rule`, trigger `gauge_types_procedure_rule` on insert and on a change of `procedure_document_id`; `QM006` → `409 calibration_rule` "The procedure must be a document of the type …"; a link made before the rule is left until someone changes it). API: document types carry `systemKey`, `GET /documents` takes `typeKey=calibration_procedure`. UI: `ProcedureDocumentPicker` lists only those documents; Settings → Data Management → Documents shows the type as **Built in · Always on** (no switch, still editable). Tests `supabase/tests/calibration_procedure_document_type.test.sql` (fixture `fixtures/20260207100000_gauge_type_procedure_document.sql`: adoption and a taken prefix), `documents.e2e-spec.ts`, `calibration.e2e-spec.ts`, `Documents.test.jsx`, `Calibration.test.jsx`. A new built-in type needs: its key in the column's CHECK and in `DOCUMENT_TYPE_KEYS` (`documents.schemas.ts`), a seed, and its line in `BUILT_IN` (`SettingsDocuments.jsx`).
- **Gauge types are added and edited in a pop-up** (frontend only): `TypeDialog` in `Settings/SettingsCalibration.jsx` (`ui/Dialog`, `max-w-xl`) replaces the form that sat on one row inside the list — Code and Name side by side, Interval and Interval unit (a `ComboField`, no native select), the Procedure picker on its own line with its explanation as a hint, Description, then Cancel / Save (or Add type); a new type's fields required on save (`CustomFieldInputs`) are in the form and an existing type's **More details** card sits under it. The list stays as it is behind the pop-up; saving closes it, Cancel discards what was typed. Tests in `Calibration.test.jsx` (settings).
- **Reading any exported text file, and longer calibration intervals** (migration `20260114100000_calibration_interval_limit.sql`, applied live 2026-11-10): the file reader (`api/src/integrations/core/file-parsers.ts`) now **works out the encoding and the separator** — a byte-order mark always wins (UTF-8, UTF-16 LE/BE), otherwise `sniffEncoding` (UTF-16 by the zero bytes, else UTF-8 when valid, else Windows-1252) and `detectDelimiter` (comma, semicolon, tab or bar found the same number of times on the first lines); NUL characters are never kept (they made the database refuse a UTF-16 file read as UTF-8, which showed as an unexpected error); empty columns at the end (a separator at the end of every line) are dropped. Upload `options` take `encoding auto|utf-8|windows-1252|utf-16le|utf-16be` and `delimiter auto|,|;|tab||`; nothing given = auto. The wizard defaults both to "Work it out from the file" (`FILE_ENCODINGS`, `FILE_DELIMITERS`). Calibration intervals on gauge types and gauges now run to **3,650** (ten years in days; was 1,000), in the database, the API and the import catalogue.
- **Fix round after SQ6** (frontend only, no migration): supplier links go to `/Suppliers/:id`; breadcrumbs name path segments that are ids via `lib/breadcrumbs.js` (`useBreadcrumbNames`, pages register a label per id segment; an unnamed UUID shows "…"); supplier-responsible NCs hide Causal process; the SCAR raise dialog starts with an NC combo and fills title / problem / requirement breached from it (`breachFrom` in `lib/supplierScars.js`); NC disposition and containment dialogs close first and save through `lib/optimisticSubmit.js` (`useOptimisticSubmit`: `patches` `{queryKey, update}`, rollback + toast on error, refresh on settle) and use combos; dialogs freeze props that change after a save (`remaining`, `lots`) so refetches cannot flash false errors. **Optimistic saves in the supplier module:** questionnaire answers (`useRespondToAssessment`), document / certification verify, remove, withdraw and edit (`QUALIFICATION_GUESSES` in `useSupplierQuality.js`). **Documents:** `DocumentDialog` (drag-and-drop area, title suggested from the file name) is exported from `supplier-quality/Qualification.jsx` and `DocumentPicker` (combo + **Add a new document**, which opens that dialog in place and chooses the result) is used by certifications, questionnaire evidence and material certificates; the Documents tab shows a picture for images and the Word / PDF / Excel icon otherwise (`FileIcon` exported from `ui/FileDrop.jsx`). `RiskBadge` is four squares (green, yellow, orange, red) filled to the rating with the word beside it; the questionnaire warns when the chosen evidence document is expired or expiring. Frontend tests need `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` / `VITE_SUPABASE_KEY` set (any values) when run outside `npm test`'s usual environment.
- **Scheduled supplier scorecards (SQ5b)** (`docs/supplier-quality-plan.md` SQ5b; migration `20260224100000_scheduled_scorecards.sql`, applied live 2026-11-13 (live guardrails 0 violations); pushing the API before it breaks the scorecard endpoints, which select `period_kind`, and the worker's `scorecards` step): **monthly, quarterly and annual are switched on and off separately** in Settings → Data Management → Supplier quality (`supplier-quality/ScorecardSchedule.jsx`, optimistic switches; scope every supplier in use or chosen classifications). `supplier_scorecard_schedules` (one per organisation, written only by `app.set_supplier_scorecard_schedule(org, jsonb)`: `supplier_quality.configure`, and the caller lends their authority, so they must hold `supplier_quality.read` + `receiving.read`). The worker step `scorecards` (`SupplierPerformanceService.runSchedule` → `app.system_run_scheduled_scorecards(200)`, API login only) saves, for each kind on, every period that has ended in the organisation's day for every supplier in scope, worked out as `set_by` (no longer allowed → nothing saved, `problem` set), written by the system (`created_by` null, `period_kind` set; unique per supplier, kind and period). Switching on starts with the period that last ended. A band that falls between consecutive scheduled scorecards of one kind writes `supplier_band_drops` → notice "A supplier's score fell" (`supplier_quality.read`, default on for `.manage`, emailed; link `/Suppliers/:id?tab=performance` via `record_id`). API `GET|PUT /supplier-quality/performance/schedule`; scorecards carry `periodKind`; `savedByWords` / `periodWords` / `PERIOD_KINDS` in `lib/supplierPerformance.js`. Tests `supabase/tests/scheduled_scorecards.test.sql`, `supplier-performance.e2e-spec.ts`, `SupplierPerformance.test.jsx`. Nothing here changes approval, risk or SCARs.
- **Supplier performance settings** (migration `20260105100000_performance_settings.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the performance and disposition endpoints): the scorecard model gains optional keys — `ppm_basis` (`rejected` | `ncs`: which PPM scores; **both are always worked out**: `metrics.ppm` = units rejected at receiving, `metrics.ppmNc` = units on live NCs linked to a received line via `receipt_line_ncrs`, once per line and capped at the quantity received), `grace_days_before` (days early that still count as on time; **absent = no limit**, so early is never late unless set), `grace_days_after` (days late that still count, also the time allowed for in full), `in_full_under_pct` / `in_full_over_pct` (default 0 / 10) and `weights.in_full` (default 0 = shown, not scored). Older versions without the keys behave as before. `receipt_line_dispositions.quantity_rejected` (only on a rejection; `app.disposition_receipt_line(…, p_quantity)`; empty = the whole line) so a part-rejected line no longer counts whole. **In full** is per PO line due in the period: received by (due + grace after) within the tolerances (a short line that can still arrive is "pending" and left out); **OTIF** = in full and nothing earlier than the grace-before allows. **Per-supplier overrides:** `supplier_performance_settings` (one row per supplier; date policy, both grace values and both in-full tolerances; null = follows the model; no table writes, `app.set_supplier_performance_settings(supplier, jsonb)` needs `supplier_quality.configure`; keys `otdPolicy|graceDaysBefore|graceDaysAfter|inFullUnderPct|inFullOverPct`, null clears one); `app.supplier_effective_config` (internal) merges them over the model and **a saved scorecard freezes the effective config**. `app.supplier_performance_metrics` now takes the config (`jsonb`) instead of a date policy. API: `GET|PUT /supplier-quality/suppliers/{id}/performance/settings`, model body/response carry `ppmBasis`, `graceDaysBefore`, `graceDaysAfter`, `inFullUnderPct`, `inFullOverPct`, `weights.inFull`; performance carries `overrides`; disposition body takes `quantity`; reports add PPM (NC), In full %, OTIF % and the settings in their basis lines. UI: Settings → Supplier quality model card (a toggle for the PPM that scores, saved as a new version with an automatic reason, optimistic) and its dialog (all the new fields), `SupplierScoringSettings.jsx` on the Performance tab (what the supplier is scored with, which parts are its own, **Change** dialog; empty = follow the model), both PPM measures, In full and OTIF in the figures table, **Quantity rejected** on the receipt-line decision dialog. Tests: `supabase/tests/supplier_performance_settings.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-performance.e2e-spec.ts`, `SupplierPerformance.test.jsx`, `IncomingInspection.test.jsx`; `DB_CHECK_ONLY=<substring>` runs only the SQL tests whose name contains it. **Not built:** PPM by supplier-quantity from NCs without a receipt link, per-part tolerances, scheduled scorecards.
- **NC ↔ received line links and unlinked NCs in the NC-based PPM** (migration `20260106100000_ncr_receipt_link.sql`, applied live 2026-11-11; pushing the API before it breaks the receipt source and performance endpoints): `receipt_line_ncrs` links can now be **ended** (`ended_at/by`, `end_reason`; never deleted — `app.receipt_ncr_link_frozen` allows only that one change); one **live** link per NC (unique index where `ended_at is null`, so an NC's pieces are never counted twice) and per (line, NC) pair. `app.link_ncr_to_receipt_line(ncr, line)` (`ncr.update` + `receiving.read`; the NC must name the same supplier as the receipt, the part must agree when both name one, not a cancelled receipt or NC) and `app.unlink_ncr_from_receipt_line(link, reason)` (`ncr.update`); `app.raise_ncr_from_receipt_line` ignores ended links. Model key **`ppm_nc_include_unlinked`** (default false; `ppmNcIncludeUnlinked` in the API): when true the NC-based PPM also counts `quantity_affected` of live supplier NCs detected in the period that have **no live receipt link**; the metric always reports `linkedUnits`, `unlinkedUnits`, `unlinkedNcs`, `unlinkedWithoutQuantity`, `includeUnlinked` and `unlinkedSources` so the estimated part is visible even when not counted (linked pieces are capped at the line's received quantity, unlinked ones have no line to cap against). API (`api/src/receiving/requirements.*`): `GET /goods-receipt-lines/lookup?supplierId[&partId&q&limit]`, `POST /ncrs/{id}/receipt-link {receiptLineId}`, `POST /receipt-line-ncrs/{id}/unlink {reason}` (both answer with `receipt-source`, whose items now carry `linkId`/`linkedAt`). UI: `NcrReceiptSource.jsx` on the NC page's Source delivery card (**Link to a received line** dialog with a searchable combo of the supplier's lines, **End link** with a reason; shown for an NC that names a supplier), a **Non-conformance PPM counts** choice in the scoring model dialog, a "Non-conformances with no receipt" row in the Performance figures. Tests: `supabase/tests/ncr_receipt_link.test.sql`, `api/test/supplier-performance.e2e-spec.ts`, `IncomingInspection.test.jsx`, `SupplierPerformance.test.jsx`.
- **Quality alert fixes** (migration `20251225100000_quality_alert_actions_expiry.sql`, applied live 2026-11-09; pushing the API before it breaks the quality-alert endpoints): **actions are line items** — `quality_alert_actions` (position, description, optional department and/or machine; written only by `app.set_quality_alert_actions(alert, jsonb)` on a draft, copied by a revision, frozen once issued; issuing needs ≥1; existing `required_action` text became action 1 and the column is no longer written or required; API bodies take `actions: [{description, departmentId?, machineId?}]` replacing the list, details carry `actions` with labels, `GET /quality-alerts/active` returns `actions: [{description, department, machine}]`; UI `ActionsEditor` in `QualityAlertForm.jsx`, `ActionsList` in `documentation/shared.jsx`). **Expiry is automatic and logged:** `quality_alert_events` (append-only; `expired` by the system, `reopened` with who/old and new expiry/reason; functions only), `app.expire_quality_alerts` (on every read) and `app.system_expire_quality_alerts()` (API login only; daily cron `GET|POST /internal/cron/expire-quality-alerts`, in `api/vercel.json`), `in_force` and the active list also check the expiry date themselves. **Reopen:** `app.reopen_quality_alert(alert, new expiry, reason)` (`documentation.issue`, only from `expired`, new expiry today or later, reason required; the frozen-content trigger lets the expiry move only inside it via `app.quality_alert_reopen`), `POST /quality-alerts/{id}/reopen {expiryDate, reason}`, detail has `history`; UI Reopen button + dialog and an Expiry history card on the alert page. **`Callout`** (`src/components/ui/Callout.jsx`, tones success / info / warning / error with a tick, info, triangle or alert icon, optional title and action) is the one notice card: use it instead of a hand-styled bordered paragraph (QA acknowledgement green, "why revision" blue, withdrawn red, many error/notice messages). **Light-mode pill contrast:** `src/index.css` ends with overrides so any element with a tint (`bg-success-color/15` etc.) AND the matching text colour gets a solid light background and a dark saturated text in light mode only. Combo boxes and date fields no longer open when their label is tapped (label `onClick` prevents the default); the date calendar hangs from its bottom edge when it opens above the field (no gap).
- **Dropdown menu** (`src/components/ui/DropdownMenu.jsx`, the one menu for "a button that opens a list of actions"; use it for every new one): `<DropdownMenu label items header trigger />` where `trigger={(props, { open }) => <button {...props}>…</button>}` and `items` are `{ label, icon, hint, tone: "danger", disabled, checked (a switch; stays open), onSelect | to (in-app link) | href (new tab) }` or `{ separator: true }`; falsy entries are skipped so `can("x") && {…}` works. It floats in a `data-popover` portal on `document.body` (below the trigger, above when there is no room, right edge aligned, follows scrolling), closes on an outside click, Escape (focus returns to the trigger), Tab or choosing an item, and has arrow / Home / End keys. First use: `ProfileMenu.jsx` (the profile picture in `Header.jsx`: name and email, Settings, Help (opens the website guides' contents page, `https://fortress-qms.com/help`, in a new tab; `VITE_HELP_URL` via `lib/help.js` overrides it), Dark mode switch, Logout). `useSignOut` is the shared sign-out; `useTheme` keeps every dark-mode switch on the page in step. **Table row menus use it too:** `TableWrapper` takes `rowActions(item) → items` and draws the "…" button with `ui/RowActionsMenu.jsx` (the grid card's Actions button passes its own `trigger`); NC rows come from `ncr/useNcrRowActions.js`. There is no hand-positioned actions pop-up any more (`ActionsModal`, `ActionsModalItem` and the `modalPos` state are gone) — a register defines `rowActions` and nothing else.
- **Where detected and Causal process are machines** (migration `20251223100000_ncr_machines_and_legacy_cleanup.sql`, applied live 2026-11-08: 14 machines made, 21 detection and 20 causal links re-pointed, 14 legacy tables dropped, live guardrail checks 0 violations): `nonconformances.detection_process_id` and `causal_process_id` (column names kept: "where detected" and "causal process" are the words people use) now have composite foreign keys to `machines (id, organisation_id)` (`nonconformances_detection_machine_fkey`, `nonconformances_causal_machine_fkey`) instead of `ncr_classifications` of kind `process`. The migration made a machine of the same organisation for every process classification (reusing a machine with the same name, any case; codes made from the name) and re-pointed the existing NCs, with the row triggers switched off for the move (it is not a user's change, and would have notified everyone). The old `process` classification rows are left as they are, unused; the Settings → Data Management "Processes" list is gone (a note points to Machines). `app.start_capa` takes the 8D's "where" from the machine. API: `detectionProcess` / `causalProcess` are `{ id, name }` of the machine, the history names machine ids, the create / update bodies take machine ids. UI: `MachinePicker` in `ncr/Pickers.jsx` (server search over `GET /machines`, shows the code as a hint); needs `master_data.read` like the part and customer pickers.
- **Saved filters** (migration `20251222100000_saved_filters.sql`, applied live 2026-11-08; SQL test `supabase/tests/saved_filters.test.sql`; API `api/src/saved-filters/`, e2e `saved-filters.e2e-spec.ts`): a person's own named filters for a register, **up to five per person, organisation and register** (`scope`, first `ncr_register`). Table `saved_filters` (`user_id` default `auth.uid()`, `name` ≤ 40 characters unique per person ignoring case, `filters` = flat jsonb of the register's URL filters, `position`): row-level security shows and changes only the owner's own rows while they belong to the organisation; table-level SELECT and DELETE, INSERT of the five columns, UPDATE of `name` and `filters` only (nothing can move a filter to someone else or another organisation); trigger `app.saved_filters_limit_check` enforces the five (advisory lock, `QM019` → `409 saved_filter_rule`) and refuses another person's name first; a duplicate name is `409 conflict`. Personal preferences, so no audit trigger (like notification preferences). API `GET /saved-filters?scope` (`{ data, limit }`), `POST` `{ scope, name, filters }` (empty values dropped, keys plain words, values ≤ 500), `PATCH /saved-filters/{id}` `{ name?, filters? }`, `DELETE` (204); any member, 404 for anyone else's id. UI: `ncr/SavedFilterBar.jsx` on the NC register replaces the quick tabs — a `SlidingToggle` of **All** and the saved filters (one click applies it to the URL, replacing every other filter, search included; the one that matches the page is highlighted, comma lists compared in any order, the `…Label` keys ignored), **Save filter** appears when filters are chosen that are not saved (asks for a name, shows what it saves; the name of an existing filter **replaces** it, a sixth is refused with advice), the "…" button opens a **Saved filters** pop-up listing every filter by name with what it contains (`describeFilter` in `lib/savedFilters.js`), each row with its own "…" menu to Rename or Delete; hooks `useSavedFilters` / `useSavedFilterMutation` (optimistic, rolled back on error) in `hooks/useSavedFilters.js`, helpers `lib/savedFilters.js`. Other registers can adopt it by adding their scope to `SAVED_FILTER_SCOPES` and rendering `SavedFilterBar` with their filter keys. Tests: `Ncr.test.jsx` (saved filters), `savedFilters.test.js`, `refresh.test.js`; the journey saves one and finds it again.
- **Pick-one bars** (`src/components/ui/SlidingToggle.jsx`, the register page's grid / list switch made general; the only one — the old `SlidingSelector`s are gone, and a test fails if a file builds its own `role="tablist"`): `<SlidingToggle ariaLabel options value onChange mode />`, options `{ value, label?, icon?, title?, count?, disabled?, id?, controls? }` or plain strings, `mode` `buttons` (default, `aria-pressed`) | `tabs` | `radio`; `onChange(value)` only when the choice changes; arrow keys move it. Used for the NC register and dashboard source tabs, the register table / card switch (`ViewToggle`), SPC alert status, platform tabs and periods, audit schedule list / calendar, My actions, serial "How many", calibration outcome and the notification filter. Not used for multi-select chips, the Pass / Fail buttons (their colours carry meaning) or option cards.
- **Settings → Data Management** (`Settings/SettingsDataManagement.jsx`) is the one place for module lists and set-up: a **sticky header** (title, the tab's description, a view-only note without the manage permission) with a `SlidingToggle` in `tabs` mode — **Non-conformance** (`SettingsNonConformance.jsx`: categories, failure modes, sources, stages, master-data links), **Calibration** (`SettingsCalibration`), **Process control** (`SettingsProcessControl`), **Audits** (`SettingsAudits`) and **Supplier quality** (`SettingsSupplierQuality`). A tab shows only to people with its read permission (`DATA_TABS`: `ncr.read`, `calibration.read`, `process_control.read`, `audits.read`, `supplier_quality.read`); the chosen tab is in the address (`?tab=non-conformance|calibration|process-control|audits|supplier-quality`); the old `/Settings/Calibration|Process-Control|Audits|Supplier-Quality` addresses redirect to their tab. The settings menu item shows when any of those permissions is held (`useCanSee` takes a list = any). Wherever older notes say "Settings → Calibration / Process control / Audits / Supplier quality", read "Settings → Data Management → that tab".
